package co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.data

import com.google.firebase.firestore.FieldValue
import com.google.firebase.firestore.FirebaseFirestore
import com.google.firebase.firestore.ListenerRegistration
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.model.DeliveryAddress

/** Direcciones guardadas del usuario: users/{uid}/addresses/{id}. */
object AddressRepository {

    private const val USERS = "users"
    private const val ADDRESSES = "addresses"

    private val db: FirebaseFirestore get() = FirebaseFirestore.getInstance()

    fun listen(uid: String, onChange: (List<DeliveryAddress>) -> Unit): ListenerRegistration =
        db.collection(USERS).document(uid).collection(ADDRESSES)
            .addSnapshotListener { snapshot, _ ->
                if (snapshot != null) {
                    onChange(
                        snapshot.documents.mapNotNull { doc ->
                            val text = doc.getString("text") ?: return@mapNotNull null
                            DeliveryAddress(
                                id = doc.id,
                                label = doc.getString("label").orEmpty(),
                                emoji = doc.getString("emoji") ?: "ðŸ“",
                                text = text
                            )
                        }
                    )
                }
            }

    fun add(uid: String, label: String, emoji: String, text: String, onResult: (Result<String>) -> Unit) {
        val data = hashMapOf<String, Any>(
            "label" to label,
            "emoji" to emoji,
            "text" to text,
            "createdAt" to FieldValue.serverTimestamp()
        )
        db.collection(USERS).document(uid).collection(ADDRESSES).add(data)
            .addOnSuccessListener { ref -> onResult(Result.success(ref.id)) }
            .addOnFailureListener { onResult(Result.failure(it)) }
    }
}

===== C:\Users\vgzar\Documents\UdeA\COMPUTACION MOVIL\PidePues\app\src\main\java\co\edu\udea\compumovil\labs20262_gr08\pidepues\data\AuthRepository.kt =====
package co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.data

import com.google.firebase.auth.FirebaseAuth
import com.google.firebase.firestore.FieldValue
import com.google.firebase.firestore.FirebaseFirestore
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.domain.Validators
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.model.UserProfile
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.model.UserRole

/**
 * SesiÃ³n y perfil con Firebase.
 *  - Firebase Authentication: correo + contraseÃ±a.
 *  - Firestore: users/{uid} con nombre, correo, celular y rol.
 * Todas las llamadas devuelven el resultado en el hilo principal.
 */
object AuthRepository {

    private const val USERS = "users"

    private val auth: FirebaseAuth get() = FirebaseAuth.getInstance()
    private val db: FirebaseFirestore get() = FirebaseFirestore.getInstance()

    val isLoggedIn: Boolean get() = auth.currentUser != null

    fun register(
        fullName: String,
        email: String,
        phone: String,
        password: String,
        role: UserRole,
        onResult: (Result<UserProfile>) -> Unit
    ) {
        auth.createUserWithEmailAndPassword(email.trim(), password)
            .addOnSuccessListener { result ->
                val user = result.user
                if (user == null) {
                    onResult(Result.failure(IllegalStateException("Cuenta creada sin usuario")))
                    return@addOnSuccessListener
                }
                val profile = UserProfile(
                    uid = user.uid,
                    fullName = fullName.trim(),
                    email = email.trim(),
                    phone = Validators.normalizePhone(phone),
                    role = role
                )
                val data = hashMapOf(
                    "fullName" to profile.fullName,
                    "email" to profile.email,
                    "phone" to profile.phone,
                    "role" to role.key,
                    "createdAt" to FieldValue.serverTimestamp()
                )
                db.collection(USERS).document(user.uid).set(data)
                    .addOnSuccessListener { onResult(Result.success(profile)) }
                    .addOnFailureListener { error ->
                        // Sin perfil la cuenta quedarÃ­a incompleta: se elimina para poder reintentar
                        user.delete()
                        onResult(Result.failure(error))
                    }
            }
            .addOnFailureListener { onResult(Result.failure(it)) }
    }

    fun login(email: String, password: String, onResult: (Result<UserProfile>) -> Unit) {
        auth.signInWithEmailAndPassword(email.trim(), password)
            .addOnSuccessListener { loadProfile(onResult) }
            .addOnFailureListener { onResult(Result.failure(it)) }
    }

    /** Lee el perfil del usuario con sesiÃ³n iniciada. */
    fun loadProfile(onResult: (Result<UserProfile>) -> Unit) {
        val user = auth.currentUser
        if (user == null) {
            onResult(Result.failure(IllegalStateException("No hay sesiÃ³n iniciada")))
            return
        }
        db.collection(USERS).document(user.uid).get()
            .addOnSuccessListener { doc ->
                if (!doc.exists()) {
                    onResult(Result.failure(IllegalStateException("La cuenta no tiene perfil")))
                    return@addOnSuccessListener
                }
                onResult(
                    Result.success(
                        UserProfile(
                            uid = user.uid,
                            fullName = doc.getString("fullName").orEmpty(),
                            email = doc.getString("email") ?: user.email.orEmpty(),
                            phone = doc.getString("phone").orEmpty(),
                            role = UserRole.fromKey(doc.getString("role"))
                        )
                    )
                )
            }
            .addOnFailureListener { onResult(Result.failure(it)) }
    }

    fun sendPasswordReset(email: String, onResult: (Result<Unit>) -> Unit) {
        auth.sendPasswordResetEmail(email.trim())
            .addOnSuccessListener { onResult(Result.success(Unit)) }
            .addOnFailureListener { onResult(Result.failure(it)) }
    }

    fun logout() = auth.signOut()
}

===== C:\Users\vgzar\Documents\UdeA\COMPUTACION MOVIL\PidePues\app\src\main\java\co\edu\udea\compumovil\labs20262_gr08\pidepues\data\FirestoreSeeder.kt =====
package co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.data

import com.google.firebase.firestore.FirebaseFirestore
import com.google.firebase.firestore.GeoPoint
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.model.MenuItem
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.model.Restaurant

/**
 * Carga los datos de ejemplo ([SampleData]) en Firestore cuando la colecciÃ³n estÃ¡ vacÃ­a.
 * Solo se ejecuta en compilaciones de depuraciÃ³n (ver MainActivity).
 */
object FirestoreSeeder {

    private val db: FirebaseFirestore get() = FirebaseFirestore.getInstance()

    /** Devuelve true si cargÃ³ datos, false si ya habÃ­a restaurantes. */
    fun seedIfEmpty(onDone: (Result<Boolean>) -> Unit) {
        db.collection(RestaurantRepository.RESTAURANTS).limit(1).get()
            .addOnSuccessListener { snapshot ->
                if (!snapshot.isEmpty) {
                    onDone(Result.success(false))
                    return@addOnSuccessListener
                }
                val batch = db.batch()
                SampleData.restaurants().forEach { restaurant ->
                    val ref = db.collection(RestaurantRepository.RESTAURANTS).document(restaurant.id.toString())
                    batch.set(ref, restaurantToMap(restaurant))
                    SampleData.menuFor(restaurant).forEachIndexed { index, item ->
                        batch.set(ref.collection(RestaurantRepository.MENU).document(item.id), menuItemToMap(item, index))
                    }
                }
                batch.commit()
                    .addOnSuccessListener { onDone(Result.success(true)) }
                    .addOnFailureListener { onDone(Result.failure(it)) }
            }
            .addOnFailureListener { onDone(Result.failure(it)) }
    }

    private fun restaurantToMap(r: Restaurant): Map<String, Any> = mapOf(
        "name" to r.name,
        "cuisine" to r.cuisine,
        "dishTag" to r.dishTag,
        "category" to r.category.name,
        "rating" to r.rating.toDouble(),
        "deliveryMinutes" to r.deliveryMinutes,
        "deliveryFee" to r.deliveryFee,
        "location" to GeoPoint(r.latitude, r.longitude),
        "emoji" to r.emoji,
        "isOpen" to r.isOpen,
        "keywords" to r.keywords
    )

    private fun menuItemToMap(item: MenuItem, order: Int): Map<String, Any> = mapOf(
        "name" to item.name,
        "description" to item.description,
        "price" to item.price,
        "emoji" to item.emoji,
        "order" to order
    )
}

===== C:\Users\vgzar\Documents\UdeA\COMPUTACION MOVIL\PidePues\app\src\main\java\co\edu\udea\compumovil\labs20262_gr08\pidepues\data\OrderRepository.kt =====
package co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.data

import com.google.firebase.firestore.DocumentSnapshot
import com.google.firebase.firestore.FieldValue
import com.google.firebase.firestore.FirebaseFirestore
import com.google.firebase.firestore.GeoPoint
import com.google.firebase.firestore.ListenerRegistration
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.model.CartLine
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.model.Order
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.model.OrderLine
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.model.OrderStatus
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.model.PaymentMethod
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.model.Restaurant
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.model.UserProfile

/**
 * Pedidos en Firestore: orders/{id}.
 * El cliente crea el pedido y lo ve actualizarse en tiempo real (addSnapshotListener).
 */
object OrderRepository {

    private const val ORDERS = "orders"

    private val db: FirebaseFirestore get() = FirebaseFirestore.getInstance()

    /**
     * Crea un pedido en estado "recibido". [deliveryPoint] solo se guarda cuando el cliente
     * eligiÃ³ "Tu ubicaciÃ³n actual" (dato de ubicaciÃ³n mÃ­nimo necesario para entregar).
     */
    fun placeOrder(
        customer: UserProfile,
        restaurant: Restaurant,
        lines: List<CartLine>,
        subtotal: Int,
        deliveryFee: Int,
        serviceFee: Int,
        total: Int,
        address: String,
        instructions: String,
        payment: PaymentMethod,
        deliveryPoint: GeoPoint?,
        onResult: (Result<String>) -> Unit
    ) {
        val data = hashMapOf<String, Any>(
            "customerId" to customer.uid,
            "customerName" to customer.fullName,
            "customerPhone" to customer.phone,
            "restaurantId" to restaurant.id,
            "restaurantName" to restaurant.name,
            "restaurantEmoji" to restaurant.emoji,
            "items" to lines.map {
                mapOf(
                    "name" to it.item.name,
                    "emoji" to it.item.emoji,
                    "unitPrice" to it.item.price,
                    "quantity" to it.quantity
                )
            },
            "subtotal" to subtotal,
            "deliveryFee" to deliveryFee,
            "serviceFee" to serviceFee,
            "total" to total,
            "address" to address,
            "instructions" to instructions,
            "paymentMethod" to payment.key,
            "status" to OrderStatus.SOLICITADO.key,
            "etaMinutes" to restaurant.deliveryMinutes,
            "createdAt" to FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
            "updatedAt" to FieldValue.serverTimestamp()
        )
        if (deliveryPoint != null) data["deliveryLocation"] = deliveryPoint

        db.collection(ORDERS).add(data)
            .addOnSuccessListener { ref -> onResult(Result.success(ref.id)) }
            .addOnFailureListener { onResult(Result.failure(it)) }
    }

    /** Escucha en tiempo real los pedidos del cliente. Devuelve el registro para cancelarlo. */
    fun listenOrders(customerId: String, onResult: (Result<List<Order>>) -> Unit): ListenerRegistration =
        db.collection(ORDERS).whereEqualTo("customerId", customerId)
            .addSnapshotListener { snapshot, error ->
                if (snapshot == null) {
                    onResult(Result.failure(error ?: IllegalStateException("Sin datos")))
                } else {
                    onResult(Result.success(snapshot.documents.mapNotNull { it.toOrderOrNull() }))
                }
            }

    /**
     * SOLO PRUEBAS (compilaciones de depuraciÃ³n): avanza el pedido al siguiente estado,
     * como lo harÃ­an el restaurante y el domiciliario. Al pasar a "en camino" asigna un
     * domiciliario de ejemplo.
     */
    fun simulateNextStatus(order: Order, onResult: (Result<Unit>) -> Unit) {
        val next = order.status.next()
        if (next == null) {
            onResult(Result.success(Unit))
            return
        }
        val update = hashMapOf<String, Any>(
            "status" to next.key,
            "updatedAt" to FieldValue.serverTimestamp()
        )
        if (next == OrderStatus.EN_CAMINO) {
            update["driverName"] = "AndrÃ©s"
            update["driverRating"] = 4.9
        }
        db.collection(ORDERS).document(order.id).update(update)
            .addOnSuccessListener { onResult(Result.success(Unit)) }
            .addOnFailureListener { onResult(Result.failure(it)) }
    }

    private fun DocumentSnapshot.toOrderOrNull(): Order? {
        val restaurantName = getString("restaurantName") ?: return null
        val rawItems = get("items") as? List<*> ?: emptyList<Any>()
        val lines = rawItems.mapNotNull { raw ->
            val item = raw as? Map<*, *> ?: return@mapNotNull null
            OrderLine(
                name = item["name"] as? String ?: return@mapNotNull null,
                emoji = item["emoji"] as? String ?: "",
                unitPrice = (item["unitPrice"] as? Number)?.toInt() ?: 0,
                quantity = (item["quantity"] as? Number)?.toInt() ?: 1
            )
        }
        return Order(
            id = id,
            restaurantId = getLong("restaurantId")?.toInt() ?: 0,
            restaurantName = restaurantName,
            restaurantEmoji = getString("restaurantEmoji").orEmpty(),
            lines = lines,
            subtotal = getLong("subtotal")?.toInt() ?: 0,
            deliveryFee = getLong("deliveryFee")?.toInt() ?: 0,
            serviceFee = getLong("serviceFee")?.toInt() ?: 0,
            total = getLong("total")?.toInt() ?: 0,
            address = getString("address").orEmpty(),
            instructions = getString("instructions").orEmpty(),
            paymentMethod = PaymentMethod.fromKey(getString("paymentMethod")),
            status = OrderStatus.fromKey(getString("status")),
            // Mientras Firestore confirma la hora del servidor, se usa una estimaciÃ³n local
            createdAtMillis = getTimestamp("createdAt", DocumentSnapshot.ServerTimestampBehavior.ESTIMATE)
                ?.toDate()?.time ?: System.currentTimeMillis(),
            etaMinutes = getLong("etaMinutes")?.toInt() ?: 30,
            driverName = getString("driverName"),
            driverRating = getDouble("driverRating")
        )
    }
}

===== C:\Users\vgzar\Documents\UdeA\COMPUTACION MOVIL\PidePues\app\src\main\java\co\edu\udea\compumovil\labs20262_gr08\pidepues\data\RestaurantRepository.kt =====
package co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.data

import com.google.firebase.firestore.DocumentSnapshot
import com.google.firebase.firestore.FieldValue
import com.google.firebase.firestore.FirebaseFirestore
import com.google.firebase.firestore.ListenerRegistration
import com.google.firebase.firestore.SetOptions
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.model.Category
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.model.MenuCategory
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.model.MenuItem
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.model.OrderItem
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.model.OrderStatus
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.model.Restaurant
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.model.RestaurantOrder
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.model.RestaurantProfile
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.model.Review

/**
 * Restaurantes y menÃºs desde Firestore.
 *   restaurants/{id}            -> datos del restaurante
 *   restaurants/{id}/menu/{id}  -> platos del menÃº
 *   orders/{id}                 -> pedidos en curso y finalizados
 */
object RestaurantRepository {

    const val RESTAURANTS = "restaurants"
    const val MENU = "menu"
    const val ORDERS = "orders"
    const val REVIEWS = "reviews"

    private val db: FirebaseFirestore get() = FirebaseFirestore.getInstance()

    fun fetchRestaurants(onResult: (Result<List<Restaurant>>) -> Unit) {
        db.collection(RESTAURANTS).get()
            .addOnSuccessListener { snapshot ->
                onResult(Result.success(snapshot.documents.mapNotNull { it.toRestaurantOrNull() }))
            }
            .addOnFailureListener { onResult(Result.failure(it)) }
    }

    fun fetchMenu(restaurantId: Int, onResult: (Result<List<MenuItem>>) -> Unit) {
        db.collection(RESTAURANTS).document(restaurantId.toString()).collection(MENU).get()
            .addOnSuccessListener { snapshot ->
                val items = snapshot.documents
                    .sortedBy { it.getLong("order") ?: Long.MAX_VALUE }
                    .mapNotNull { it.toMenuItemOrNull(restaurantId.toString()) }
                onResult(Result.success(items))
            }
            .addOnFailureListener { onResult(Result.failure(it)) }
    }

    fun findOrCreateOwnedRestaurant(
        ownerUid: String,
        restaurantName: String,
        onResult: (Result<String>) -> Unit
    ) {
        val restaurants = db.collection(RESTAURANTS)
        restaurants.whereEqualTo("ownerUid", ownerUid).limit(1).get()
            .addOnSuccessListener { ownedRestaurants ->
                val ownedRestaurant = ownedRestaurants.documents.firstOrNull()
                if (ownedRestaurant != null) {
                    onResult(Result.success(ownedRestaurant.id))
                    return@addOnSuccessListener
                }
                val restaurantDocument = restaurants.document(ownerUid)
                restaurantDocument.get()
                    .addOnSuccessListener { existing ->
                        val existingOwner = existing.getString("ownerUid")
                        if (existing.exists() && existingOwner != null && existingOwner != ownerUid) {
                            onResult(Result.failure(SecurityException("El restaurante pertenece a otra cuenta")))
                            return@addOnSuccessListener
                        }
                        val name = existing.getString("name")
                            ?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() }
                            ?: restaurantName
                        restaurantDocument.set(
                            mapOf("ownerUid" to ownerUid, "name" to name),
                            SetOptions.merge()
                        ).addOnSuccessListener { onResult(Result.success(ownerUid)) }
                            .addOnFailureListener { onResult(Result.failure(it)) }
                    }
                    .addOnFailureListener { onResult(Result.failure(it)) }
            }
            .addOnFailureListener { onResult(Result.failure(it)) }
    }

    fun listenMenu(
        restaurantDocumentId: String,
        onResult: (Result<List<MenuItem>>) -> Unit
    ): ListenerRegistration = db.collection(RESTAURANTS)
        .document(restaurantDocumentId)
        .collection(MENU)
        .addSnapshotListener { snapshot, error ->
            if (error != null) {
                onResult(Result.failure(error))
                return@addSnapshotListener
            }
            if (snapshot == null) {
                onResult(Result.failure(IllegalStateException("No se recibiÃ³ el menÃº del restaurante")))
                return@addSnapshotListener
            }
            val items = snapshot.documents
                .sortedBy { it.getLong("order") ?: Long.MAX_VALUE }
                .mapNotNull { it.toMenuItemOrNull(restaurantDocumentId) }
            onResult(Result.success(items))
        }

    fun setMenuItemAvailability(
        restaurantDocumentId: String,
        itemId: String,
        isAvailable: Boolean,
        onResult: (Result<Unit>) -> Unit
    ) {
        db.collection(RESTAURANTS).document(restaurantDocumentId)
            .collection(MENU).document(itemId)
            .update("isAvailable", isAvailable)
            .addOnSuccessListener { onResult(Result.success(Unit)) }
            .addOnFailureListener { onResult(Result.failure(it)) }
    }

    fun saveMenuItem(
        restaurantDocumentId: String,
        item: MenuItem,
        onResult: (Result<Unit>) -> Unit
    ) {
        val menu = db.collection(RESTAURANTS).document(restaurantDocumentId).collection(MENU)
        val isNew = item.id.isBlank()
        val itemDocument = if (isNew) menu.document() else menu.document(item.id)
        val data = mutableMapOf<String, Any>(
            "name" to item.name.trim(),
            "description" to item.description.trim(),
            "price" to item.price,
            "emoji" to item.emoji,
            "menuCategory" to item.category.key,
            "isAvailable" to item.isAvailable
        )
        if (isNew) data["order"] = System.currentTimeMillis()
        item.imageUri?.let { data["imageUri"] = it }
        itemDocument.set(data, SetOptions.merge())
            .addOnSuccessListener { onResult(Result.success(Unit)) }
            .addOnFailureListener { onResult(Result.failure(it)) }
    }

    private fun DocumentSnapshot.toRestaurantOrNull(): Restaurant? {
        val restaurantId = id.toIntOrNull() ?: return null
        val point = getGeoPoint("location") ?: return null
        val categoryName = getString("category") ?: return null
        val category = runCatching { Category.valueOf(categoryName) }.getOrNull() ?: return null
        return Restaurant(
            id = restaurantId,
            name = getString("name") ?: return null,
            cuisine = getString("cuisine").orEmpty(),
            dishTag = getString("dishTag").orEmpty(),
            category = category,
            rating = getDouble("rating")?.toFloat() ?: 0f,
            deliveryMinutes = getLong("deliveryMinutes")?.toInt() ?: 0,
            deliveryFee = getLong("deliveryFee")?.toInt() ?: 0,
            latitude = point.latitude,
            longitude = point.longitude,
            emoji = getString("emoji").orEmpty(),
            isOpen = getBoolean("isOpen") ?: true,
            keywords = getString("keywords").orEmpty()
        )
    }

    private fun DocumentSnapshot.toMenuItemOrNull(restaurantDocumentId: String): MenuItem? {
        val name = getString("name") ?: return null
        return MenuItem(
            id = id,
            restaurantId = restaurantDocumentId.toIntOrNull() ?: 0,
            restaurantDocumentId = restaurantDocumentId,
            name = name,
            description = getString("description").orEmpty(),
            price = getLong("price")?.toInt() ?: 0,
            emoji = getString("emoji").orEmpty(),
            category = MenuCategory.fromKey(getString("menuCategory") ?: getString("category")),
            isAvailable = getBoolean("isAvailable") ?: getBoolean("available") ?: true,
            imageUri = getString("imageUri")
        )
    }

    fun listenRestaurantOrders(
        restaurantId: String,
        onResult: (Result<List<RestaurantOrder>>) -> Unit
    ): ListenerRegistration = db.collection(ORDERS)
        .whereEqualTo("restaurantId", restaurantId)
        .addSnapshotListener { snapshot, error ->
            if (error != null) {
                onResult(Result.failure(error))
                return@addSnapshotListener
            }
            if (snapshot == null) {
                onResult(Result.success(emptyList()))
                return@addSnapshotListener
            }
            val orders = snapshot.documents.mapNotNull { it.toRestaurantOrderOrNull() }
                .sortedByDescending { it.createdAtMillis }
            onResult(Result.success(orders))
        }

    fun updateOrderStatus(
        orderId: String,
        newStatus: OrderStatus,
        onResult: (Result<Unit>) -> Unit
    ) {
        val updates = mapOf(
            "status" to newStatus.label,
            "estado" to newStatus.label,
            "statusKey" to newStatus.key,
            "updatedAt" to FieldValue.serverTimestamp()
        )
        db.collection(ORDERS).document(orderId)
            .update(updates)
            .addOnSuccessListener { onResult(Result.success(Unit)) }
            .addOnFailureListener {
                db.collection(ORDERS).document(orderId)
                    .set(updates, SetOptions.merge())
                    .addOnSuccessListener { onResult(Result.success(Unit)) }
                    .addOnFailureListener { error -> onResult(Result.failure(error)) }
            }
    }

    fun listenRestaurantReviews(
        restaurantId: String,
        onResult: (Result<List<Review>>) -> Unit
    ): ListenerRegistration = db.collection(RESTAURANTS)
        .document(restaurantId)
        .collection(REVIEWS)
        .addSnapshotListener { snapshot, error ->
            if (error != null) {
                onResult(Result.failure(error))
                return@addSnapshotListener
            }
            if (snapshot == null) {
                onResult(Result.success(emptyList()))
                return@addSnapshotListener
            }
            val reviews = snapshot.documents.mapNotNull { it.toReviewOrNull() }
                .sortedByDescending { it.createdAtMillis }
            onResult(Result.success(reviews))
        }

    fun replyToReview(
        restaurantId: String,
        reviewId: String,
        replyText: String,
        onResult: (Result<Unit>) -> Unit
    ) {
        val updates = mapOf(
            "reply" to replyText.trim(),
            "respuesta" to replyText.trim(),
            "repliedAt" to FieldValue.serverTimestamp()
        )
        db.collection(RESTAURANTS).document(restaurantId)
            .collection(REVIEWS).document(reviewId)
            .set(updates, SetOptions.merge())
            .addOnSuccessListener { onResult(Result.success(Unit)) }
            .addOnFailureListener { onResult(Result.failure(it)) }
    }

    fun listenRestaurantProfile(
        restaurantId: String,
        onResult: (Result<RestaurantProfile>) -> Unit
    ): ListenerRegistration = db.collection(RESTAURANTS)
        .document(restaurantId)
        .addSnapshotListener { snapshot, error ->
            if (error != null) {
                onResult(Result.failure(error))
                return@addSnapshotListener
            }
            if (snapshot == null || !snapshot.exists()) {
                onResult(Result.success(RestaurantProfile(uid = restaurantId)))
                return@addSnapshotListener
            }
            val profile = snapshot.toRestaurantProfile(restaurantId)
            onResult(Result.success(profile))
        }

    private fun DocumentSnapshot.toRestaurantProfile(restaurantId: String): RestaurantProfile {
        return RestaurantProfile(
            uid = restaurantId,
            name = getString("name").orEmpty(),
            address = getString("address") ?: getString("direccion").orEmpty(),
            phone = getString("phone") ?: getString("telefono").orEmpty(),
            email = getString("email") ?: getString("correo").orEmpty(),
            logoUrl = getString("logoUrl") ?: getString("imageUrl") ?: getString("photoUrl"),
            bankName = getString("bankName") ?: getString("banco").orEmpty(),
            accountType = getString("accountType") ?: getString("tipoCuenta") ?: RestaurantProfile.ACCOUNT_TYPE_SAVINGS,
            accountNumber = getString("accountNumber") ?: getString("numeroCuenta").orEmpty(),
            accountHolder = getString("accountHolder") ?: getString("titular").orEmpty(),
            notifyNewOrders = getBoolean("notifyNewOrders") ?: true,
            notifyNewReviews = getBoolean("notifyNewReviews") ?: true,
            notifyAlertSound = getBoolean("notifyAlertSound") ?: true,
            notifyDailySummary = getBoolean("notifyDailySummary") ?: false
        )
    }

    fun saveRestaurantProfile(
        profile: RestaurantProfile,
        onResult: (Result<Unit>) -> Unit
    ) {
        val data = mutableMapOf<String, Any>(
            "name" to profile.name.trim(),
            "address" to profile.address.trim(),
            "phone" to profile.phone.trim(),
            "email" to profile.email.trim(),
            "bankName" to profile.bankName.trim(),
            "accountType" to profile.accountType,
            "accountNumber" to profile.accountNumber.trim(),
            "accountHolder" to profile.accountHolder.trim(),
            "notifyNewOrders" to profile.notifyNewOrders,
            "notifyNewReviews" to profile.notifyNewReviews,
            "notifyAlertSound" to profile.notifyAlertSound,
            "notifyDailySummary" to profile.notifyDailySummary,
            "updatedAt" to FieldValue.serverTimestamp()
        )
        profile.logoUrl?.let { data["logoUrl"] = it }

        db.collection(RESTAURANTS).document(profile.uid)
            .set(data, SetOptions.merge())
            .addOnSuccessListener {
                if (profile.name.isNotBlank() || profile.phone.isNotBlank()) {
                    val userUpdates = mutableMapOf<String, Any>()
                    if (profile.name.isNotBlank()) userUpdates["fullName"] = profile.name.trim()
                    if (profile.phone.isNotBlank()) userUpdates["phone"] = profile.phone.trim()
                    db.collection("users").document(profile.uid)
                        .set(userUpdates, SetOptions.merge())
                }
                onResult(Result.success(Unit))
            }
            .addOnFailureListener { onResult(Result.failure(it)) }
    }

    fun inviteCourier(
        restaurantId: String,
        phone: String,
        onResult: (Result<Unit>) -> Unit
    ) {
        val invitation = mapOf(
            "phone" to phone.trim(),
            "createdAt" to FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
            "createdAtMillis" to System.currentTimeMillis(),
            "status" to "pending",
            "restaurantId" to restaurantId
        )
        db.collection(RESTAURANTS).document(restaurantId)
            .collection("courier_invitations")
            .add(invitation)
            .addOnSuccessListener { onResult(Result.success(Unit)) }
            .addOnFailureListener { onResult(Result.failure(it)) }
    }

    private fun DocumentSnapshot.toReviewOrNull(): Review? {
        val reviewId = id
        val restaurantId = getString("restaurantId") ?: ""
        val customerName = getString("customerName")
            ?: getString("userName")
            ?: getString("clientName")
            ?: getString("name")
            ?: "Cliente"
        val ratingRaw = getDouble("rating")
            ?: getLong("rating")?.toDouble()
            ?: getDouble("stars")
            ?: getLong("stars")?.toDouble()
            ?: 5.0
        val rating = ratingRaw.toFloat().coerceIn(1f, 5f)
        val comment = getString("comment")
            ?: getString("review")
            ?: getString("text")
            ?: getString("comentario")
            ?: ""
        val reply = getString("reply")
            ?: getString("respuesta")
            ?: getString("replyText")
            ?: ""

        val timestamp = getTimestamp("createdAt")
            ?: getTimestamp("timestamp")
            ?: getTimestamp("date")
        val createdAtMillis = timestamp?.toDate()?.time
            ?: getLong("createdAt")
            ?: getLong("timestamp")
            ?: System.currentTimeMillis()

        return Review(
            id = reviewId,
            restaurantId = restaurantId,
            customerName = customerName,
            rating = rating,
            comment = comment,
            createdAtMillis = createdAtMillis,
            reply = reply
        )
    }

    private fun DocumentSnapshot.toRestaurantOrderOrNull(): RestaurantOrder? {
        val orderId = this.id
        val restaurantId = getString("restaurantId")
            ?: getString("restaurantDocumentId")
            ?: getLong("restaurantId")?.toString()
            ?: ""

        val orderNumber = getString("orderNumber")
            ?: getString("code")
            ?: getString("orderCode")
            ?: getLong("orderNumber")?.toString()
            ?: orderId.takeLast(4).uppercase()

        val customerName = getString("customerName")
            ?: getString("clientName")
            ?: getString("userName")
            ?: (get("customer") as? Map<*, *>)?.get("name") as? String
            ?: "Cliente"

        val customerPhone = getString("customerPhone")
            ?: getString("phone")
            ?: getString("telefono")
            ?: (get("customer") as? Map<*, *>)?.get("phone") as? String
            ?: ""

        val customerAddress = getString("customerAddress")
            ?: getString("address")
            ?: getString("direccion")
            ?: getString("deliveryAddress")
            ?: (get("customer") as? Map<*, *>)?.get("address") as? String
            ?: ""

        val customerNote = getString("note")
            ?: getString("notes")
            ?: getString("nota")
            ?: getString("customerNote")
            ?: getString("instructions")
            ?: getString("observaciones")
            ?: ""

        val paymentMethod = getString("paymentMethod")
            ?: getString("metodoPago")
            ?: getString("pago")
            ?: (get("payment") as? Map<*, *>)?.get("method") as? String
            ?: "Efectivo"

        val courierName = getString("courierName")
            ?: getString("driverName")
            ?: getString("repartidorNombre")
            ?: (get("courier") as? Map<*, *>)?.get("name") as? String
            ?: (get("repartidor") as? Map<*, *>)?.get("nombre") as? String
            ?: ""

        val courierPhone = getString("courierPhone")
            ?: getString("driverPhone")
            ?: getString("repartidorTelefono")
            ?: (get("courier") as? Map<*, *>)?.get("phone") as? String
            ?: (get("repartidor") as? Map<*, *>)?.get("telefono") as? String
            ?: ""

        val rawItems = (get("items") ?: get("products") ?: get("lines")) as? List<*>
        val items = rawItems?.mapNotNull { itemAny ->
            when (itemAny) {
                is Map<*, *> -> {
                    val name = itemAny["name"] as? String
                        ?: itemAny["productName"] as? String
                        ?: itemAny["title"] as? String
                        ?: ""
                    val quantity = (itemAny["quantity"] as? Number)?.toInt()
                        ?: (itemAny["cant"] as? Number)?.toInt()
                        ?: 1
                    val price = (itemAny["price"] as? Number)?.toInt()
                        ?: (itemAny["unitPrice"] as? Number)?.toInt()
                        ?: 0
                    val total = (itemAny["total"] as? Number)?.toInt() ?: (price * quantity)
                    if (name.isNotBlank()) OrderItem(name, quantity, price, total) else null
                }
                else -> null
            }
        } ?: emptyList()

        val calculatedSubtotal = items.sumOf { it.total }
        val subtotalCop = (getLong("subtotal")
            ?: getLong("subtotalCop")
            ?: getDouble("subtotal")?.toInt()
            ?: calculatedSubtotal).toInt()

        val deliveryFeeCop = (getLong("deliveryFee")
            ?: getLong("deliveryFeeCop")
            ?: getLong("domicilio")
            ?: getLong("costoEnvio")
            ?: getDouble("deliveryFee")?.toInt()
            ?: getDouble("domicilio")?.toInt()
            ?: 0).toInt()

        val totalCop = (getLong("total")
            ?: getLong("totalCop")
            ?: getLong("totalPrice")
            ?: getDouble("total")?.toInt()
            ?: (subtotalCop + deliveryFeeCop)).toInt()

        val statusRaw = getString("status")
            ?: getString("estado")
            ?: getString("statusKey")
        val status = OrderStatus.fromKey(statusRaw)

        val timestamp = getTimestamp("createdAt")
            ?: getTimestamp("timestamp")
            ?: getTimestamp("date")
        val createdAtMillis = timestamp?.toDate()?.time
            ?: getLong("createdAt")
            ?: getLong("timestamp")
            ?: System.currentTimeMillis()

        return RestaurantOrder(
            id = orderId,
            orderNumber = orderNumber,
            restaurantId = restaurantId,
            customerName = customerName,
            customerPhone = customerPhone,
            customerAddress = customerAddress,
            items = items,
            totalCop = totalCop,
            subtotalCop = subtotalCop,
            deliveryFeeCop = deliveryFeeCop,
            status = status,
            createdAtMillis = createdAtMillis,
            customerNote = customerNote,
            paymentMethod = paymentMethod,
            courierName = courierName,
            courierPhone = courierPhone
        )
    }
}

package co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.data

import com.google.firebase.auth.FirebaseAuth
import com.google.firebase.firestore.FirebaseFirestore
import kotlinx.coroutines.tasks.await
import kotlin.math.roundToInt

class ReviewRepository(
    private val db: FirebaseFirestore = FirebaseFirestore.getInstance(),
    private val auth: FirebaseAuth = FirebaseAuth.getInstance()
) {
    /**
     * Guarda la reseÃ±a y recalcula el promedio del restaurante de forma atÃ³mica.
     */
    suspend fun submitReview(
        orderId: String,
        orderShortCode: String,
        restaurantId: String,
        restaurantName: String,
        stars: Int,
        comment: String
    ): Result<Unit> = runCatching {
        require(stars in 1..5) { "La calificaciÃ³n debe estar entre 1 y 5" }

        val user = auth.currentUser ?: error("Usuario no autenticado")
        val reviewRef = db.collection("reviews").document()
        val restaurantRef = db.collection("restaurants").document(restaurantId)
        val orderRef = db.collection("orders").document(orderId)

        val review = Review(
            id = reviewRef.id,
            orderId = orderId,
            orderShortCode = orderShortCode,
            restaurantId = restaurantId,
            restaurantName = restaurantName,
            userId = user.uid,
            userName = user.displayName ?: "Cliente",
            rating = stars,
            comment = comment.trim(),
            createdAtMillis = System.currentTimeMillis()
        )

        db.runTransaction { tx ->
            val restSnap = tx.get(restaurantRef)
            val currentRating = restSnap.getDouble("rating") ?: 0.0
            val currentCount = (restSnap.getLong("reviewCount") ?: 0L).toInt()

            val newCount = currentCount + 1
            val rawAvg = ((currentRating * currentCount) + stars) / newCount
            // Redondeo a 1 decimal (ej. 4.8)
            val newAverage = (rawAvg * 10.0).roundToInt() / 10.0

            tx.set(reviewRef, review)
            tx.update(
                restaurantRef,
                mapOf(
                    "rating" to newAverage,
                    "reviewCount" to newCount
                )
            )
            tx.update(
                orderRef,
                mapOf(
                    "rated" to true,
                    "userRating" to stars
                )
            )
        }.await()
    }
}

===== C:\Users\vgzar\Documents\UdeA\COMPUTACION MOVIL\PidePues\app\src\main\java\co\edu\udea\compumovil\labs20262_gr08\pidepues\domain\Cart.kt =====
package co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.domain

import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.model.CartLine
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.model.MenuItem
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.model.Restaurant

/** Resultado de intentar agregar un plato. */
enum class AddResult { ADDED, DIFFERENT_RESTAURANT }

/**
 * Carrito de compras: solo admite platos de UN restaurante a la vez.
 * Es Kotlin puro: la lÃ³gica se prueba sin Android.
 */
class Cart {

    var restaurant: Restaurant? = null
        private set

    private val quantities = linkedMapOf<String, CartLine>()

    val lines: List<CartLine> get() = quantities.values.toList()
    val itemCount: Int get() = quantities.values.sumOf { it.quantity }
    val subtotal: Int get() = quantities.values.sumOf { it.total }
    val deliveryFee: Int get() = if (quantities.isEmpty()) 0 else restaurant?.deliveryFee ?: 0
    val serviceFee: Int get() = if (quantities.isEmpty()) 0 else SERVICE_FEE
    val total: Int get() = subtotal + deliveryFee + serviceFee
    val isEmpty: Boolean get() = quantities.isEmpty()

    fun quantityOf(itemId: String): Int = quantities[itemId]?.quantity ?: 0

    /** Agrega una unidad. Si el carrito es de otro restaurante, no agrega y avisa. */
    fun add(from: Restaurant, item: MenuItem): AddResult {
        val current = restaurant
        if (current != null && current.id != from.id && !isEmpty) return AddResult.DIFFERENT_RESTAURANT
        restaurant = from
        val line = quantities[item.id]
        quantities[item.id] = CartLine(item, (line?.quantity ?: 0) + 1)
        return AddResult.ADDED
    }

    /** VacÃ­a el carrito y empieza uno nuevo con este plato. */
    fun replaceWith(from: Restaurant, item: MenuItem) {
        clear()
        add(from, item)
    }

    fun increment(itemId: String) {
        val line = quantities[itemId] ?: return
        quantities[itemId] = line.copy(quantity = line.quantity + 1)
    }

    fun decrement(itemId: String) {
        val line = quantities[itemId] ?: return
        if (line.quantity <= 1) quantities.remove(itemId) else quantities[itemId] = line.copy(quantity = line.quantity - 1)
        if (quantities.isEmpty()) restaurant = null
    }

    fun clear() {
        quantities.clear()
        restaurant = null
    }

    companion object {
        /** Cargo por servicio de PidePues por pedido (en pesos). */
        const val SERVICE_FEE = 1000
    }
}

package co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.domain

import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.model.Restaurant
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.model.RestaurantFilters
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.model.RestaurantWithDistance
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.model.SortOption
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.model.UserLocation
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.util.haversineKm
import java.text.Normalizer

/**
 * logica de cercanÃ­a + calificaciÃ³n
 * Kotlin puro para  probarla con pruebas unitarias
 */
object RestaurantRanking {

    /**distancia a partir de la cual la cercania ya no suma puntos al ranking */
    private const val MAX_REFERENCE_KM = 10.0
    private const val WEIGHT_RATING = 0.6
    private const val WEIGHT_PROXIMITY = 0.4

    /**
     * Puntaje de Recomendados: 60 % calificaciÃ³n + 40 % cercanÃ­a (0 a 1).
     * Es la linea base que en la semana 10 la IA reemplaza/enriquece con preferencias e historial.
     */
    fun recommendationScore(rating: Float, distanceKm: Double): Double {
        val ratingPart = (rating / 5f).toDouble().coerceIn(0.0, 1.0)
        val proximityPart = 1.0 - (distanceKm / MAX_REFERENCE_KM).coerceIn(0.0, 1.0)
        return WEIGHT_RATING * ratingPart + WEIGHT_PROXIMITY * proximityPart
    }

    /**calcula la distancia de cada restaurante al usuario */
    fun withDistance(restaurants: List<Restaurant>, location: UserLocation): List<RestaurantWithDistance> =
        restaurants.map {
            RestaurantWithDistance(
                it,
                haversineKm(location.latitude, location.longitude, it.latitude, it.longitude)
            )
        }

    /**aplica los filtros (distancia, calificaciÃ³n, tipo de cocina) y el orden elegido */
    fun filterAndSort(
        items: List<RestaurantWithDistance>,
        filters: RestaurantFilters
    ): List<RestaurantWithDistance> {
        val filtered = items.filter {
            it.distanceKm <= filters.maxDistanceKm &&
                it.restaurant.rating >= filters.minRating &&
                (filters.categories.isEmpty() || it.restaurant.category in filters.categories)
        }
        val byOption: Comparator<RestaurantWithDistance> = when (filters.sort) {
            SortOption.RECOMMENDED -> compareByDescending {
                recommendationScore(it.restaurant.rating, it.distanceKm)
            }
            SortOption.NEAREST -> compareBy { it.distanceKm }
            SortOption.BEST_RATED -> compareByDescending<RestaurantWithDistance> { it.restaurant.rating }
                .thenBy { it.distanceKm }
            SortOption.FASTEST -> compareBy<RestaurantWithDistance> { it.restaurant.deliveryMinutes }
                .thenBy { it.distanceKm }
        }
        // Los restaurantes cerrados siempre van al final de la lista.
        return filtered.sortedWith(compareByDescending<RestaurantWithDistance> { it.restaurant.isOpen }.then(byOption))
    }

    /**busqueda por texto en nombre, cocina, etiqueta, categorÃ­a y palabras clave */
    fun matchesQuery(restaurant: Restaurant, query: String): Boolean {
        val tokens = query.normalized().split(" ").filter { it.isNotBlank() }
        if (tokens.isEmpty()) return true
        val haystack = listOf(
            restaurant.name, restaurant.cuisine, restaurant.dishTag,
            restaurant.category.label, restaurant.keywords
        ).joinToString(" ").normalized()
        return tokens.all { haystack.contains(it) }
    }

    fun search(items: List<RestaurantWithDistance>, query: String): List<RestaurantWithDistance> =
        items.filter { matchesQuery(it.restaurant, query) }

    private fun String.normalized(): String =
        Normalizer.normalize(this.lowercase(), Normalizer.Form.NFD)
            .replace(Regex("\\p{M}+"), "")
}

package co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.domain

/** Reglas de los formularios de registro e inicio de sesiÃ³n (sin Android, fÃ¡ciles de probar). */
object Validators {

    private val EMAIL = Regex("^[A-Za-z0-9._%+-]+@[A-Za-z0-9.-]+\\.[A-Za-z]{2,}$")
    const val MIN_PASSWORD_LENGTH = 8

    fun isValidEmail(email: String): Boolean = EMAIL.matches(email.trim())

    fun isValidPassword(password: String): Boolean = password.length >= MIN_PASSWORD_LENGTH

    /** Al menos dos letras; el formulario pide "Nombre completo". */
    fun isValidFullName(name: String): Boolean = name.trim().length >= 3 && name.trim().any { it.isLetter() }

    /** Celular colombiano: 10 dÃ­gitos que empiezan por 3, con o sin +57. */
    fun isValidPhone(phone: String): Boolean {
        val digits = phone.filter { it.isDigit() }
        val national = if (digits.length == 12 && digits.startsWith("57")) digits.drop(2) else digits
        return national.length == 10 && national.startsWith("3")
    }

    /** Guarda siempre el celular en formato +57XXXXXXXXXX. */
    fun normalizePhone(phone: String): String {
        val digits = phone.filter { it.isDigit() }
        val national = if (digits.length == 12 && digits.startsWith("57")) digits.drop(2) else digits
        return "+57$national"
    }
}

===== C:\Users\vgzar\Documents\UdeA\COMPUTACION MOVIL\PidePues\app\src\main\java\co\edu\udea\compumovil\labs20262_gr08\pidepues\location\LocationProvider.kt =====
package co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.location

import android.Manifest
import android.annotation.SuppressLint
import android.content.Context
import android.content.pm.PackageManager
import android.location.Location
import android.location.LocationListener
import android.location.LocationManager
import android.os.Bundle
import android.os.Handler
import android.os.Looper
import androidx.core.content.ContextCompat
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.model.UserLocation
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.util.haversineKm

/**
 * Obtiene la ubicaciÃ³n del dispositivo con el LocationManager del sistema
 * (sin dependencias extra). Si algo falla (sin permiso, GPS apagado, tiempo agotado)
 * devuelve null y la app usa [DEMO_CENTER].
 *
 * Mejora futura (semana 11): migrar a FusedLocationProviderClient (Google Play Services).
 */
object LocationProvider {

    /** UbicaciÃ³n por defecto: El Poblado, MedellÃ­n (donde estÃ¡n los datos de ejemplo). */
    val DEMO_CENTER = UserLocation(latitude = 6.2088, longitude = -75.5671, isFromDevice = false)

    /**
     * Mientras los restaurantes sean datos de ejemplo de MedellÃ­n, una ubicaciÃ³n real
     * a mÃ¡s de este radio se ignora (si no, la lista saldrÃ­a vacÃ­a en otra ciudad).
     */
    const val DEMO_RADIUS_KM = 60.0

    private const val TIMEOUT_MS = 8_000L

    fun hasPermission(context: Context): Boolean =
        ContextCompat.checkSelfPermission(context, Manifest.permission.ACCESS_FINE_LOCATION) ==
            PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED ||
            ContextCompat.checkSelfPermission(context, Manifest.permission.ACCESS_COARSE_LOCATION) ==
            PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED

    @SuppressLint("MissingPermission")
    fun getCurrentLocation(context: Context, onResult: (UserLocation?) -> Unit) {
        if (!hasPermission(context)) {
            onResult(null)
            return
        }
        val manager = context.getSystemService(Context.LOCATION_SERVICE) as? LocationManager
        if (manager == null) {
            onResult(null)
            return
        }

        val providers = runCatching { manager.getProviders(true) }.getOrDefault(emptyList())

        // 1) Ãšltima ubicaciÃ³n conocida (instantÃ¡nea)
        val last = providers
            .mapNotNull { runCatching { manager.getLastKnownLocation(it) }.getOrNull() }
            .maxByOrNull { it.time }
        if (last != null) {
            onResult(last.toUserLocationOrNull())
            return
        }

        // 2) Si no hay, pedimos una sola actualizaciÃ³n (con tiempo mÃ¡ximo)
        val provider = when {
            LocationManager.NETWORK_PROVIDER in providers -> LocationManager.NETWORK_PROVIDER
            LocationManager.GPS_PROVIDER in providers -> LocationManager.GPS_PROVIDER
            else -> null
        }
        if (provider == null) {
            onResult(null)
            return
        }
        SingleUpdate(manager, onResult).start(provider)
    }

    private fun Location.toUserLocationOrNull(): UserLocation? {
        val km = haversineKm(latitude, longitude, DEMO_CENTER.latitude, DEMO_CENTER.longitude)
        return if (km <= DEMO_RADIUS_KM) UserLocation(latitude, longitude, isFromDevice = true) else null
    }

    private class SingleUpdate(
        private val manager: LocationManager,
        private val onResult: (UserLocation?) -> Unit
    ) : LocationListener {

        private val handler = Handler(Looper.getMainLooper())
        private var done = false

        @SuppressLint("MissingPermission")
        @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
        fun start(provider: String) {
            try {
                manager.requestSingleUpdate(provider, this, Looper.getMainLooper())
                handler.postDelayed({ finish(null) }, TIMEOUT_MS)
            } catch (e: SecurityException) {
                finish(null)
            }
        }

        private fun finish(result: UserLocation?) {
            if (done) return
            done = true
            handler.removeCallbacksAndMessages(null)
            runCatching { manager.removeUpdates(this) }
            onResult(result)
        }

        override fun onLocationChanged(location: Location) {
            finish(location.toUserLocationOrNull())
        }

        // En Android 9 o menor estos mÃ©todos son abstractos: hay que implementarlos.
        @Deprecated("Deprecated in Java")
        override fun onStatusChanged(provider: String?, status: Int, extras: Bundle?) = Unit
        override fun onProviderEnabled(provider: String) = Unit
        override fun onProviderDisabled(provider: String) = Unit
    }
}

/** Rol de la cuenta. [key] es el valor guardado en Firestore (users/{uid}.role). */
enum class UserRole(val key: String) {
    CLIENT("cliente"),
    RESTAURANT("restaurante"),
    DELIVERY("domiciliario");

    companion object {
        fun fromKey(key: String?): UserRole = values().firstOrNull { it.key == key } ?: CLIENT
    }
}

data class UserProfile(
    val uid: String,
    val fullName: String,
    val email: String,
    val phone: String,
    val role: UserRole
) {
    val firstName: String get() = fullName.trim().substringBefore(" ")

    val initials: String
        get() = fullName.trim().split(" ").filter { it.isNotBlank() }
            .take(2).joinToString("") { it.first().uppercase() }
}

package co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.model

import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.R
import java.text.SimpleDateFormat
import java.util.Calendar
import java.util.Date
import java.util.Locale

/** Estados del pedido segÃºn el flujo operativo de la app. */
enum class OrderStatus(val key: String, val label: String) {
    SOLICITADO("solicitado", "Solicitado"),
    TOMADO("tomado", "Tomado"),
    PREPARADO("preparado", "Preparado"),
    EN_CAMINO("en_camino", "En camino"),
    ENTREGADO("entregado", "Entregado");

    /** true mientras el pedido no ha sido entregado. */
    val isActive: Boolean get() = this != ENTREGADO

    /** Siguiente estado, o null si ya fue entregado. */
    fun next(): OrderStatus? = entries.getOrNull(ordinal + 1)

    fun labelRes(): Int = when (this) {
        SOLICITADO -> R.string.orders_status_solicitado
        TOMADO -> R.string.orders_status_tomado
        PREPARADO -> R.string.orders_status_preparado
        EN_CAMINO -> R.string.orders_status_en_camino
        ENTREGADO -> R.string.orders_status_entregado
    }

    companion object {
        fun fromKey(key: String?): OrderStatus {
            if (key == null) return SOLICITADO
            val normalized = key.trim().lowercase().replace(" ", "_")
            return when (normalized) {
                "solicitado", "nuevo", "pending", "requested", "received" -> SOLICITADO
                "tomado", "aceptado", "en_preparacion", "in_progress", "preparing" -> TOMADO
                "preparado", "listo", "ready" -> PREPARADO
                "en_camino", "on_the_way", "dispatched", "delivering" -> EN_CAMINO
                "entregado", "completado", "completed", "delivered" -> ENTREGADO
                else -> entries.firstOrNull { it.key.equals(normalized, ignoreCase = true) } ?: SOLICITADO
            }
        }
    }
}

/** Formas de pago (por ahora se coordinan al recibir el pedido; no se procesa ningÃºn cobro en lÃ­nea). */
enum class PaymentMethod(val key: String) {
    CARD("tarjeta"),
    NEQUI("nequi"),
    DAVIPLATA("daviplata"),
    CASH("efectivo");

    companion object {
        fun fromKey(key: String?): PaymentMethod = entries.firstOrNull { it.key == key } ?: CASH
    }
}

    val name: String,
    val emoji: String,
    val unitPrice: Int,
    val quantity: Int
) {
    val total: Int get() = unitPrice * quantity
}

/** Pedido guardado en Firestore (orders/{id}) - vista cliente. */
data class Order(
    val id: String,
    val restaurantId: Int,
    val restaurantName: String,
    val restaurantEmoji: String,
    val lines: List<OrderLine>,
    val subtotal: Int,
    val deliveryFee: Int,
    val serviceFee: Int,
    val total: Int,
    val address: String,
    val instructions: String,
    val paymentMethod: PaymentMethod,
    val status: OrderStatus,
    val createdAtMillis: Long,
    /** Minutos totales estimados al crear el pedido (los del restaurante). */
    val etaMinutes: Int,
    val driverName: String? = null,
    val driverRating: Double? = null
) {
    /** CÃ³digo corto para mostrar, ej. "#PP-AB3XK". */
    val shortCode: String get() = "#PP-" + id.take(5).uppercase()

    val itemCount: Int get() = lines.sumOf { it.quantity }

    /**
     * Minutos que faltan segÃºn el estado: baja a medida que el pedido avanza.
     * (EstimaciÃ³n simple; cuando haya domiciliarios reales se reemplaza por su ruta.)
     */
    fun remainingMinutes(): Int = when (status) {
        OrderStatus.SOLICITADO -> etaMinutes
        OrderStatus.TOMADO -> maxOf(10, (etaMinutes * 0.75).toInt())
        OrderStatus.PREPARADO -> maxOf(8, (etaMinutes * 0.55).toInt())
        OrderStatus.EN_CAMINO -> maxOf(5, (etaMinutes * 0.35).toInt())
        OrderStatus.ENTREGADO -> 0
    }
}

)

/** Pedido real de Firestore asociado a un restaurante. */
data class RestaurantOrder(
    val id: String,
    val orderNumber: String,
    val restaurantId: String,
    val customerName: String,
    val customerPhone: String = "",
    val customerAddress: String = "",
    val items: List<OrderItem> = emptyList(),
    val totalCop: Int = 0,
    val subtotalCop: Int = 0,
    val deliveryFeeCop: Int = 0,
    val status: OrderStatus = OrderStatus.SOLICITADO,
    val createdAtMillis: Long = System.currentTimeMillis(),
    val customerNote: String = "",
    val paymentMethod: String = "Efectivo",
    val courierName: String = "",
    val courierPhone: String = "",
) {
    val itemCount: Int
        get() = if (items.isNotEmpty()) items.sumOf { it.quantity } else 0

    val itemsSummary: String
        get() = items.joinToString(", ") { "${it.quantity}Ã— ${it.name}" }

    val formattedTime: String
        get() {
            val sdf = SimpleDateFormat("HH:mm", Locale.getDefault())
            return sdf.format(Date(createdAtMillis))
        }

    /** Fecha relativa y hora: ej. "Hoy Â· 12:41" o "20 nov Â· 12:41" */
    val formattedDateTime: String
        get() {
            val now = Calendar.getInstance()
            val orderCal = Calendar.getInstance().apply { timeInMillis = createdAtMillis }
            val timeStr = SimpleDateFormat("HH:mm", Locale.getDefault()).format(Date(createdAtMillis))
            return when {
                now.get(Calendar.YEAR) == orderCal.get(Calendar.YEAR) &&
                        now.get(Calendar.DAY_OF_YEAR) == orderCal.get(Calendar.DAY_OF_YEAR) -> "Hoy Â· $timeStr"
                now.get(Calendar.YEAR) == orderCal.get(Calendar.YEAR) &&
                        now.get(Calendar.DAY_OF_YEAR) - orderCal.get(Calendar.DAY_OF_YEAR) == 1 -> "Ayer Â· $timeStr"
                else -> {
                    val dateStr = SimpleDateFormat("dd MMM", Locale.getDefault()).format(Date(createdAtMillis))
                    "$dateStr Â· $timeStr"
                }
            }
        }

    val customerInitial: String
        get() = customerName.trim().firstOrNull()?.uppercaseChar()?.toString() ?: "C"

    val stepIndex: Int
        get() = when (status) {
            OrderStatus.SOLICITADO -> 1
            OrderStatus.TOMADO -> 2
            OrderStatus.PREPARADO -> 3
            OrderStatus.EN_CAMINO -> 4
            OrderStatus.ENTREGADO -> 5
        }

    val isActive: Boolean
        get() = status != OrderStatus.ENTREGADO
}

===== C:\Users\vgzar\Documents\UdeA\COMPUTACION MOVIL\PidePues\app\src\main\java\co\edu\udea\compumovil\labs20262_gr08\pidepues\model\Review.kt =====
package co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.model

import java.text.SimpleDateFormat
import java.util.Calendar
import java.util.Date
import java.util.Locale

/** ReseÃ±a de un cliente sobre un restaurante. */
data class Review(
    val id: String,
    val restaurantId: String,
    val customerName: String,
    val rating: Float = 5f,
    val comment: String = "",
    val createdAtMillis: Long = System.currentTimeMillis(),
    val reply: String = ""
) {
    val customerInitial: String
        get() = customerName.trim().firstOrNull()?.uppercaseChar()?.toString() ?: "C"

    val ratingInt: Int
        get() = rating.toInt().coerceIn(1, 5)

    val hasReply: Boolean
        get() = reply.isNotBlank()

    val formattedRelativeDate: String
        get() {
            val todayStart = Calendar.getInstance().apply {
                set(Calendar.HOUR_OF_DAY, 0)
                set(Calendar.MINUTE, 0)
                set(Calendar.SECOND, 0)
                set(Calendar.MILLISECOND, 0)
            }.timeInMillis

            val yesterdayStart = Calendar.getInstance().apply {
                timeInMillis = todayStart
                add(Calendar.DAY_OF_MONTH, -1)
            }.timeInMillis

            val reviewStartOfDay = Calendar.getInstance().apply {
                timeInMillis = createdAtMillis
                set(Calendar.HOUR_OF_DAY, 0)
                set(Calendar.MINUTE, 0)
                set(Calendar.SECOND, 0)
                set(Calendar.MILLISECOND, 0)
            }.timeInMillis

            return when {
                reviewStartOfDay >= todayStart -> "Hoy"
                reviewStartOfDay >= yesterdayStart -> "Ayer"
                else -> {
                    val diffDays = ((todayStart - reviewStartOfDay) / (24 * 60 * 60 * 1000)).toInt()
                    if (diffDays in 2..30) {
                        "Hace $diffDays dÃ­as"
                    } else {
                        SimpleDateFormat("dd MMM", Locale.getDefault()).format(Date(createdAtMillis))
                    }
                }
            }
        }
}

package co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.ui.auth

import androidx.compose.foundation.*
import androidx.compose.foundation.interaction.*
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.*

import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.ui.theme.*
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.ui.components.*
import android.widget.Toast
import androidx.compose.ui.platform.LocalContext
import androidx.lifecycle.compose.collectAsStateWithLifecycle
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.domain.Validators
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.model.UserProfile
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.ui.main.AuthViewModel

@Composable
fun LoginScreen(
    authViewModel: AuthViewModel,
    onLoggedIn: (UserProfile) -> Unit,
    onRegister: () -> Unit
) {
    StatusBarAppearance(darkIcons = true)
    val context = LocalContext.current
    val state by authViewModel.state.collectAsStateWithLifecycle()

    var email by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf("") }
    var password by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf("") }
    var emailError by remember { mutableStateOf<String?>(null) }
    var passwordError by remember { mutableStateOf<String?>(null) }

    val errorEmail = stringResource(R.string.error_email)
    val errorPasswordRequired = stringResource(R.string.error_password_required)
    val errorResetEmail = stringResource(R.string.reset_enter_email)
    val googleSoon = stringResource(R.string.google_soon)

    // Muestra los mensajes (errores o avisos) del ViewModel una sola vez
    LaunchedEffect(state.messageRes) {
        val message = state.messageRes
        if (message != null) {
            Toast.makeText(context, message, Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show()
            authViewModel.clearMessage()
        }
    }

    fun submit() {
        emailError = if (Validators.isValidEmail(email)) null else errorEmail
        passwordError = if (password.isEmpty()) errorPasswordRequired else null
        if (emailError == null && passwordError == null) {
            authViewModel.login(email.trim(), password, onLoggedIn)
        }
    }

    Column(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxSize()
            .background(Color.White)
            .verticalScroll(rememberScrollState())
            .statusBarsPadding()
            .navigationBarsPadding()
            .imePadding()
            .padding(horizontal = 24.dp, vertical = 16.dp)
    ) {
        Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
            Box(
                modifier = Modifier.size(40.dp).clip(RoundedCornerShape(12.dp)).background(Primary),
                contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
            ) {
                Icon(
                    painter = painterResource(R.drawable.ic_restaurant),
                    contentDescription = stringResource(R.string.cd_logo),
                    tint = Color.White,
                    modifier = Modifier.size(22.dp)
                )
            }
            Text(
                text = stringResource(R.string.app_name),
                color = Ink,
                fontSize = 20.sp,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                modifier = Modifier.padding(start = 12.dp)
            )
        }

        Text(
            text = stringResource(R.string.login_title),
            color = Ink,
            fontSize = 30.sp,
            lineHeight = 36.sp,
            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
            modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 32.dp)
        )
        Text(
            text = stringResource(R.string.login_subtitle),
            color = Muted,
            fontSize = 14.sp,
            modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 8.dp)
        )

        AppTextField(
            label = stringResource(R.string.label_email),
            value = email,
            onValueChange = { email = it },
            placeholder = stringResource(R.string.hint_email),
            error = emailError,
            keyboardType = KeyboardType.Email,
            autoCorrect = false,
            modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 32.dp)
        )
        AppTextField(
            label = stringResource(R.string.label_password),
            value = password,
            onValueChange = { password = it },
            placeholder = stringResource(R.string.hint_password_login),
            error = passwordError,
            keyboardType = KeyboardType.Password,
            autoCorrect = false,
            isPassword = true,
            imeAction = ImeAction.Done,
            onImeAction = { submit() },
            modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 16.dp)
        )

        Row(Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(top = 8.dp), horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.End) {
            LinkText(
                text = stringResource(R.string.login_forgot),
                fontSize = 13.sp,
                onClick = {
                    if (Validators.isValidEmail(email)) {
                        authViewModel.sendPasswordReset(email.trim())
                    } else {
                        emailError = errorResetEmail
                    }
                }
            )
        }

        PrimaryButton(
            text = stringResource(if (state.isLoading) R.string.login_loading else R.string.login_button),
            onClick = { submit() },
            enabled = !state.isLoading,
            modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 12.dp)
        )

        Row(
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(top = 24.dp),
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
        ) {
            HorizontalDivider(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f), color = OutlineGray)
            Text(
                text = stringResource(R.string.login_or_continue),
                color = Hint,
                fontSize = 12.sp,
                modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = 12.dp)
            )
            HorizontalDivider(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f), color = OutlineGray)
        }

        OutlinedActionButton(
            text = stringResource(R.string.login_google),
            onClick = { Toast.makeText(context, googleSoon, Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show() },
            modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 20.dp),
            leadingIcon = {
                Icon(
                    painter = painterResource(R.drawable.ic_google_g),
                    contentDescription = null,
                    tint = Color.Unspecified,
                    modifier = Modifier.size(22.dp)
                )
            }
        )

        Row(
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(top = 32.dp),
            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.Center,
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
        ) {
            Text(text = stringResource(R.string.login_no_account), color = Muted, fontSize = 14.sp)
            LinkText(text = stringResource(R.string.login_register_link), onClick = onRegister)
        }
    }
}

package co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.ui.auth

import androidx.compose.foundation.*
import androidx.compose.foundation.interaction.*
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.*

import android.widget.Toast
import androidx.compose.ui.platform.LocalContext
import androidx.lifecycle.compose.collectAsStateWithLifecycle
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.domain.Validators
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.model.UserProfile
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.model.UserRole
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.ui.main.AuthViewModel

@OptIn(ExperimentalLayoutApi::class)
@Composable
fun RegisterScreen(
    authViewModel: AuthViewModel,
    onBack: () -> Unit,
    onRegistered: (UserProfile) -> Unit
) {
    StatusBarAppearance(darkIcons = true)
    val context = LocalContext.current
    val state by authViewModel.state.collectAsStateWithLifecycle()

    var fullName by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf("") }
    var email by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf("") }
    var phone by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf("") }
    var password by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf("") }
    var role by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(UserRole.CLIENT) }
    var acceptedTerms by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(false) }

    var nameError by remember { mutableStateOf<String?>(null) }
    var emailError by remember { mutableStateOf<String?>(null) }
    var phoneError by remember { mutableStateOf<String?>(null) }
    var passwordError by remember { mutableStateOf<String?>(null) }

    val errorName = stringResource(R.string.error_name)
    val errorEmail = stringResource(R.string.error_email)
    val errorPhone = stringResource(R.string.error_phone)
    val errorPassword = stringResource(R.string.error_password)

    LaunchedEffect(state.messageRes) {
        val message = state.messageRes
        if (message != null) {
            Toast.makeText(context, message, Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show()
            authViewModel.clearMessage()
        }
    }

    fun submit() {
        nameError = if (Validators.isValidFullName(fullName)) null else errorName
        emailError = if (Validators.isValidEmail(email)) null else errorEmail
        phoneError = if (Validators.isValidPhone(phone)) null else errorPhone
        passwordError = if (Validators.isValidPassword(password)) null else errorPassword
        val valid = nameError == null && emailError == null && phoneError == null && passwordError == null
        if (valid) {
            authViewModel.register(fullName, email, phone, password, role, onRegistered)
        }
    }

    // "TÃ©rminos de servicio" y "PolÃ­tica de privacidad" resaltados en naranja
    val termsPrefix = stringResource(R.string.register_terms_prefix)
    val termsService = stringResource(R.string.register_terms_service)
    val termsAnd = stringResource(R.string.register_terms_and)
    val termsPrivacy = stringResource(R.string.register_terms_privacy)
    val termsText = buildAnnotatedString {
        append(termsPrefix)
        withStyle(SpanStyle(color = Primary, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)) { append(termsService) }
        append(termsAnd)
        withStyle(SpanStyle(color = Primary, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)) { append(termsPrivacy) }
    }

    Column(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxSize()
            .background(Color.White)
            .verticalScroll(rememberScrollState())
            .statusBarsPadding()
            .navigationBarsPadding()
            .imePadding()
            .padding(horizontal = 24.dp, vertical = 16.dp)
    ) {
        CircleIconButton(
            icon = R.drawable.ic_arrow_back,
            onClick = onBack,
            contentDescription = stringResource(R.string.cd_back)
        )

        Text(
            text = stringResource(R.string.register_title),
            color = Ink,
            fontSize = 30.sp,
            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
            modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 24.dp)
        )
        Text(
            text = stringResource(R.string.register_subtitle),
            color = Muted,
            fontSize = 14.sp,
            modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 8.dp)
        )

        // MayÃºscula inicial y sin sugerencias en el nombre
        AppTextField(
            label = stringResource(R.string.label_full_name),
            value = fullName,
            onValueChange = { fullName = it },
            placeholder = stringResource(R.string.hint_full_name),
            error = nameError,
            capitalization = KeyboardCapitalization.Words,
            autoCorrect = false,
            modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 28.dp)
        )
        AppTextField(
            label = stringResource(R.string.label_email),
            value = email,
            onValueChange = { email = it },
            placeholder = stringResource(R.string.hint_email),
            error = emailError,
            keyboardType = KeyboardType.Email,
            autoCorrect = false,
            modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 16.dp)
        )
        AppTextField(
            label = stringResource(R.string.label_phone),
            value = phone,
            onValueChange = { phone = it },
            placeholder = stringResource(R.string.hint_phone),
            error = phoneError,
            keyboardType = KeyboardType.Phone,
            modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 16.dp)
        )
        AppTextField(
            label = stringResource(R.string.label_password),
            value = password,
            onValueChange = { password = it },
            placeholder = stringResource(R.string.hint_password_register),
            error = passwordError,
            keyboardType = KeyboardType.Password,
            autoCorrect = false,
            isPassword = true,
            imeAction = ImeAction.Done,
            modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 16.dp)
        )

        // Tipo de cuenta
        Text(
            text = stringResource(R.string.role_label),
            color = InkSoft,
            fontSize = 14.sp,
            fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium,
            modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 16.dp, bottom = 8.dp)
        )
        FlowRow(
            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp),
            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)
        ) {
            PidePuesChip(stringResource(R.string.role_client), role == UserRole.CLIENT, { role = UserRole.CLIENT })
            PidePuesChip(stringResource(R.string.role_restaurant), role == UserRole.RESTAURANT, { role = UserRole.RESTAURANT })
            PidePuesChip(stringResource(R.string.role_delivery), role == UserRole.DELIVERY, { role = UserRole.DELIVERY })
        }

        Row(
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(top = 12.dp),
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
        ) {
            Checkbox(
                checked = acceptedTerms,
                onCheckedChange = { acceptedTerms = it },
                colors = CheckboxDefaults.colors(checkedColor = Primary)
            )
            Text(text = termsText, color = InkSoft, fontSize = 14.sp)
        }

        // Deshabilitado (salmÃ³n) hasta aceptar los tÃ©rminos
        PrimaryButton(
            text = stringResource(if (state.isLoading) R.string.register_loading else R.string.register_button),
            onClick = { submit() },
            enabled = acceptedTerms && !state.isLoading,
            modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 16.dp)
        )

        Row(
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(top = 24.dp),
            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.Center,
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
        ) {
            Text(text = stringResource(R.string.register_have_account), color = Muted, fontSize = 14.sp)
            LinkText(text = stringResource(R.string.register_login_link), onClick = onBack)
        }
    }
}

package co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.ui.cart

import androidx.compose.foundation.*
import androidx.compose.foundation.interaction.*
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.*
import androidx.compose.foundation.lazy.*
import androidx.compose.foundation.shape.*
