/** Carrito: platos, cantidades y resumen del pedido (la confirmaciÃ³n llega en la semana 8). */
@Composable
fun CartScreen(
    cartViewModel: CartViewModel,
    onExplore: () -> Unit,
    onCheckout: () -> Unit
) {
    StatusBarAppearance(darkIcons = true)
    val cart by cartViewModel.state.collectAsStateWithLifecycle()

    Column(Modifier.fillMaxSize().background(ScreenBg)) {
        Row(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .shadow(2.dp)
                .background(Color.White)
                .statusBarsPadding()
                .padding(horizontal = 20.dp, vertical = 14.dp),
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
        ) {
            Text(
                text = stringResource(R.string.cart_screen_title),
                color = Ink,
                fontSize = 22.sp,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)
            )
            if (!cart.isEmpty) {
                LinkText(text = stringResource(R.string.cart_clear), onClick = { cartViewModel.clear() })
            }
        }

        if (cart.isEmpty) {
            Column(
                modifier = Modifier.weight(1f).fillMaxWidth().padding(32.dp),
                horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
                verticalArrangement = Arrangement.Center
            ) {
                Text(text = stringResource(R.string.cart_emoji), fontSize = 64.sp)
                Text(
                    text = stringResource(R.string.cart_title),
                    color = Ink,
                    fontSize = 18.sp,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                    modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 16.dp)
                )
                Text(
                    text = stringResource(R.string.cart_subtitle),
                    color = Muted,
                    fontSize = 14.sp,
                    textAlign = TextAlign.Center,
                    modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 8.dp)
                )
                PrimaryButton(
                    text = stringResource(R.string.cart_explore),
                    onClick = onExplore,
                    fillWidth = false,
                    modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 24.dp)
                )
            }
        } else {
            LazyColumn(
                modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
                contentPadding = PaddingValues(16.dp)
            ) {
                item {
                    Text(
                        text = stringResource(R.string.cart_from, cart.restaurant?.name.orEmpty()),
                        color = Ink,
                        fontSize = 16.sp,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
                    )
                }
                item {
                    AppCard(Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(top = 12.dp)) {
                        Column(Modifier.padding(horizontal = 16.dp)) {
                            cart.lines.forEachIndexed { index, line ->
                                if (index > 0) HorizontalDivider(color = DividerGray)
                                Row(
                                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(vertical = 12.dp),
                                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                                ) {
                                    EmojiTile(
                                        emoji = line.item.emoji,
                                        background = TileOrange,
                                        size = 48.dp,
                                        fontSize = 24.sp
                                    )
                                    Column(Modifier.weight(1f).padding(horizontal = 12.dp)) {
                                        Text(line.item.name, color = Ink, fontSize = 15.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                                        Text(
                                            text = stringResource(R.string.cart_unit_price, line.item.price.toCop()),
                                            color = Muted,
                                            fontSize = 12.sp,
                                            modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 2.dp)
                                        )
                                        Text(
                                            text = line.total.toCop(),
                                            color = Primary,
                                            fontSize = 14.sp,
                                            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                                            modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 2.dp)
                                        )
                                    }
                                    QuantityStepper(
                                        quantity = line.quantity,
                                        onMinus = { cartViewModel.decrement(line.item.id) },
                                        onPlus = { cartViewModel.increment(line.item.id) }
                                    )
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
                item {
                    AppCard(Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(top = 16.dp)) {
                        Column(Modifier.padding(16.dp)) {
                            SummaryRow(stringResource(R.string.summary_subtotal), cart.subtotal.toCop())
                            SummaryRow(
                                label = stringResource(R.string.summary_delivery),
                                value = if (cart.deliveryFee == 0) stringResource(R.string.delivery_free) else cart.deliveryFee.toCop(),
                                modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 8.dp)
                            )
                            SummaryRow(
                                label = stringResource(R.string.summary_service),
                                value = cart.serviceFee.toCop(),
                                modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 8.dp)
                            )
                            HorizontalDivider(color = DividerGray, modifier = Modifier.padding(vertical = 12.dp))
                            SummaryRow(
                                label = stringResource(R.string.summary_total),
                                value = cart.total.toCop(),
                                emphasized = true
                            )
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            Column(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .shadow(8.dp)
                    .background(Color.White)
                    .padding(16.dp)
            ) {
                PrimaryButton(
                    text = stringResource(R.string.checkout_format, cart.total.toCop()),
                    onClick = onCheckout
                )
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun SummaryRow(
    label: String,
    value: String,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    emphasized: Boolean = false
) {
    Row(modifier = modifier.fillMaxWidth()) {
        Text(
            text = label,
            color = if (emphasized) Ink else Muted,
            fontSize = if (emphasized) 16.sp else 14.sp,
            fontWeight = if (emphasized) FontWeight.Bold else FontWeight.Normal,
            modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)
        )
        Text(
            text = value,
            color = if (emphasized) Primary else Ink,
            fontSize = if (emphasized) 18.sp else 14.sp,
            fontWeight = if (emphasized) FontWeight.Bold else FontWeight.Normal
        )
    }
}

package co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.ui.checkout

import androidx.compose.foundation.*
import androidx.compose.foundation.interaction.*

import androidx.compose.ui.text.font.*
import androidx.compose.ui.text.input.*
import androidx.compose.ui.text.style.*

import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.R
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.ui.theme.*

import androidx.lifecycle.compose.collectAsStateWithLifecycle
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.model.DeliveryAddress
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.ui.main.CheckoutViewModel
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.ui.main.RestaurantsViewModel
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.ui.main.SessionViewModel

/** Paso 1 del pedido: elegir dÃ³nde se entrega (ubicaciÃ³n actual o una direcciÃ³n guardada). */
@Composable
fun DeliveryAddressScreen(
    checkoutViewModel: CheckoutViewModel,
    restaurantsViewModel: RestaurantsViewModel,
    sessionViewModel: SessionViewModel,
    onBack: () -> Unit,
    onConfirm: () -> Unit
) {
    StatusBarAppearance(darkIcons = true)
    val checkout by checkoutViewModel.state.collectAsStateWithLifecycle()
    val restaurants by restaurantsViewModel.state.collectAsStateWithLifecycle()
    val profile by sessionViewModel.profile.collectAsStateWithLifecycle()
    var showAddDialog by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }

    Column(Modifier.fillMaxSize().background(ScreenBg)) {
        AppTopBar(title = stringResource(R.string.address_title), onBack = onBack)

        Column(
            modifier = Modifier
                .weight(1f)
                .verticalScroll(rememberScrollState())
                .padding(16.dp)
        ) {
            MapCard(height = 160.dp) {
                MapPin(Modifier.align(Alignment.Center))
            }

            // ----- UbicaciÃ³n actual -----
            Text(
                text = stringResource(R.string.address_current_title),
                color = Ink,
                fontSize = 16.sp,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 20.dp, bottom = 8.dp)
            )
            AddressOption(
                emoji = "ðŸ“",
                title = stringResource(R.string.address_current_title),
                subtitle = stringResource(
                    if (restaurants.location.isFromDevice) R.string.address_current_device
                    else R.string.address_current_default
                ),
                selected = checkout.useCurrentLocation,
                onClick = { checkoutViewModel.selectCurrentLocation() }
            )

            // ----- Direcciones guardadas -----
            Text(
                text = stringResource(R.string.address_saved_title),
                color = Ink,
                fontSize = 16.sp,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 20.dp, bottom = 8.dp)
            )
            if (checkout.addresses.isEmpty()) {
                Text(
                    text = stringResource(R.string.address_saved_empty),
                    color = Muted,
                    fontSize = 13.sp,
                    modifier = Modifier.padding(bottom = 8.dp)
                )
            }
            checkout.addresses.forEach { address ->
                AddressOption(
                    emoji = address.emoji,
                    title = address.label,
                    subtitle = address.text,
                    selected = !checkout.useCurrentLocation && checkout.selectedAddressId == address.id,
                    onClick = { checkoutViewModel.selectSaved(address.id) },
                    modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 8.dp)
                )
            }
            Row(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .padding(top = 12.dp)
                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(14.dp))
                    .border(1.dp, Primary, RoundedCornerShape(14.dp))
                    .clickable { showAddDialog = true }
                    .padding(14.dp),
                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.Center,
                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
            ) {
                Icon(
                    painter = painterResource(R.drawable.ic_add),
                    contentDescription = null,
                    tint = Primary,
                    modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp)
                )
                Text(
                    text = stringResource(R.string.address_add),
                    color = Primary,
                    fontSize = 14.sp,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                    modifier = Modifier.padding(start = 8.dp)
                )
            }

            // ----- Instrucciones -----
            Row(
                modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 24.dp, bottom = 8.dp),
                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
            ) {
                Text(
                    text = stringResource(R.string.address_instructions_title),
                    color = Ink,
                    fontSize = 16.sp,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
                )
                Text(
                    text = stringResource(R.string.address_optional),
                    color = Muted,
                    fontSize = 11.sp,
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .padding(start = 8.dp)
                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(8.dp))
                        .background(DividerGray)
                        .padding(horizontal = 8.dp, vertical = 2.dp)
                )
            }
            AppTextField(
                label = "",
                value = checkout.instructions,
                onValueChange = { checkoutViewModel.setInstructions(it) },
                placeholder = stringResource(R.string.address_instructions_hint),
                singleLine = false,
                minLines = 3
            )
        }

        BottomActionBar {
            PrimaryButton(
                text = stringResource(R.string.address_confirm),
                onClick = onConfirm
            )
        }
    }

    if (showAddDialog) {
        AddAddressDialog(
            onDismiss = { showAddDialog = false },
            onSave = { label, text ->
                val uid = profile?.uid
                if (uid != null) {
                    checkoutViewModel.addAddress(uid, label, emojiForLabel(label), text)
                }
                showAddDialog = false
            }
        )
    }
}

/** Casa â†’ ðŸ , Oficina/Trabajo â†’ ðŸ¢, otro â†’ ðŸ“. */
private fun emojiForLabel(label: String): String {
    val l = label.lowercase()
    return when {
        l.contains("casa") || l.contains("hogar") -> "ðŸ "
        l.contains("oficina") || l.contains("trabajo") -> "ðŸ¢"
        else -> "ðŸ“"
    }
}

@Composable
private fun AddressOption(
    emoji: String,
    title: String,
    subtitle: String,
    selected: Boolean,
    onClick: () -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
) {
    Row(
        modifier = modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(14.dp))
            .background(if (selected) TileOrange else Color.White)
            .border(1.dp, if (selected) Primary else OutlineGray, RoundedCornerShape(14.dp))
            .clickable(onClick = onClick)
            .padding(12.dp),
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
    ) {
        EmojiTile(emoji = emoji, background = if (selected) Color.White else TileGray, size = 44.dp)
        Column(Modifier.weight(1f).padding(horizontal = 12.dp)) {
            Text(title, color = Ink, fontSize = 15.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
            Text(subtitle, color = Muted, fontSize = 12.sp, modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 2.dp))
        }
        RadioButton(
            selected = selected,
            onClick = null,
            colors = RadioButtonDefaults.colors(selectedColor = Primary, unselectedColor = OutlineGray)
        )
    }
}

@Composable
private fun AddAddressDialog(onDismiss: () -> Unit, onSave: (String, String) -> Unit) {
    var label by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var text by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var showError by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }

    AlertDialog(
        onDismissRequest = onDismiss,
        title = { Text(stringResource(R.string.address_add_title)) },
        text = {
            Column {
                AppTextField(
                    label = stringResource(R.string.address_name_label),
                    value = label,
                    onValueChange = { label = it },
                    placeholder = stringResource(R.string.address_name_hint),
                    capitalization = KeyboardCapitalization.Words
                )
                AppTextField(
                    label = stringResource(R.string.address_text_label),
                    value = text,
                    onValueChange = { text = it },
                    placeholder = stringResource(R.string.address_text_hint),
                    capitalization = KeyboardCapitalization.Sentences,
                    imeAction = ImeAction.Done,
                    modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 12.dp)
                )
                if (showError) {
                    Text(
                        text = stringResource(R.string.address_required),
                        color = Danger,
                        fontSize = 12.sp,
                        modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 8.dp)
                    )
                }
            }
        },
        confirmButton = {
            TextButton(
                onClick = {
                    if (label.isBlank() || text.isBlank()) showError = true
                    else onSave(label.trim(), text.trim())
                }
            ) { Text(stringResource(R.string.address_save), color = Primary) }
        },
        dismissButton = {
            TextButton(onClick = onDismiss) { Text(stringResource(R.string.cancel), color = Muted) }
        }
    )
}

package co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.ui.checkout

import androidx.compose.foundation.*
import androidx.compose.foundation.interaction.*
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.*
import androidx.compose.foundation.lazy.*

import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.ui.main.CartViewModel
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.ui.main.CheckoutViewModel
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.ui.main.RestaurantsViewModel
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.ui.main.SessionViewModel

/** Paso 2 del pedido: resumen, mÃ©todo de pago y confirmaciÃ³n (se crea el pedido en Firestore). */
@Composable
fun PaymentScreen(
    cartViewModel: CartViewModel,
    checkoutViewModel: CheckoutViewModel,
    restaurantsViewModel: RestaurantsViewModel,
    sessionViewModel: SessionViewModel,
    onBack: () -> Unit,
    onPlaced: (orderId: String) -> Unit
) {
    StatusBarAppearance(darkIcons = true)
    val context = LocalContext.current
    val cart by cartViewModel.state.collectAsStateWithLifecycle()
    val checkout by checkoutViewModel.state.collectAsStateWithLifecycle()
    val restaurants by restaurantsViewModel.state.collectAsStateWithLifecycle()
    val profile by sessionViewModel.profile.collectAsStateWithLifecycle()
    val currentLocationText = stringResource(R.string.address_current_text)

    // Muestra los errores al crear el pedido una sola vez
    LaunchedEffect(checkout.messageRes) {
        val message = checkout.messageRes
        if (message != null) {
            Toast.makeText(context, message, Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show()
            checkoutViewModel.clearMessage()
        }
    }

    val phone = profile?.phone.orEmpty()

    Column(Modifier.fillMaxSize().background(ScreenBg)) {
        AppTopBar(title = stringResource(R.string.payment_title), onBack = onBack)

        Column(
            modifier = Modifier
                .weight(1f)
                .verticalScroll(rememberScrollState())
                .padding(16.dp)
        ) {
            // ----- Resumen -----
            AppCard(Modifier.fillMaxWidth()) {
                Column(Modifier.padding(16.dp)) {
                    Text(
                        text = stringResource(R.string.payment_summary_title),
                        color = Ink,
                        fontSize = 16.sp,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
                    )
                    cart.restaurant?.let {
                        Text(
                            text = it.emoji + " " + it.name,
                            color = Muted,
                            fontSize = 13.sp,
                            modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 2.dp, bottom = 8.dp)
                        )
                    }
                    cart.lines.forEach { line ->
                        SummaryLine(
                            label = line.quantity.toString() + " Ã— " + line.item.name,
                            value = line.total.toCop()
                        )
                    }
                    HorizontalDivider(color = DividerGray, modifier = Modifier.padding(vertical = 10.dp))
                    SummaryLine(stringResource(R.string.summary_subtotal), cart.subtotal.toCop())
                    SummaryLine(
                        stringResource(R.string.summary_delivery),
                        if (cart.deliveryFee == 0) stringResource(R.string.delivery_free) else cart.deliveryFee.toCop()
                    )
                    SummaryLine(stringResource(R.string.summary_service), cart.serviceFee.toCop())
                    HorizontalDivider(color = DividerGray, modifier = Modifier.padding(vertical = 10.dp))
                    SummaryLine(
                        label = stringResource(R.string.summary_total),
                        value = cart.total.toCop(),
                        emphasized = true
                    )
                }
            }

            // ----- MÃ©todos de pago -----
            Text(
                text = stringResource(R.string.payment_choose),
                color = Ink,
                fontSize = 16.sp,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 20.dp, bottom = 8.dp)
            )
            PaymentOption(
                emoji = "ðŸ’³",
                title = stringResource(R.string.pay_card_title),
                subtitle = stringResource(R.string.pay_card_subtitle),
                selected = checkout.payment == PaymentMethod.CARD,
                onClick = { checkoutViewModel.setPayment(PaymentMethod.CARD) }
            )
            PaymentOption(
                emoji = "ðŸ“±",
                title = stringResource(R.string.pay_nequi_title),
                subtitle = stringResource(R.string.pay_nequi_subtitle, phone),
                selected = checkout.payment == PaymentMethod.NEQUI,
                onClick = { checkoutViewModel.setPayment(PaymentMethod.NEQUI) }
            )
            PaymentOption(
                emoji = "ðŸ“²",
                title = stringResource(R.string.pay_daviplata_title),
                subtitle = stringResource(R.string.pay_daviplata_subtitle, phone),
                selected = checkout.payment == PaymentMethod.DAVIPLATA,
                onClick = { checkoutViewModel.setPayment(PaymentMethod.DAVIPLATA) }
            )
            PaymentOption(
                emoji = "ðŸ’µ",
                title = stringResource(R.string.pay_cash_title),
                subtitle = stringResource(R.string.pay_cash_subtitle),
                selected = checkout.payment == PaymentMethod.CASH,
                onClick = { checkoutViewModel.setPayment(PaymentMethod.CASH) }
            )

            Text(
                text = stringResource(R.string.payment_note),
                color = Muted,
                fontSize = 12.sp,
                lineHeight = 17.sp,
                modifier = Modifier
                    .padding(top = 16.dp)
                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(12.dp))
                    .background(Peach)
                    .border(1.dp, PeachStroke, RoundedCornerShape(12.dp))
                    .padding(12.dp)
            )
        }

        BottomActionBar {
            PrimaryButton(
                text = stringResource(R.string.payment_confirm_format, cart.total.toCop()),
                enabled = !cart.isEmpty && profile != null && !checkout.isPlacing,
                onClick = {
                    val customer = profile
                    if (customer != null) {
                        checkoutViewModel.placeOrder(
                            customer = customer,
                            cart = cart,
                            device = restaurants.location,
                            currentLocationText = currentLocationText,
                            onPlaced = onPlaced
                        )
                    }
                }
            )
            if (checkout.isPlacing) {
                Box(Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(top = 12.dp), contentAlignment = Alignment.Center) {
                    CircularProgressIndicator(color = Primary, modifier = Modifier.size(24.dp), strokeWidth = 2.dp)
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun SummaryLine(label: String, value: String, emphasized: Boolean = false) {
    Row(Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(vertical = 3.dp)) {
        Text(
            text = label,
            color = if (emphasized) Ink else Muted,
            fontSize = if (emphasized) 16.sp else 14.sp,
            fontWeight = if (emphasized) FontWeight.Bold else FontWeight.Normal,
            modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)
        )
        Text(
            text = value,
            color = if (emphasized) Primary else Ink,
            fontSize = if (emphasized) 18.sp else 14.sp,
            fontWeight = if (emphasized) FontWeight.Bold else FontWeight.Normal
        )
    }
}

@Composable
private fun PaymentOption(
    emoji: String,
    title: String,
    subtitle: String,
    selected: Boolean,
    onClick: () -> Unit
) {
    Row(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .padding(bottom = 8.dp)
            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(14.dp))
            .background(if (selected) TileOrange else Color.White)
            .border(1.dp, if (selected) Primary else OutlineGray, RoundedCornerShape(14.dp))
            .clickable(onClick = onClick)
            .padding(12.dp),
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
    ) {
        EmojiTile(emoji = emoji, background = if (selected) Color.White else TileGray, size = 44.dp)
        Column(Modifier.weight(1f).padding(horizontal = 12.dp)) {
            Text(title, color = Ink, fontSize = 15.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
            Text(subtitle, color = Muted, fontSize = 12.sp, modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 2.dp))
        }
        RadioButton(
            selected = selected,
            onClick = null,
            colors = RadioButtonDefaults.colors(selectedColor = Primary, unselectedColor = OutlineGray)
        )
    }
}

===== C:\Users\vgzar\Documents\UdeA\COMPUTACION MOVIL\PidePues\app\src\main\java\co\edu\udea\compumovil\labs20262_gr08\pidepues\ui\common\AuthErrors.kt =====
package co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.ui.common

import androidx.annotation.StringRes
import com.google.firebase.FirebaseNetworkException
import com.google.firebase.FirebaseTooManyRequestsException
import com.google.firebase.auth.FirebaseAuthInvalidCredentialsException
import com.google.firebase.auth.FirebaseAuthInvalidUserException
import com.google.firebase.auth.FirebaseAuthUserCollisionException
import com.google.firebase.auth.FirebaseAuthWeakPasswordException
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.R

/** Traduce los errores de Firebase a mensajes claros para el usuario. */
@StringRes
fun Throwable.authMessageRes(): Int = when (this) {
    is FirebaseAuthUserCollisionException -> R.string.auth_error_email_in_use
    // WeakPassword hereda de InvalidCredentials: debe ir primero
    is FirebaseAuthWeakPasswordException -> R.string.auth_error_weak_password
    is FirebaseAuthInvalidUserException,
    is FirebaseAuthInvalidCredentialsException -> R.string.auth_error_credentials
    is FirebaseTooManyRequestsException -> R.string.auth_error_too_many
    is FirebaseNetworkException -> R.string.auth_error_network
    else -> R.string.auth_error_generic
}

package co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.ui.common

import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.R

private val FOOD_COLORS = intArrayOf(
    R.color.food_1, R.color.food_2, R.color.food_3,
    R.color.food_4, R.color.food_5, R.color.food_6
)

/** Color pastel de la "foto" de un restaurante (hasta que haya imÃ¡genes reales). */
fun foodColorRes(restaurantId: Int): Int = FOOD_COLORS[restaurantId % FOOD_COLORS.size]

import androidx.compose.foundation.*

import androidx.compose.ui.unit.*
import androidx.annotation.DrawableRes
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.ui.theme.*

/** BotÃ³n naranja principal: se oscurece al presionar, pasar el mouse o enfocar; salmÃ³n si estÃ¡ deshabilitado. */
@Composable
fun PrimaryButton(
    text: String,
    onClick: () -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    enabled: Boolean = true,
    fillWidth: Boolean = true
) {
    val interaction = remember { MutableInteractionSource() }
    val pressed by interaction.collectIsPressedAsState()
    val hovered by interaction.collectIsHoveredAsState()
    val focused by interaction.collectIsFocusedAsState()
    val container = when {
        !enabled -> PrimaryDisabled
        pressed || hovered || focused -> PrimaryDark
        else -> Primary
    }
    Button(
        onClick = onClick,
        modifier = (if (fillWidth) modifier.fillMaxWidth() else modifier).height(56.dp),
        enabled = enabled,
        shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp),
        colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(
            containerColor = container,
            contentColor = Color.White,
            disabledContainerColor = PrimaryDisabled,
            disabledContentColor = Color.White
        ),
        interactionSource = interaction
    ) {
        Text(text, fontSize = 16.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
    }
}

/** BotÃ³n blanco con borde (ej. "Continuar con Google"). */
@Composable
fun OutlinedActionButton(
    text: String,
    onClick: () -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    leadingIcon: (@Composable () -> Unit)? = null
) {
    OutlinedButton(
        onClick = onClick,
        modifier = modifier.fillMaxWidth().height(56.dp),
        shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp),
        border = BorderStroke(1.dp, OutlineGray),
        colors = ButtonDefaults.outlinedButtonColors(containerColor = Color.White, contentColor = Ink)
    ) {
        if (leadingIcon != null) {
            leadingIcon()
            Spacer(Modifier.width(12.dp))
        }
        Text(text, fontSize = 15.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium)
    }
}

/** Texto naranja en negrita que se puede tocar (enlaces). */
@Composable
fun LinkText(
    text: String,
    onClick: () -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    fontSize: TextUnit = 14.sp,
    color: Color = Primary
) {
    Text(
        text = text,
        color = color,
        fontSize = fontSize,
        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
        modifier = modifier
            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(6.dp))
            .clickable(onClick = onClick)
            .padding(4.dp)
    )
}

/** BotÃ³n circular con un icono (atrÃ¡s, mÃ¡s, menos...). */
@Composable
fun CircleIconButton(
    @DrawableRes icon: Int,
    onClick: () -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    containerColor: Color = CircleGray,
    tint: Color = Ink,
    size: Dp = 40.dp,
    iconPadding: Dp = 10.dp,
    contentDescription: String? = null
) {
    Box(
        modifier = modifier
            .size(size)
            .clip(CircleShape)
            .background(containerColor)
            .clickable(onClick = onClick),
        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
    ) {
        Icon(
            painter = painterResource(icon),
            contentDescription = contentDescription,
            tint = tint,
            modifier = Modifier.size(size - iconPadding * 2)
        )
    }
}

/** Chip de selecciÃ³n: naranja cuando estÃ¡ elegido, blanco con borde cuando no. */
@Composable
fun PidePuesChip(
    text: String,
    selected: Boolean,
    onClick: () -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
) {
    Surface(
        onClick = onClick,
        modifier = modifier,
        shape = RoundedCornerShape(20.dp),
        color = if (selected) Primary else Color.White,
        contentColor = if (selected) Color.White else InkSoft,
        border = BorderStroke(1.dp, if (selected) Primary else OutlineGray)
    ) {
        Text(
            text = text,
            fontSize = 13.sp,
            fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium,
            modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = 14.dp, vertical = 8.dp)
        )
    }
}

/** Tarjeta blanca de esquinas redondeadas con sombra suave. */
@Composable
fun AppCard(
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    onClick: (() -> Unit)? = null,
    cornerRadius: Dp = 20.dp,
    elevation: Dp = 2.dp,
    content: @Composable ColumnScope.() -> Unit
) {
    val shape = RoundedCornerShape(cornerRadius)
    val colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = Color.White)
    val cardElevation = CardDefaults.cardElevation(defaultElevation = elevation)
    if (onClick != null) {
        Card(onClick = onClick, modifier = modifier, shape = shape, colors = colors, elevation = cardElevation, content = content)
    } else {
        Card(modifier = modifier, shape = shape, colors = colors, elevation = cardElevation, content = content)
    }
}

            .size(size)
            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(cornerRadius))

package co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.ui.components

import androidx.compose.foundation.*
import androidx.compose.foundation.interaction.*
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.*

import androidx.compose.ui.text.font.*

import androidx.compose.ui.platform.LocalFocusManager

/**
 * Campo de texto con etiqueta arriba: fondo gris redondeado, borde naranja al enfocar
 * y mensaje de error debajo.
 */
@Composable
fun AppTextField(
    label: String,
    value: String,
    onValueChange: (String) -> Unit,
    placeholder: String,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    error: String? = null,
    keyboardType: KeyboardType = KeyboardType.Text,
    capitalization: KeyboardCapitalization = KeyboardCapitalization.None,
    autoCorrect: Boolean = true,
    isPassword: Boolean = false,
    imeAction: ImeAction = ImeAction.Next,
    onImeAction: (() -> Unit)? = null,
    singleLine: Boolean = true,
    minLines: Int = 1
) {
    val focusManager = LocalFocusManager.current
    Column(modifier) {
        if (label.isNotEmpty()) {
            Text(
                text = label,
                fontSize = 14.sp,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium,
                color = InkSoft,
                modifier = Modifier.padding(bottom = 8.dp)
            )
        }
        OutlinedTextField(
            value = value,
            onValueChange = onValueChange,
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
            singleLine = singleLine,
            minLines = minLines,
            placeholder = { Text(placeholder, color = Hint, fontSize = 15.sp) },
            isError = error != null,
            shape = RoundedCornerShape(14.dp),
            textStyle = TextStyle(fontSize = 15.sp, color = Ink),
            visualTransformation = if (isPassword) PasswordVisualTransformation() else VisualTransformation.None,
            keyboardOptions = KeyboardOptions(
                capitalization = capitalization,
                autoCorrectEnabled = autoCorrect,
                keyboardType = keyboardType,
                imeAction = imeAction
            ),
            keyboardActions = KeyboardActions(
                onDone = {
                    focusManager.clearFocus()
                    onImeAction?.invoke()
                }
            ),
            colors = OutlinedTextFieldDefaults.colors(
                focusedContainerColor = Color.White,
                unfocusedContainerColor = FieldBg,
                errorContainerColor = FieldBg,
                focusedBorderColor = Primary,
                unfocusedBorderColor = FieldStroke,
                errorBorderColor = Danger,
                cursorColor = Primary,
                focusedTextColor = Ink,
                unfocusedTextColor = Ink
            )
        )
        if (error != null) {
            Text(
                text = error,
                color = Danger,
                fontSize = 12.sp,
                modifier = Modifier.padding(start = 4.dp, top = 4.dp)
            )
        }
    }
}

/** Tarjeta de restaurante (Inicio y resultados de Buscar). */
@Composable
fun RestaurantCard(
    item: RestaurantWithDistance,
    onClick: () -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
) {
    val restaurant = item.restaurant
    AppCard(modifier = modifier.fillMaxWidth(), onClick = onClick) {
        // "Foto": color pastel + emoji (se cambia por una imagen real mÃ¡s adelante)
        Box(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .height(168.dp)
                .background(foodColor(restaurant.id))
        ) {
            Text(
                text = restaurant.emoji,
                fontSize = 72.sp,
                modifier = Modifier.align(Alignment.Center)
            )
            if (!restaurant.isOpen) {
                Box(Modifier.matchParentSize().background(Scrim55))
                Text(
                    text = stringResource(R.string.restaurant_closed),
                    color = Color.White,
                    fontSize = 13.sp,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                    modifier = Modifier.align(Alignment.Center)
                )
            }
            Row(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .align(Alignment.TopEnd)
                    .padding(12.dp)
                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(14.dp))
                    .background(White90)
                    .padding(horizontal = 10.dp, vertical = 5.dp),
                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
            ) {
                Icon(
                    painter = painterResource(R.drawable.ic_star),
                    contentDescription = null,
                    tint = Gold,
                    modifier = Modifier.size(14.dp)
                )
                Text(
                    text = String.format(Locale.US, "%.1f", restaurant.rating),
                    color = Ink,
                    fontSize = 13.sp,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                    modifier = Modifier.padding(start = 4.dp)
                )
            }
            Text(
                text = restaurant.dishTag,
                color = Ink,
                fontSize = 12.sp,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                modifier = Modifier
                    .align(Alignment.BottomStart)
                    .padding(12.dp)
                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(12.dp))
                    .background(White90)
                    .padding(horizontal = 10.dp, vertical = 5.dp)
            )
        }
        Column(Modifier.padding(16.dp)) {
            Text(restaurant.name, color = Ink, fontSize = 17.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
            Text(
                text = restaurant.cuisine,
                color = Muted,
                fontSize = 13.sp,
                modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 2.dp)
            )
            Row(
                modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 10.dp),
                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
            ) {
                InfoItem(R.drawable.ic_schedule, stringResource(R.string.delivery_minutes, restaurant.deliveryMinutes))
                Spacer(Modifier.width(14.dp))
                InfoItem(
                    R.drawable.ic_location,
                    stringResource(R.string.delivery_distance, String.format(Locale.US, "%.1f", item.distanceKm))
                )
                Spacer(Modifier.width(14.dp))
                Text(
                    text = if (restaurant.deliveryFee == 0) {
                        stringResource(R.string.delivery_free)
                    } else {
                        stringResource(R.string.delivery_fee, restaurant.deliveryFee.toCopNumber())
                    },
                    color = Muted,
                    fontSize = 12.sp
                )
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun InfoItem(icon: Int, text: String) {
    Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
        Icon(
            painter = painterResource(icon),
            contentDescription = null,
            tint = Muted,
            modifier = Modifier.size(14.dp)
        )
        Text(text = text, color = Muted, fontSize = 12.sp, modifier = Modifier.padding(start = 4.dp))
    }
}

package co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.ui.components

import android.app.Activity
import android.content.Context
import android.content.ContextWrapper
import androidx.compose.runtime.Composable
import androidx.compose.runtime.SideEffect
import androidx.compose.ui.platform.LocalView
import androidx.core.view.WindowCompat

private tailrec fun Context.findActivity(): Activity? = when (this) {
    is Activity -> this
    is ContextWrapper -> baseContext.findActivity()
    else -> null
}

/**
 * Color de los iconos de la barra de estado: [darkIcons] = true sobre fondos claros,
 * false sobre fondos oscuros o de color (ej. la cabecera naranja).
 */
@Composable
fun StatusBarAppearance(darkIcons: Boolean) {
    val view = LocalView.current
    if (!view.isInEditMode) {
        SideEffect {
            val window = view.context.findActivity()?.window ?: return@SideEffect
            WindowCompat.getInsetsController(window, view).isAppearanceLightStatusBars = darkIcons
        }
    }
}

/** Barra superior blanca con flecha atrÃ¡s, tÃ­tulo centrado y subtÃ­tulo opcional. */
@Composable
fun AppTopBar(
    title: String,
    onBack: () -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    subtitle: String? = null
) {
    Box(
        modifier = modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .shadow(2.dp)
            .background(Color.White)
            .statusBarsPadding()
            .padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 12.dp)
    ) {
        CircleIconButton(
            icon = R.drawable.ic_arrow_back,
            onClick = onBack,
            modifier = Modifier.align(Alignment.CenterStart),
            contentDescription = stringResource(R.string.cd_back)
        )
        Column(
            modifier = Modifier.align(Alignment.Center),
            horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally
        ) {
            Text(text = title, color = Ink, fontSize = 18.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
            if (subtitle != null) {
                Text(text = subtitle, color = Muted, fontSize = 12.sp)
            }
        }
    }
}

/** Mapa decorativo (calles dibujadas) sobre el que se colocan pines y etiquetas. */
@Composable
fun MapCard(
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    height: Dp = 180.dp,
    content: @Composable BoxScope.() -> Unit = {}
) {
    Box(
        modifier = modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .height(height)
            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(20.dp))
            .background(MapPeach)
    ) {
        Canvas(Modifier.matchParentSize()) {
            val w = size.width
            val h = size.height
            val road = 10.dp.toPx()
            drawLine(Color.White, Offset(0f, h * 0.35f), Offset(w, h * 0.55f), strokeWidth = road)
            drawLine(Color.White, Offset(w * 0.30f, 0f), Offset(w * 0.45f, h), strokeWidth = road)
            drawLine(Color.White, Offset(w * 0.75f, 0f), Offset(w * 0.65f, h), strokeWidth = road * 0.7f)
            drawLine(MapRing, Offset(0f, h * 0.82f), Offset(w, h * 0.70f), strokeWidth = road * 0.6f)
        }
        content()
    }
}

    }
}

import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.data.RestaurantRepository
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.domain.AddResult
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.model.MenuItem
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.ui.main.CartViewModel
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.ui.main.RestaurantsViewModel
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.util.toCop
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.util.toCopNumber
import java.util.Locale

/** Detalle de un restaurante: datos, menÃº (Firestore) y agregar platos al carrito. */
@Composable
fun RestaurantDetailScreen(
    restaurantId: Int,
    restaurantsViewModel: RestaurantsViewModel,
    cartViewModel: CartViewModel,
    onBack: () -> Unit,
    onViewCart: () -> Unit
) {
    StatusBarAppearance(darkIcons = true)
    // Se lee el estado para recomponer si cambian los restaurantes o la ubicaciÃ³n
    restaurantsViewModel.state.collectAsStateWithLifecycle()
    val cart by cartViewModel.state.collectAsStateWithLifecycle()
    val found = restaurantsViewModel.findById(restaurantId)

    if (found == null) {
        LaunchedEffect(Unit) { onBack() }
        return
    }
    val restaurant = found.restaurant

    var menu by remember(restaurantId) { mutableStateOf<List<MenuItem>>(emptyList()) }
    var isLoading by remember(restaurantId) { mutableStateOf(true) }
    var loadFailed by remember(restaurantId) { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var allProductsUnavailable by remember(restaurantId) { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var pendingReplace by remember { mutableStateOf<MenuItem?>(null) }

    DisposableEffect(restaurantId) {
        isLoading = true
        val registration = RestaurantRepository.listenMenu(restaurantId.toString()) { result ->
            val items = result.getOrNull()
            if (items != null) {
                menu = items.filter { it.isAvailable }
                allProductsUnavailable = items.isNotEmpty() && menu.isEmpty()
                loadFailed = false
            } else {
                loadFailed = true
            }
            isLoading = false
        }
        onDispose { registration.remove() }
    }

    Box(Modifier.fillMaxSize().background(ScreenBg)) {
        LazyColumn(
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
            contentPadding = PaddingValues(bottom = 112.dp)
        ) {
            // "Foto" + tarjeta con los datos que sube sobre ella
            item {
                Box(Modifier.fillMaxWidth()) {
                    Box(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .height(220.dp)
                            .background(foodColor(restaurant.id)),
                        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                    ) {
                        Text(text = restaurant.emoji, fontSize = 96.sp)
                    }
                    Column(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .padding(top = 196.dp)
                            .background(ScreenBg, RoundedCornerShape(topStart = 24.dp, topEnd = 24.dp))
                            .padding(start = 20.dp, top = 20.dp, end = 20.dp, bottom = 8.dp)
                    ) {
                        Text(restaurant.name, color = Ink, fontSize = 24.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                        Text(
                            text = restaurant.cuisine,
                            color = Muted,
                            fontSize = 14.sp,
                            modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 2.dp)
                        )
                        Row(
                            modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 12.dp),
                            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                        ) {
                            Icon(
                                painter = painterResource(R.drawable.ic_star),
                                contentDescription = null,
                                tint = Gold,
                                modifier = Modifier.size(16.dp)
                            )
                            Text(
                                text = String.format(Locale.US, "%.1f", restaurant.rating),
                                color = Ink,
                                fontSize = 13.sp,
                                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                                modifier = Modifier.padding(start = 4.dp)
                            )
                            Icon(
                                painter = painterResource(R.drawable.ic_schedule),
                                contentDescription = null,
                                tint = Muted,
                                modifier = Modifier.padding(start = 16.dp).size(14.dp)
                            )
                            Text(
                                text = stringResource(R.string.delivery_minutes, restaurant.deliveryMinutes),
                                color = Muted,
                                fontSize = 13.sp,
                                modifier = Modifier.padding(start = 4.dp)
                            )
                            Icon(
                                painter = painterResource(R.drawable.ic_location),
                                contentDescription = null,
                                tint = Muted,
                                modifier = Modifier.padding(start = 16.dp).size(14.dp)
                            )
                            Text(
                                text = stringResource(
                                    R.string.delivery_distance,
                                    String.format(Locale.US, "%.1f", found.distanceKm)
                                ),
                                color = Muted,
                                fontSize = 13.sp,
                                modifier = Modifier.padding(start = 4.dp)
                            )
                        }
                        Text(
                            text = if (restaurant.deliveryFee == 0) {
                                stringResource(R.string.delivery_free)
                            } else {
                                stringResource(R.string.delivery_fee, restaurant.deliveryFee.toCopNumber())
                            },
                            color = Muted,
                            fontSize = 13.sp,
                            modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 6.dp)
                        )
                        if (!restaurant.isOpen) {
                            Text(
                                text = stringResource(R.string.restaurant_closed),
                                color = Danger,
                                fontSize = 12.sp,
                                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .padding(top = 12.dp)
                                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(20.dp))
                                    .background(Color.White)
                                    .border(1.dp, OutlineGray, RoundedCornerShape(20.dp))
                                    .padding(horizontal = 12.dp, vertical = 6.dp)
                            )
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            item {
                Text(
                    text = stringResource(R.string.menu_title),
                    color = Ink,
                    fontSize = 18.sp,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                    modifier = Modifier.padding(start = 16.dp, top = 8.dp, bottom = 12.dp)
                )
            }

            if (isLoading) {
                item {
                    Box(Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(16.dp), contentAlignment = Alignment.Center) {
                        CircularProgressIndicator(color = Primary)
                    }
                }
            } else if (loadFailed || menu.isEmpty()) {
                item {
                    Text(
                        text = stringResource(
                            when {
                                loadFailed -> R.string.error_loading_menu
                                allProductsUnavailable -> R.string.menu_all_products_unavailable
                                else -> R.string.menu_empty
                            }
                        ),
                        color = Muted,
                        fontSize = 14.sp,
                        textAlign = TextAlign.Center,
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(16.dp)
                    )
                }
            } else {
                items(menu, key = { it.id }) { dish ->
                    MenuRow(
                        dish = dish,
                        quantity = cart.quantityOf(dish.id),
                        onAdd = {
                            if (cartViewModel.add(restaurant, dish) == AddResult.DIFFERENT_RESTAURANT) {
                                pendingReplace = dish
                            }
                        },
                        onPlus = { cartViewModel.increment(dish.id) },
                        onMinus = { cartViewModel.decrement(dish.id) },
                        modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 5.dp)
                    )
                }
            }
        }

        // BotÃ³n atrÃ¡s fijo arriba
        CircleIconButton(
            icon = R.drawable.ic_arrow_back,
            onClick = onBack,
            containerColor = Color.White,
            modifier = Modifier.statusBarsPadding().padding(16.dp),
            contentDescription = stringResource(R.string.cd_back)
        )

        // Barra inferior: ver carrito (solo si hay platos)
        if (!cart.isEmpty) {
            Column(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .align(Alignment.BottomCenter)
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .shadow(8.dp)
                    .background(Color.White)
                    .navigationBarsPadding()
                    .padding(16.dp)
            ) {
                PrimaryButton(
                    text = stringResource(R.string.view_cart_format, cart.itemCount, cart.total.toCop()),
                    onClick = onViewCart
                )
            }
        }
    }

    // El carrito es de otro restaurante: Â¿empezar uno nuevo?
    val replacing = pendingReplace
    if (replacing != null) {
        AlertDialog(
            onDismissRequest = { pendingReplace = null },
            title = { Text(stringResource(R.string.cart_replace_title)) },
            text = {
                Text(
                    stringResource(
                        R.string.cart_replace_message,
                        cart.restaurant?.name.orEmpty(),
                        restaurant.name
                    )
                )
            },
            confirmButton = {
                TextButton(
                    onClick = {
                        cartViewModel.replaceWith(restaurant, replacing)
                        pendingReplace = null
                    }
                ) { Text(stringResource(R.string.cart_replace_confirm), color = Primary) }
            },
            dismissButton = {
                TextButton(onClick = { pendingReplace = null }) {
                    Text(stringResource(R.string.cancel), color = Muted)
                }
            }
        )
    }
}

@Composable
private fun MenuRow(
    dish: MenuItem,
    quantity: Int,
    onAdd: () -> Unit,
    onPlus: () -> Unit,
    onMinus: () -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
) {
    AppCard(modifier = modifier.fillMaxWidth(), cornerRadius = 16.dp, elevation = 1.dp) {
        Row(
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(12.dp),
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
        ) {
            EmojiTile(emoji = dish.emoji, background = TileOrange, size = 56.dp, fontSize = 28.sp)
            Column(Modifier.weight(1f).padding(horizontal = 12.dp)) {
                Text(dish.name, color = Ink, fontSize = 15.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                Text(
                    text = dish.description,
                    color = Muted,
                    fontSize = 12.sp,
                    maxLines = 2,
                    overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
                    modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 2.dp)
                )
                Text(
                    text = dish.price.toCop(),
                    color = Primary,
                    fontSize = 14.sp,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                    modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 4.dp)
                )
            }
            // Cantidad 0: botÃ³n "+". Cantidad > 0: selector  âˆ’  1  +
            if (quantity == 0) {
                CircleIconButton(
                    icon = R.drawable.ic_add,
                    onClick = onAdd,
                    containerColor = Primary,
                    tint = Color.White,
                    size = 36.dp,
                    iconPadding = 8.dp,
                    contentDescription = stringResource(R.string.cd_add)
                )
            } else {
                QuantityStepper(quantity = quantity, onMinus = onMinus, onPlus = onPlus)
            }
        }
    }
}

===== C:\Users\vgzar\Documents\UdeA\COMPUTACION MOVIL\PidePues\app\src\main\java\co\edu\udea\compumovil\labs20262_gr08\pidepues\ui\filters\FiltersScreen.kt =====
package co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.ui.filters

import androidx.compose.foundation.*
import androidx.compose.foundation.interaction.*
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.*
import androidx.compose.foundation.lazy.*
import androidx.compose.foundation.shape.*

/**
 * Pantalla de filtros. Trabaja sobre un borrador: los cambios solo se aplican
 * al presionar "Aplicar filtros"; la flecha atrÃ¡s los descarta.
 */
@OptIn(ExperimentalLayoutApi::class)
@Composable
fun FiltersScreen(
    restaurantsViewModel: RestaurantsViewModel,
    onClose: () -> Unit
) {
    StatusBarAppearance(darkIcons = true)
    val state by restaurantsViewModel.state.collectAsStateWithLifecycle()
    var draft by remember { mutableStateOf(state.filters) }

    Column(Modifier.fillMaxSize().background(ScreenBg)) {
        // Barra superior: atrÃ¡s | Filtros | Limpiar
        Box(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .shadow(2.dp)
                .background(Color.White)
                .statusBarsPadding()
                .padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 12.dp)
        ) {
            CircleIconButton(
                icon = R.drawable.ic_arrow_back,
                onClick = onClose,
                modifier = Modifier.align(Alignment.CenterStart),
                contentDescription = stringResource(R.string.cd_back)
            )
            Text(
                text = stringResource(R.string.filters_title),
                color = Ink,
                fontSize = 18.sp,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                modifier = Modifier.align(Alignment.Center)
            )
            LinkText(
                text = stringResource(R.string.filters_clear),
                onClick = { draft = RestaurantFilters() },
                modifier = Modifier.align(Alignment.CenterEnd)
            )
        }

        Column(
            modifier = Modifier
                .weight(1f)
                .verticalScroll(rememberScrollState())
                .padding(16.dp),
            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(16.dp)
        ) {
            // ----- Ordenar por -----
            AppCard(Modifier.fillMaxWidth()) {
                Column(Modifier.padding(16.dp), verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
                    Text(
                        text = stringResource(R.string.filters_sort_title),
                        color = Ink,
                        fontSize = 16.sp,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                        modifier = Modifier.padding(bottom = 4.dp)
                    )
                    SortOption.values().forEach { option ->
                        SortRow(
                            option = option,
                            selected = draft.sort == option,
                            onClick = { draft = draft.copy(sort = option) }
                        )
                    }
                }
            }

            // ----- Distancia mÃ¡xima -----
            AppCard(Modifier.fillMaxWidth()) {
                Column(Modifier.padding(16.dp)) {
                    Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                        Text(
                            text = stringResource(R.string.filters_distance_title),
                            color = Ink,
                            fontSize = 16.sp,
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                            modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)
                        )
                        Text(
                            text = formatKm(draft.maxDistanceKm),
                            color = Primary,
                            fontSize = 15.sp,
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
                        )
                    }
                    Slider(
                        value = draft.maxDistanceKm,
                        onValueChange = { draft = draft.copy(maxDistanceKm = Math.round(it * 2f) / 2f) },
                        valueRange = 0.5f..10f,
                        steps = 18,
                        colors = SliderDefaults.colors(
                            thumbColor = Primary,
                            activeTrackColor = Primary,
                            inactiveTrackColor = OutlineGray
                        )
                    )
                    Row(Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(horizontal = 8.dp)) {
                        Text(
                            text = stringResource(R.string.filters_distance_min),
                            color = Hint,
                            fontSize = 12.sp,
                            modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)
                        )
                        Text(
                            text = stringResource(R.string.filters_distance_max),
                            color = Hint,
                            fontSize = 12.sp
                        )
                    }
                }
            }

            // ----- CalificaciÃ³n mÃ­nima -----
            AppCard(Modifier.fillMaxWidth()) {
                Column(Modifier.padding(16.dp)) {
                    Text(
                        text = stringResource(R.string.filters_rating_title),
                        color = Ink,
                        fontSize = 16.sp,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
                    )
                    FlowRow(
                        modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 12.dp),
                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp),
                        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)
                    ) {
                        listOf(0f, 3f, 3.5f, 4f, 4.5f).forEach { rating ->
                            PidePuesChip(
                                text = if (rating == 0f) stringResource(R.string.rating_all) else ratingLabel(rating),
                                selected = draft.minRating == rating,
                                onClick = { draft = draft.copy(minRating = rating) }
                            )
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            // ----- Tipo de cocina (selecciÃ³n mÃºltiple) -----
            AppCard(Modifier.fillMaxWidth()) {
                Column(Modifier.padding(16.dp)) {
                    Text(
                        text = stringResource(R.string.filters_cuisine_title),
                        color = Ink,
                        fontSize = 16.sp,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
                    )
                    FlowRow(
                        modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 12.dp),
                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp),
                        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)
                    ) {
                        Category.values().forEach { category ->
                            val selected = category in draft.categories
                            PidePuesChip(
                                text = category.emoji + " " + category.label,
                                selected = selected,
                                onClick = {
                                    val updated = if (selected) draft.categories - category else draft.categories + category
                                    draft = draft.copy(categories = updated)
                                }
                            )
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        // BotÃ³n fijo abajo
        Column(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .shadow(8.dp)
                .background(Color.White)
                .navigationBarsPadding()
                .padding(16.dp)
        ) {
            PrimaryButton(
                text = stringResource(R.string.filters_apply),
                onClick = {
                    restaurantsViewModel.setFilters(draft)
                    onClose()
                }
            )
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun SortRow(option: SortOption, selected: Boolean, onClick: () -> Unit) {
    Row(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(14.dp))
            .background(if (selected) TileOrange else FieldBg)
            .clickable(onClick = onClick)
            .padding(start = 14.dp, top = 12.dp, end = 12.dp, bottom = 12.dp),
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
    ) {
        Text(text = option.emoji, fontSize = 18.sp, modifier = Modifier.width(32.dp))
        Column(Modifier.weight(1f)) {
            Text(
                text = stringResource(option.titleRes),
                color = Ink,
                fontSize = 15.sp,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
            )
            Text(
                text = stringResource(option.subtitleRes),
                color = Muted,
                fontSize = 12.sp,
                modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 2.dp)
            )
        }
        RadioButton(
            selected = selected,
            onClick = null,
            colors = RadioButtonDefaults.colors(selectedColor = Primary, unselectedColor = OutlineGray)
        )
    }
}

private fun formatKm(value: Float): String = when {
    value < 1f -> (value * 1000).toInt().toString() + " m"
    value % 1f == 0f -> value.toInt().toString() + " km"
    else -> value.toString() + " km"
}

private fun ratingLabel(value: Float): String =
    (if (value % 1f == 0f) value.toInt().toString() else value.toString()) + "+"

package co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.ui.home

import androidx.compose.foundation.*
import androidx.compose.foundation.interaction.*
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.*
import androidx.compose.foundation.lazy.*
import androidx.compose.foundation.shape.*
import androidx.compose.foundation.text.*
import androidx.compose.material3.*
import androidx.compose.runtime.*
import androidx.compose.runtime.saveable.*
import androidx.compose.ui.*
import androidx.compose.ui.draw.*
import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.*
import androidx.compose.ui.res.*
import androidx.compose.ui.text.*
import androidx.compose.ui.text.font.*
import androidx.compose.ui.text.input.*
import androidx.compose.ui.text.style.*
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.*
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.R
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.ui.theme.*
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.ui.components.*
import androidx.lifecycle.compose.collectAsStateWithLifecycle
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.model.Order
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.model.SortOption
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.ui.common.emoji
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.ui.common.infoRes
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.ui.common.labelRes
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.ui.common.listTitleRes
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.ui.main.RestaurantsViewModel
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.ui.main.SessionViewModel

@Composable
fun HomeScreen(
    restaurantsViewModel: RestaurantsViewModel,
    sessionViewModel: SessionViewModel,
    onSearchClick: () -> Unit,
    onFiltersClick: () -> Unit,
    onRestaurantClick: (Int) -> Unit,
    activeOrder: Order?,
    onActiveOrderClick: (String) -> Unit
) {
    StatusBarAppearance(darkIcons = false)
    val state by restaurantsViewModel.state.collectAsStateWithLifecycle()
    val profile by sessionViewModel.profile.collectAsStateWithLifecycle()

    val greeting = profile?.let { stringResource(R.string.home_greeting_name, it.firstName) }
        ?: stringResource(R.string.home_greeting_default)
    val locationLabel = stringResource(
        if (state.location.isFromDevice) R.string.location_device_label else R.string.location_demo_label
    )
    val hasItems = state.nearby.isNotEmpty()
    val isRetry = state.loadError && !hasItems

    Column(Modifier.fillMaxSize().background(ScreenBg)) {
        HomeHeader(
            greeting = greeting,
            locationLabel = locationLabel,
            onSearchClick = onSearchClick,
            onFiltersClick = onFiltersClick
        )

        LazyColumn(
            modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
            contentPadding = PaddingValues(16.dp)
        ) {
            if (activeOrder != null) {
                item {
                    ActiveOrderBanner(
                        order = activeOrder,
                        onClick = { onActiveOrderClick(activeOrder.id) },
                        modifier = Modifier.padding(bottom = 16.dp)
                    )
                }
            }
            item { PromoBanner(onApply = { restaurantsViewModel.applyTopRatedPromo() }) }
            item {
                SortChips(
                    selected = state.filters.sort,
                    onSelect = { restaurantsViewModel.setSort(it) }
                )
            }
            item {
                Text(
                    text = stringResource(state.filters.sort.infoRes),
                    color = InkSoft,
                    fontSize = 12.sp,
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .padding(top = 12.dp)
                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(20.dp))
                        .background(Color.White)
                        .border(1.dp, OutlineGray, RoundedCornerShape(20.dp))
                        .padding(horizontal = 12.dp, vertical = 8.dp)
                )
            }
            item {
                Row(
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(top = 20.dp, bottom = 12.dp),
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                ) {
                    Text(
                        text = stringResource(state.filters.sort.listTitleRes),
                        color = Ink,
                        fontSize = 18.sp,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                        modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)
                    )
                    LinkText(text = stringResource(R.string.see_all), onClick = onSearchClick)
                }
            }

            if (state.isLoading && !hasItems) {
                item {
                    Box(Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(32.dp), contentAlignment = Alignment.Center) {
                        CircularProgressIndicator(color = Primary)
                    }
                }
            } else if (!hasItems) {
                item {
                    Column(
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(32.dp),
                        horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally
                    ) {
                        Text(
                            text = stringResource(
                                if (isRetry) R.string.error_loading_restaurants else R.string.empty_results
                            ),
                            color = Muted,
                            fontSize = 14.sp,
                            textAlign = TextAlign.Center
                        )
                        LinkText(
                            text = stringResource(if (isRetry) R.string.retry else R.string.empty_clear),
                            onClick = {
                                if (isRetry) restaurantsViewModel.loadRestaurants()
                                else restaurantsViewModel.clearFilters()
                            },
                            modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 8.dp)
                        )
                    }
                }
            } else {
                items(state.nearby, key = { it.restaurant.id }) { item ->
                    RestaurantCard(
                        item = item,
                        onClick = { onRestaurantClick(item.restaurant.id) },
                        modifier = Modifier.padding(bottom = 16.dp)
                    )
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

        SortOption.RECOMMENDED to R.string.chip_all,
        SortOption.NEAREST to R.string.chip_nearby,

    ) {

            PidePuesChip(
                text = stringResource(label),
                selected = option == selected,
