/**
 * "Â¿DÃ³nde estÃ¡s?": pide el permiso de ubicaciÃ³n. Si ya estaba concedido, avanza solo.
 * [onDone] recibe true si se debe usar la ubicaciÃ³n del dispositivo.
 */
@Composable
fun LocationPermissionScreen(onDone: (useDeviceLocation: Boolean) -> Unit) {
    StatusBarAppearance(darkIcons = true)
    val context = LocalContext.current
    val alreadyGranted = remember { LocationProvider.hasPermission(context) }
    val currentOnDone by rememberUpdatedState(onDone)
    val deniedHint = stringResource(R.string.location_denied_hint)
    val manualHint = stringResource(R.string.location_manual_hint)

    val launcher = rememberLauncherForActivityResult(
        ActivityResultContracts.RequestMultiplePermissions()
    ) { result ->
        val granted = result[Manifest.permission.ACCESS_FINE_LOCATION] == true ||
            result[Manifest.permission.ACCESS_COARSE_LOCATION] == true
        if (granted) {
            currentOnDone(true)
        } else {
            Toast.makeText(context, deniedHint, Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show()
        }
    }

    LaunchedEffect(alreadyGranted) {
        if (alreadyGranted) currentOnDone(true)
    }
    if (alreadyGranted) {
        Box(Modifier.fillMaxSize().background(Color.White))
        return
    }

    // "cercanÃ­a y calificaciÃ³n" resaltado en naranja
    val prefix = stringResource(R.string.location_description_prefix)
    val highlight = stringResource(R.string.location_description_highlight)
    val suffix = stringResource(R.string.location_description_suffix)
    val description = buildAnnotatedString {
        append(prefix)
        withStyle(SpanStyle(color = Primary, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)) { append(highlight) }
        append(suffix)
    }

    Column(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxSize()
            .background(Color.White)
            .statusBarsPadding()
            .navigationBarsPadding()
    ) {
        Column(
            modifier = Modifier
                .weight(1f)
                .verticalScroll(rememberScrollState())
                .padding(horizontal = 24.dp, vertical = 24.dp),
            horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally
        ) {
            MapIllustration(Modifier.padding(top = 16.dp))

            Text(
                text = stringResource(R.string.location_title),
                color = Ink,
                fontSize = 26.sp,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                textAlign = TextAlign.Center,
                modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 32.dp)
            )
            Text(
                text = description,
                color = Muted,
                fontSize = 15.sp,
                lineHeight = 22.sp,
                textAlign = TextAlign.Center,
                modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 12.dp)
            )

            // Aviso de privacidad (pilar de seguridad del proyecto)
            Row(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .padding(top = 24.dp)
                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(16.dp))
                    .background(Peach)
                    .border(1.dp, PeachStroke, RoundedCornerShape(16.dp))
                    .padding(16.dp),
                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
            ) {
                Box(
                    modifier = Modifier.size(40.dp).clip(CircleShape).background(TileOrange),
                    contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                ) {
                    Icon(
                        painter = painterResource(R.drawable.ic_shield),
                        contentDescription = null,
                        tint = Primary,
                        modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp)
                    )
                }
                Column(Modifier.padding(start = 12.dp)) {
                    Text(
                        text = stringResource(R.string.location_privacy_title),
                        color = Ink,
                        fontSize = 14.sp,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
                    )
                    Text(
                        text = stringResource(R.string.location_privacy_subtitle),
                        color = Muted,
                        fontSize = 12.sp,
                        modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 2.dp)
                    )
                }
            }
        }

        Column(
            modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = 24.dp, vertical = 16.dp),
            horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally
        ) {
            PrimaryButton(
                text = stringResource(R.string.location_allow),
                onClick = {
                    launcher.launch(
                        arrayOf(
                            Manifest.permission.ACCESS_FINE_LOCATION,
                            Manifest.permission.ACCESS_COARSE_LOCATION
                        )
                    )
                }
            )
            // Sin permiso se usa la ubicaciÃ³n por defecto (El Poblado). MÃ¡s adelante: buscador de direcciones.
            Text(
                text = stringResource(R.string.location_manual),
                color = Muted,
                fontSize = 14.sp,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                modifier = Modifier
                    .padding(top = 8.dp)
                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(8.dp))
                    .clickable {
                        Toast.makeText(context, manualHint, Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show()
                        currentOnDone(false)
                    }
                    .padding(12.dp)
            )
        }
    }
}

package co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.ui.main

import androidx.annotation.StringRes
import androidx.lifecycle.ViewModel
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.R
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.data.AuthRepository
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.model.UserProfile
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.model.UserRole
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.ui.common.authMessageRes
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow

/** Estado de las pantallas de inicio de sesiÃ³n y registro (cargando / mensaje). */
class AuthViewModel : ViewModel() {

    data class UiState(
        val isLoading: Boolean = false,
        /** Mensaje para mostrar al usuario (error o aviso). */
        @StringRes val messageRes: Int? = null,
        val isError: Boolean = true
    )

    private val _state = MutableStateFlow(UiState())
    val state: StateFlow<UiState> = _state.asStateFlow()

    fun clearMessage() {
        _state.value = _state.value.copy(messageRes = null)
    }

    fun login(email: String, password: String, onSuccess: (UserProfile) -> Unit) {
        _state.value = UiState(isLoading = true)
        AuthRepository.login(email, password) { result ->
            val profile = result.getOrNull()
            if (profile != null) {
                _state.value = UiState()
                onSuccess(profile)
            } else {
                _state.value = UiState(messageRes = result.exceptionOrNull()?.authMessageRes() ?: R.string.auth_error_generic)
            }
        }
    }

    fun register(
        fullName: String,
        email: String,
        phone: String,
        password: String,
        role: UserRole,
        onSuccess: (UserProfile) -> Unit
    ) {
        _state.value = UiState(isLoading = true)
        AuthRepository.register(fullName, email, phone, password, role) { result ->
            val profile = result.getOrNull()
            if (profile != null) {
                _state.value = UiState()
                onSuccess(profile)
            } else {
                _state.value = UiState(messageRes = result.exceptionOrNull()?.authMessageRes() ?: R.string.auth_error_generic)
            }
        }
    }

    fun sendPasswordReset(email: String) {
        AuthRepository.sendPasswordReset(email) { result ->
            _state.value = if (result.isSuccess) {
                UiState(messageRes = R.string.reset_email_sent, isError = false)
            } else {
                UiState(messageRes = result.exceptionOrNull()?.authMessageRes() ?: R.string.auth_error_generic)
            }
        }
    }
}

===== C:\Users\vgzar\Documents\UdeA\COMPUTACION MOVIL\PidePues\app\src\main\java\co\edu\udea\compumovil\labs20262_gr08\pidepues\ui\main\CartViewModel.kt =====
package co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.ui.main

import androidx.lifecycle.ViewModel
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.domain.AddResult
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.domain.Cart
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.model.CartLine
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.model.MenuItem
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.model.Restaurant
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow

/** Carrito compartido por el detalle del restaurante, la pestaÃ±a Carrito y la barra inferior. */
class CartViewModel : ViewModel() {

    data class CartState(
        val restaurant: Restaurant?,
        val lines: List<CartLine>,
        val itemCount: Int,
        val subtotal: Int,
        val deliveryFee: Int,
        val serviceFee: Int,
        val total: Int
    ) {
        val isEmpty: Boolean get() = lines.isEmpty()
        fun quantityOf(itemId: String): Int = lines.firstOrNull { it.item.id == itemId }?.quantity ?: 0
    }

    private val cart = Cart()

    private val _state = MutableStateFlow(snapshot())
    val state: StateFlow<CartState> = _state.asStateFlow()

    private fun snapshot() = CartState(
        cart.restaurant, cart.lines, cart.itemCount, cart.subtotal, cart.deliveryFee, cart.serviceFee, cart.total
    )

    private fun publish() {
        _state.value = snapshot()
    }

    /** Devuelve DIFFERENT_RESTAURANT si el carrito es de otro restaurante (la UI pregunta quÃ© hacer). */
    fun add(restaurant: Restaurant, item: MenuItem): AddResult {
        val result = cart.add(restaurant, item)
        publish()
        return result
    }

    fun replaceWith(restaurant: Restaurant, item: MenuItem) {
        cart.replaceWith(restaurant, item)
        publish()
    }

    fun increment(itemId: String) {
        cart.increment(itemId)
        publish()
    }

    fun decrement(itemId: String) {
        cart.decrement(itemId)
        publish()
    }

    fun clear() {
        cart.clear()
        publish()
    }
}

===== C:\Users\vgzar\Documents\UdeA\COMPUTACION MOVIL\PidePues\app\src\main\java\co\edu\udea\compumovil\labs20262_gr08\pidepues\ui\main\CheckoutViewModel.kt =====
package co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.ui.main

import androidx.annotation.StringRes
import androidx.lifecycle.ViewModel
import com.google.firebase.FirebaseNetworkException
import com.google.firebase.firestore.GeoPoint
import com.google.firebase.firestore.ListenerRegistration
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.R
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.data.AddressRepository
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.data.OrderRepository
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.model.DeliveryAddress
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.model.PaymentMethod
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.model.UserLocation
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.model.UserProfile
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow

/** Estado del flujo de compra: direcciÃ³n â†’ mÃ©todo de pago â†’ crear el pedido. */
class CheckoutViewModel : ViewModel() {

    data class UiState(
        val addresses: List<DeliveryAddress> = emptyList(),
        /** true = "Tu ubicaciÃ³n actual"; false = una direcciÃ³n guardada. */
        val useCurrentLocation: Boolean = true,
        val selectedAddressId: String? = null,
        val instructions: String = "",
        val payment: PaymentMethod = PaymentMethod.CASH,
        val isPlacing: Boolean = false,
        @StringRes val messageRes: Int? = null
    ) {
        val selectedAddress: DeliveryAddress?
            get() = if (useCurrentLocation) null else addresses.firstOrNull { it.id == selectedAddressId }
    }

    private var registration: ListenerRegistration? = null

    private val _state = MutableStateFlow(UiState())
    val state: StateFlow<UiState> = _state.asStateFlow()

    /** Empieza a escuchar las direcciones guardadas del usuario. */
    fun start(uid: String) {
        if (registration != null) return
        registration = AddressRepository.listen(uid) { list ->
            val sorted = list.sortedBy { it.label.lowercase() }
            val current = _state.value
            // Si la direcciÃ³n elegida fue borrada, se vuelve a "ubicaciÃ³n actual"
            val stillThere = sorted.any { it.id == current.selectedAddressId }
            _state.value = current.copy(
                addresses = sorted,
                useCurrentLocation = current.useCurrentLocation || !stillThere,
                selectedAddressId = if (stillThere) current.selectedAddressId else null
            )
        }
    }

    fun stop() {
        registration?.remove()
        registration = null
        _state.value = UiState()
    }

    fun selectCurrentLocation() {
        _state.value = _state.value.copy(useCurrentLocation = true, selectedAddressId = null)
    }

    fun selectSaved(id: String) {
        _state.value = _state.value.copy(useCurrentLocation = false, selectedAddressId = id)
    }

    fun setInstructions(text: String) {
        _state.value = _state.value.copy(instructions = text)
    }

    fun setPayment(method: PaymentMethod) {
        _state.value = _state.value.copy(payment = method)
    }

    fun clearMessage() {
        _state.value = _state.value.copy(messageRes = null)
    }

    /** Guarda una direcciÃ³n nueva y la deja seleccionada. */
    fun addAddress(uid: String, label: String, emoji: String, text: String) {
        AddressRepository.add(uid, label, emoji, text) { result ->
            val id = result.getOrNull()
            if (id != null) selectSaved(id)
            else _state.value = _state.value.copy(messageRes = R.string.address_error)
        }
    }

    /**
     * Crea el pedido con lo que hay en el carrito. [currentLocationText] es el texto de la
     * direcciÃ³n cuando se eligiÃ³ "Tu ubicaciÃ³n actual".
     */
    fun placeOrder(
        customer: UserProfile,
        cart: CartViewModel.CartState,
        device: UserLocation,
        currentLocationText: String,
        onPlaced: (String) -> Unit
    ) {
        val restaurant = cart.restaurant
        val current = _state.value
        if (restaurant == null || cart.isEmpty || current.isPlacing) return

        val selected = current.selectedAddress
        // La ubicaciÃ³n GPS solo se guarda si el cliente eligiÃ³ "Tu ubicaciÃ³n actual"
        val point = if (current.useCurrentLocation && device.isFromDevice) {
            GeoPoint(device.latitude, device.longitude)
        } else {
            null
        }
        _state.value = current.copy(isPlacing = true, messageRes = null)
        OrderRepository.placeOrder(
            customer = customer,
            restaurant = restaurant,
            lines = cart.lines,
            subtotal = cart.subtotal,
            deliveryFee = cart.deliveryFee,
            serviceFee = cart.serviceFee,
            total = cart.total,
            address = selected?.text ?: currentLocationText,
            instructions = current.instructions.trim(),
            payment = current.payment,
            deliveryPoint = point
        ) { result ->
            val orderId = result.getOrNull()
            if (orderId != null) {
                _state.value = _state.value.copy(isPlacing = false, instructions = "")
                onPlaced(orderId)
            } else {
                val offline = result.exceptionOrNull() is FirebaseNetworkException
                _state.value = _state.value.copy(
                    isPlacing = false,
                    messageRes = if (offline) R.string.auth_error_network else R.string.order_error
                )
            }
        }
    }

    override fun onCleared() {
        registration?.remove()
        registration = null
    }
}

package co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.ui.main

/** PestaÃ±as de la barra inferior. */
enum class MainTab(@StringRes val label: Int, @DrawableRes val icon: Int) {
    HOME(R.string.nav_home, R.drawable.ic_home),
    SEARCH(R.string.nav_search, R.drawable.ic_search),
    CART(R.string.nav_cart, R.drawable.ic_shopping_bag),
    PROFILE(R.string.nav_profile, R.drawable.ic_person)
}

/**
 * Contenedor principal: barra inferior con Inicio, Buscar, Carrito (con globo de cantidad) y Perfil.
 * La pestaÃ±a actual vive en [tab] para poder cambiarla desde otras pantallas.
 */
@Composable
fun MainScreen(
    tab: MainTab,
    onTabChange: (MainTab) -> Unit,
    restaurantsViewModel: RestaurantsViewModel,
    cartViewModel: CartViewModel,
    sessionViewModel: SessionViewModel,
    ordersViewModel: OrdersViewModel,
    onOpenFilters: () -> Unit,
    onOpenRestaurant: (Int) -> Unit,
    onCheckout: () -> Unit,
    onOpenOrders: () -> Unit,
    onOpenOrderTracking: (String) -> Unit,
    onLogout: () -> Unit
) {
    // Estando en otra pestaÃ±a, "atrÃ¡s" vuelve a Inicio antes de salir de la app
    BackHandler(enabled = tab != MainTab.HOME) { onTabChange(MainTab.HOME) }
    val cart by cartViewModel.state.collectAsStateWithLifecycle()
    val orders by ordersViewModel.state.collectAsStateWithLifecycle()

    Scaffold(
        containerColor = ScreenBg,
        contentWindowInsets = WindowInsets(0.dp, 0.dp, 0.dp, 0.dp),
        bottomBar = {
            NavigationBar(containerColor = Color.White) {
                MainTab.values().forEach { item ->
                    NavigationBarItem(
                        selected = tab == item,
                        onClick = { onTabChange(item) },
                        icon = {
                            if (item == MainTab.CART && cart.itemCount > 0) {
                                BadgedBox(
                                    badge = {
                                        Badge(containerColor = Primary, contentColor = Color.White) {
                                            Text(cart.itemCount.toString())
                                        }
                                    }
                                ) {
                                    Icon(painterResource(item.icon), contentDescription = null)
                                }
                            } else {
                                Icon(painterResource(item.icon), contentDescription = null)
                            }
                        },
                        label = { Text(stringResource(item.label), fontSize = 11.sp) },
                        colors = NavigationBarItemDefaults.colors(
                            selectedIconColor = Primary,
                            selectedTextColor = Primary,
                            indicatorColor = TileOrange,
                            unselectedIconColor = Hint,
                            unselectedTextColor = Hint
                        )
                    )
                }
            }
        }
    ) { innerPadding ->
        Box(Modifier.padding(innerPadding).fillMaxSize()) {
            when (tab) {
                MainTab.HOME -> HomeScreen(
                    restaurantsViewModel = restaurantsViewModel,
                    sessionViewModel = sessionViewModel,
                    onSearchClick = { onTabChange(MainTab.SEARCH) },
                    onFiltersClick = onOpenFilters,
                    onRestaurantClick = onOpenRestaurant,
                    activeOrder = orders.active.firstOrNull(),
                    onActiveOrderClick = onOpenOrderTracking
                )
                MainTab.SEARCH -> SearchScreen(
                    restaurantsViewModel = restaurantsViewModel,
                    onFiltersClick = onOpenFilters,
                    onRestaurantClick = onOpenRestaurant
                )
                MainTab.CART -> CartScreen(
                    cartViewModel = cartViewModel,
                    onExplore = { onTabChange(MainTab.HOME) },
                    onCheckout = onCheckout
                )
                MainTab.PROFILE -> ProfileScreen(
                    sessionViewModel = sessionViewModel,
                    onOpenOrders = onOpenOrders,
                    onLogout = onLogout
                )
            }
        }
    }
}

===== C:\Users\vgzar\Documents\UdeA\COMPUTACION MOVIL\PidePues\app\src\main\java\co\edu\udea\compumovil\labs20262_gr08\pidepues\ui\main\OrdersViewModel.kt =====
package co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.ui.main

import androidx.lifecycle.ViewModel
import com.google.firebase.firestore.ListenerRegistration
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.data.OrderRepository
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.model.Order
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow

/**
 * Pedidos del cliente en tiempo real: un solo oyente de Firestore alimenta el historial,
 * el seguimiento y el aviso de "pedido en curso" de Inicio.
 */
class OrdersViewModel : ViewModel() {

    data class UiState(
        /** MÃ¡s recientes primero. */
        val orders: List<Order> = emptyList(),
        val isLoading: Boolean = false,
        val loadError: Boolean = false
    ) {
        val active: List<Order> get() = orders.filter { it.status.isActive }
        val history: List<Order> get() = orders.filter { !it.status.isActive }
        fun find(id: String): Order? = orders.firstOrNull { it.id == id }
    }

    private var registration: ListenerRegistration? = null

    private val _state = MutableStateFlow(UiState())
    val state: StateFlow<UiState> = _state.asStateFlow()

    /** Empieza a escuchar los pedidos del usuario (se llama al iniciar sesiÃ³n como cliente). */
    fun start(customerId: String) {
        if (registration != null) return
        _state.value = UiState(isLoading = true)
        registration = OrderRepository.listenOrders(customerId) { result ->
            val list = result.getOrNull()
            _state.value = if (list != null) {
                UiState(orders = list.sortedByDescending { it.createdAtMillis })
            } else {
                UiState(loadError = true)
            }
        }
    }

    /** Deja de escuchar y limpia (se llama al cerrar sesiÃ³n). */
    fun stop() {
        registration?.remove()
        registration = null
        _state.value = UiState()
    }

    /** SOLO PRUEBAS: simula que el restaurante / domiciliario avanzan el estado. */
    fun simulateNextStatus(order: Order, onError: () -> Unit) {
        OrderRepository.simulateNextStatus(order) { result ->
            if (result.isFailure) onError()
        }
    }

    override fun onCleared() {
        registration?.remove()
        registration = null
    }
}

===== C:\Users\vgzar\Documents\UdeA\COMPUTACION MOVIL\PidePues\app\src\main\java\co\edu\udea\compumovil\labs20262_gr08\pidepues\ui\main\RestaurantsViewModel.kt =====
package co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.ui.main

import android.content.Context
import androidx.lifecycle.ViewModel
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.data.FirestoreSeeder
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.data.RestaurantRepository
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.domain.RestaurantRanking
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.location.LocationProvider
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.model.Category
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.model.Restaurant
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.model.RestaurantFilters
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.model.RestaurantWithDistance
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.model.SortOption
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.model.UserLocation
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.util.haversineKm
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow

/**
 * Estado compartido entre Inicio, Buscar, Filtros y el detalle del restaurante.
 * Los restaurantes vienen de Firestore; cada cambio recalcula las listas con [RestaurantRanking].
 */
class RestaurantsViewModel : ViewModel() {

    data class UiState(
        val location: UserLocation,
        val query: String,
        val filters: RestaurantFilters,
        /** Restaurantes que cumplen los filtros (pantalla Inicio). */
        val nearby: List<RestaurantWithDistance>,
        /** Los anteriores, ademÃ¡s del texto buscado (pantalla Buscar). */
        val searchResults: List<RestaurantWithDistance>,
        val recentSearches: List<String>,
        val isLoading: Boolean,
        val loadError: Boolean
    ) {
        /** Buscar muestra resultados si hay texto o algÃºn filtro distinto del predeterminado. */
        val isSearching: Boolean get() = query.isNotBlank() || !filters.isDefault
    }

    private var restaurants: List<Restaurant> = emptyList()
    private var location: UserLocation = LocationProvider.DEMO_CENTER
    private var query = ""
    private var filters = RestaurantFilters()
    private var recents: List<String> = DEFAULT_RECENTS
    private var isLoading = false
    private var loadError = false
    private var seedIfEmpty = false

    private val _state = MutableStateFlow(buildState())
    val state: StateFlow<UiState> = _state.asStateFlow()

    private fun buildState(): UiState {
        val all = RestaurantRanking.withDistance(restaurants, location)
        val nearby = RestaurantRanking.filterAndSort(all, filters)
        return UiState(
            location, query, filters, nearby, RestaurantRanking.search(nearby, query),
            recents, isLoading, loadError
        )
    }

    private fun render() {
        _state.value = buildState()
    }

    // ---------- Datos (Firestore) ----------
    /**
     * Descarga los restaurantes. Con [seed] = true y la colecciÃ³n vacÃ­a, carga primero los
     * datos de ejemplo (solo en depuraciÃ³n).
     */
    fun loadRestaurants(seed: Boolean = seedIfEmpty) {
        seedIfEmpty = seed
        isLoading = true
        loadError = false
        render()
        RestaurantRepository.fetchRestaurants { result ->
            val list = result.getOrNull()
            if (list != null && list.isEmpty() && seedIfEmpty) {
                FirestoreSeeder.seedIfEmpty { seeded ->
                    if (seeded.isSuccess) {
                        RestaurantRepository.fetchRestaurants { applyFetch(it) }
                    } else {
                        applyFetch(Result.failure(seeded.exceptionOrNull() ?: IllegalStateException()))
                    }
                }
            } else {
                applyFetch(result)
            }
        }
    }

    private fun applyFetch(result: Result<List<Restaurant>>) {
        isLoading = false
        val list = result.getOrNull()
        if (list != null) {
            restaurants = list
            loadError = false
        } else {
            loadError = true
        }
        render()
    }

    /** Busca un restaurante por id (sin aplicar filtros) con su distancia al usuario. */
    fun findById(id: Int): RestaurantWithDistance? =
        restaurants.firstOrNull { it.id == id }?.let {
            RestaurantWithDistance(it, haversineKm(location.latitude, location.longitude, it.latitude, it.longitude))
        }

    // ---------- UbicaciÃ³n ----------
    fun loadDeviceLocation(context: Context) {
        LocationProvider.getCurrentLocation(context.applicationContext) { device ->
            if (device != null) {
                location = device
                render()
            }
        }
    }

    // ---------- BÃºsqueda ----------
    fun setQuery(newQuery: String) {
        if (newQuery == query) return
        query = newQuery
        render()
    }

    /** Se llama al presionar "buscar" en el teclado: guarda la bÃºsqueda en recientes. */
    fun submitSearch(text: String) {
        val clean = text.trim()
        if (clean.isEmpty()) return
        query = clean
        recents = pushRecent(clean)
        render()
    }

    fun selectRecent(text: String) {
        query = text
        recents = pushRecent(text)
        render()
    }

    fun clearRecents() {
        recents = emptyList()
        render()
    }

    private fun pushRecent(text: String): List<String> =
        (listOf(text) + recents.filterNot { it.equals(text, ignoreCase = true) }).take(MAX_RECENTS)

    // ---------- Filtros ----------
    fun setSort(sort: SortOption) {
        if (sort == filters.sort) return
        filters = filters.copy(sort = sort)
        render()
    }

    fun setFilters(newFilters: RestaurantFilters) {
        if (newFilters == filters) return
        filters = newFilters
        render()
    }

    fun applyCategory(category: Category) {
        filters = filters.copy(categories = setOf(category))
        render()
    }

    /** BotÃ³n "Aplicar" del banner de envÃ­o gratis: solo restaurantes con 4.5+. */
    fun applyTopRatedPromo() {
        filters = filters.copy(minRating = 4.5f)
        render()
    }

    fun clearFilters() {
        filters = RestaurantFilters()
        render()
    }

    fun clearAll() {
        query = ""
        filters = RestaurantFilters()
        render()
    }

    companion object {
        private const val MAX_RECENTS = 8
        private val DEFAULT_RECENTS = listOf(
            "Bandeja Paisa", "Sushi", "Hamburguesas", "Pizza italiana", "Comida saludable"
        )
    }
}

import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow

/** Perfil del usuario con sesiÃ³n iniciada (se descarga una vez y lo comparten Inicio, Perfil y roles). */
class SessionViewModel : ViewModel() {

    private val _profile = MutableStateFlow<UserProfile?>(null)
    val profile: StateFlow<UserProfile?> = _profile.asStateFlow()

    /** Guarda un perfil ya conocido (por ejemplo, el que devolviÃ³ el inicio de sesiÃ³n). */
    fun set(profile: UserProfile) {
        _profile.value = profile
    }

    fun load() {

            result.getOrNull()?.let { _profile.value = it }

    fun clear() {

/** Rutas de la app (una sola Activity con un NavHost). */
object Routes {
    const val SPLASH = "splash"
    const val LOGIN = "login"
    const val REGISTER = "register"
    const val LOCATION = "location"
    const val MAIN = "main"
    const val FILTERS = "filters"
    const val RESTAURANT = "restaurant/{id}"
    const val ROLE = "role/{role}"

    // Compra y pedidos (semana 8)
    const val ADDRESS = "checkout/address"
    const val PAYMENT = "checkout/payment"
    const val CONFIRMATION = "order/confirmation/{id}"
    const val TRACKING = "order/tracking/{id}"
    const val ORDERS = "orders"

    fun restaurant(id: Int): String = "restaurant/" + id
    fun role(role: UserRole): String = "role/" + role.key
    fun confirmation(orderId: String): String = "order/confirmation/" + orderId
    fun tracking(orderId: String): String = "order/tracking/" + orderId
}

private fun isDebuggable(context: android.content.Context): Boolean =
    (context.applicationInfo.flags and ApplicationInfo.FLAG_DEBUGGABLE) != 0

@Composable
fun AppNavigation() {
    val navController = rememberNavController()
    val context = LocalContext.current
    val isDebug = remember { isDebuggable(context) }

    // ViewModels compartidos por toda la app (Ã¡mbito de la Activity)
    val restaurantsViewModel: RestaurantsViewModel = viewModel()
    val cartViewModel: CartViewModel = viewModel()
    val sessionViewModel: SessionViewModel = viewModel()
    val authViewModel: AuthViewModel = viewModel()
    val ordersViewModel: OrdersViewModel = viewModel()
    val checkoutViewModel: CheckoutViewModel = viewModel()

    var selectedTab by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(MainTab.HOME) }

    /** Navega a [route] y vacÃ­a la pila (no se vuelve atrÃ¡s al login/splash). */
    fun goClearingBackStack(route: String) {
        navController.navigate(route) {
            popUpTo(navController.graph.id) { inclusive = true }
        }
    }

    /** DespuÃ©s de iniciar sesiÃ³n o registrarse: destino segÃºn el rol. */
    fun goAfterAuth(profile: UserProfile) {
        sessionViewModel.set(profile)
        if (profile.role == UserRole.CLIENT) {
            // En depuraciÃ³n, si Firestore estÃ¡ vacÃ­o se cargan los datos de ejemplo
            restaurantsViewModel.loadRestaurants(seed = isDebug)
            // Pedidos (tiempo real) y direcciones guardadas del cliente
            ordersViewModel.start(profile.uid)
            checkoutViewModel.start(profile.uid)
            selectedTab = MainTab.HOME
            goClearingBackStack(Routes.LOCATION)
        } else {
            goClearingBackStack(Routes.role(profile.role))
        }
    }

    fun logout() {
        // Primero se cancelan los oyentes de Firestore, luego se cierra la sesiÃ³n
        ordersViewModel.stop()
        checkoutViewModel.stop()
        AuthRepository.logout()
        cartViewModel.clear()
        sessionViewModel.clear()
        goClearingBackStack(Routes.LOGIN)
    }

    NavHost(navController = navController, startDestination = Routes.SPLASH) {
        composable(Routes.SPLASH) {
            SplashScreen(
                onFinished = { profile ->
                    if (profile != null) goAfterAuth(profile) else goClearingBackStack(Routes.LOGIN)
                }
            )
        }
        composable(Routes.LOGIN) {
            LoginScreen(
                authViewModel = authViewModel,
                onLoggedIn = { goAfterAuth(it) },
                onRegister = { navController.navigate(Routes.REGISTER) }
            )
        }
        composable(Routes.REGISTER) {
            RegisterScreen(
                authViewModel = authViewModel,
                onBack = { navController.popBackStack() },
                onRegistered = { goAfterAuth(it) }
            )
        }
        composable(Routes.LOCATION) {
            LocationPermissionScreen(
                onDone = { useDeviceLocation ->
                    if (useDeviceLocation) restaurantsViewModel.loadDeviceLocation(context)
                    navController.navigate(Routes.MAIN) {
                        popUpTo(Routes.LOCATION) { inclusive = true }
                    }
                }
            )
        }
        composable(Routes.MAIN) {
            MainScreen(
                tab = selectedTab,
                onTabChange = { selectedTab = it },
                restaurantsViewModel = restaurantsViewModel,
                cartViewModel = cartViewModel,
                sessionViewModel = sessionViewModel,
                ordersViewModel = ordersViewModel,
                onOpenFilters = { navController.navigate(Routes.FILTERS) },
                onOpenRestaurant = { id -> navController.navigate(Routes.restaurant(id)) },
                onCheckout = { navController.navigate(Routes.ADDRESS) },
                onOpenOrders = { navController.navigate(Routes.ORDERS) },
                onOpenOrderTracking = { orderId -> navController.navigate(Routes.tracking(orderId)) },
                onLogout = { logout() }
            )
        }
        composable(Routes.FILTERS) {
            FiltersScreen(
                restaurantsViewModel = restaurantsViewModel,
                onClose = { navController.popBackStack() }
            )
        }
        composable(
            route = Routes.RESTAURANT,
            arguments = listOf(navArgument("id") { type = NavType.IntType })
        ) { entry ->
            RestaurantDetailScreen(
                restaurantId = entry.arguments?.getInt("id") ?: 0,
                restaurantsViewModel = restaurantsViewModel,
                cartViewModel = cartViewModel,
                onBack = { navController.popBackStack() },
                onViewCart = {
                    selectedTab = MainTab.CART
                    navController.popBackStack()
                }
            )
        }
        composable(
            route = Routes.ROLE,
            arguments = listOf(navArgument("role") { type = NavType.StringType })
        ) { entry ->
            RoleHomeScreen(
                role = UserRole.fromKey(entry.arguments?.getString("role")),
                sessionViewModel = sessionViewModel,
                onLogout = { logout() }
            )
        }

        // ----- Compra: direcciÃ³n â†’ pago â†’ confirmaciÃ³n â†’ seguimiento -----
        composable(Routes.ADDRESS) {
            DeliveryAddressScreen(
                checkoutViewModel = checkoutViewModel,
                restaurantsViewModel = restaurantsViewModel,
                sessionViewModel = sessionViewModel,
                onBack = { navController.popBackStack() },
                onConfirm = { navController.navigate(Routes.PAYMENT) }
            )
        }
        composable(Routes.PAYMENT) {
            PaymentScreen(
                cartViewModel = cartViewModel,
                checkoutViewModel = checkoutViewModel,
                restaurantsViewModel = restaurantsViewModel,
                sessionViewModel = sessionViewModel,
                onBack = { navController.popBackStack() },
                onPlaced = { orderId ->
                    cartViewModel.clear()
                    // Se quitan direcciÃ³n y pago de la pila: "atrÃ¡s" desde la confirmaciÃ³n vuelve a la app
                    navController.navigate(Routes.confirmation(orderId)) {
                        popUpTo(Routes.MAIN)
                    }
                }
            )
        }
        composable(
            route = Routes.CONFIRMATION,
            arguments = listOf(navArgument("id") { type = NavType.StringType })
        ) { entry ->
            val orderId = entry.arguments?.getString("id").orEmpty()
            OrderConfirmationScreen(
                orderId = orderId,
                ordersViewModel = ordersViewModel,
                onTrack = {
                    navController.navigate(Routes.tracking(orderId)) { popUpTo(Routes.MAIN) }
                },
                onHome = {
                    selectedTab = MainTab.HOME
                    navController.popBackStack(Routes.MAIN, false)
                }
            )
        }
        composable(
            route = Routes.TRACKING,
            arguments = listOf(navArgument("id") { type = NavType.StringType })
        ) { entry ->
            OrderTrackingScreen(
                orderId = entry.arguments?.getString("id").orEmpty(),
                ordersViewModel = ordersViewModel,
                isDebug = isDebug,
                onBack = { navController.popBackStack() }
            )
        }
        composable(Routes.ORDERS) {
            OrdersScreen(
                ordersViewModel = ordersViewModel,
                onBack = { navController.popBackStack() },
                onOpenOrder = { orderId -> navController.navigate(Routes.tracking(orderId)) }
            )
        }
    }
}

            )
        }
    }

package co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.ui.orders

import androidx.compose.foundation.*
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.*
import androidx.compose.foundation.lazy.*

import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.util.formatOrderDate
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.util.toCop

/** "Mis pedidos": pestaÃ±as En curso e Historial. */
@Composable
fun OrdersScreen(
    ordersViewModel: OrdersViewModel,
    onBack: () -> Unit,
    onOpenOrder: (String) -> Unit
) {
    StatusBarAppearance(darkIcons = true)
    val state by ordersViewModel.state.collectAsStateWithLifecycle()
    var showHistory by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(false) }

    val list = if (showHistory) state.history else state.active

    Column(Modifier.fillMaxSize().background(ScreenBg)) {
        AppTopBar(title = stringResource(R.string.orders_title), onBack = onBack)

        Row(
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(start = 16.dp, top = 16.dp, end = 16.dp),
            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)
        ) {
            PidePuesChip(stringResource(R.string.orders_tab_active), !showHistory, { showHistory = false })
            PidePuesChip(stringResource(R.string.orders_tab_history), showHistory, { showHistory = true })
        }

        LazyColumn(
            modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
            contentPadding = PaddingValues(16.dp)
        ) {
            if (state.isLoading && state.orders.isEmpty()) {
                item {
                    Box(Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(32.dp), contentAlignment = Alignment.Center) {
                        CircularProgressIndicator(color = Primary)
                    }
                }
            } else if (state.loadError && state.orders.isEmpty()) {
                item { CenteredMessage(stringResource(R.string.orders_load_error)) }
            } else if (list.isEmpty()) {
                item {
                    CenteredMessage(
                        stringResource(if (showHistory) R.string.orders_empty_history else R.string.orders_empty_active)
                    )
                }
            } else {
                items(list, key = { it.id }) { order ->
                    OrderCard(
                        order = order,
                        onClick = { onOpenOrder(order.id) },
                        modifier = Modifier.padding(bottom = 12.dp)
                    )
                }
                if (!showHistory && list.size == 1) {
                    item {
                        Text(
                            text = stringResource(R.string.orders_footer),
                            color = Muted,
                            fontSize = 12.sp,
                            textAlign = TextAlign.Center,
                            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(top = 4.dp)
                        )
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun CenteredMessage(text: String) {
    Text(
        text = text,
        color = Muted,
        fontSize = 14.sp,
        textAlign = TextAlign.Center,
        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(32.dp)
    )
}

@Composable
private fun OrderCard(order: Order, onClick: () -> Unit, modifier: Modifier = Modifier) {
    val resources = LocalContext.current.resources
    val dishes = resources.getQuantityString(R.plurals.order_dishes, order.itemCount, order.itemCount)
    AppCard(modifier = modifier.fillMaxWidth(), onClick = onClick, cornerRadius = 16.dp) {
        Row(
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(14.dp),
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
        ) {
            EmojiTile(
                emoji = order.restaurantEmoji,
                background = foodColor(order.restaurantId),
                size = 52.dp,
                fontSize = 26.sp,
                cornerRadius = 14.dp
            )
            Column(Modifier.weight(1f).padding(horizontal = 12.dp)) {
                Text(order.restaurantName, color = Ink, fontSize = 15.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                Text(
                    text = order.shortCode + " Â· " + formatOrderDate(order.createdAtMillis),
                    color = Muted,
                    fontSize = 12.sp,
                    modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 2.dp)
                )
                Text(
                    text = dishes + " Â· " + order.total.toCop(),
                    color = InkSoft,
                    fontSize = 13.sp,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium,
                    modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 4.dp)
                )
            }
            StatusChip(order.status)
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun StatusChip(status: OrderStatus) {
    val (background, content) = when (status) {
        OrderStatus.SOLICITADO -> TileBlue to IconBlue
        OrderStatus.TOMADO -> TileAmber to Primary
        OrderStatus.PREPARADO -> Color(0xFFEDE9FE) to Color(0xFF6D28D9)
        OrderStatus.EN_CAMINO -> TileOrange to Primary
        OrderStatus.ENTREGADO -> TileGreen to IconGreen
    }
    Text(
        text = stringResource(status.labelRes),
        color = content,
        fontSize = 11.sp,
        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
        modifier = Modifier
            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(10.dp))
            .background(background)
            .padding(horizontal = 8.dp, vertical = 4.dp)
    )
}

private enum class StepState { DONE, CURRENT, PENDING }

/** Seguimiento en vivo: el estado se actualiza solo cuando cambia el pedido en Firestore. */
@Composable
fun OrderTrackingScreen(
    orderId: String,
    ordersViewModel: OrdersViewModel,
    isDebug: Boolean,
    onBack: () -> Unit
) {
    StatusBarAppearance(darkIcons = true)
    val context = LocalContext.current
    val orders by ordersViewModel.state.collectAsStateWithLifecycle()
    val order = orders.find(orderId)
    val contactSoon = stringResource(R.string.contact_soon)
    val rateSoon = stringResource(R.string.rate_soon)
    val simulateError = stringResource(R.string.simulate_error)

    Column(Modifier.fillMaxSize().background(ScreenBg)) {
        AppTopBar(
            title = stringResource(R.string.tracking_title),
            subtitle = order?.shortCode,
            onBack = onBack
        )

        if (order == null) {
            Box(Modifier.weight(1f).fillMaxWidth(), contentAlignment = Alignment.Center) {
                CircularProgressIndicator(color = Primary)
            }
            return@Column
        }

        Column(
            modifier = Modifier
                .weight(1f)
                .verticalScroll(rememberScrollState())
                .padding(16.dp)
        ) {
            // ----- Mapa con el tiempo restante -----
            MapCard(height = 190.dp) {
                MapPin(Modifier.align(Alignment.Center))
                if (order.status == OrderStatus.EN_CAMINO) {
                    Text(
                        text = "ðŸ›µ",
                        fontSize = 30.sp,
                        modifier = Modifier.align(Alignment.CenterStart).padding(start = 40.dp, top = 40.dp)
                    )
                }
                Text(
                    text = if (order.status.isActive) {
                        stringResource(R.string.tracking_arrives_in, order.remainingMinutes())
                    } else {
                        stringResource(R.string.status_delivered)
                    },
                    color = Color.White,
                    fontSize = 13.sp,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .align(Alignment.BottomCenter)
                        .padding(bottom = 12.dp)
                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(16.dp))
                        .background(Primary)
                        .padding(horizontal = 14.dp, vertical = 6.dp)
                )
            }

            // ----- Estado actual -----
            AppCard(Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(top = 16.dp)) {
                Row(
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(16.dp),
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                ) {
                    EmojiTile(
                        emoji = order.status.emoji,
                        background = TileOrange,
                        size = 52.dp,
                        fontSize = 26.sp,
                        cornerRadius = 16.dp
                    )
                    Column(Modifier.padding(start = 12.dp)) {
                        Text(
                            text = stringResource(order.status.labelRes),
                            color = Ink,
                            fontSize = 17.sp,
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
                        )
                        Text(
                            text = stringResource(subtitleFor(order.status)),
                            color = Muted,
                            fontSize = 13.sp,
                            modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 2.dp)
                        )
                    }
                }
            }

            // ----- Repartidor (cuando ya fue asignado) -----
            val driver = order.driverName
            if (driver != null) {
                AppCard(Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(top = 16.dp)) {
                    Row(
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(16.dp),
                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                    ) {
                        Box(
                            modifier = Modifier.size(48.dp).clip(CircleShape).background(TileOrange),
                            contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                        ) {
                            Text(driver.take(1).uppercase(), color = Primary, fontSize = 20.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                        }
                        Column(Modifier.weight(1f).padding(horizontal = 12.dp)) {
                            Text(driver, color = Ink, fontSize = 15.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                            Text(
                                text = stringResource(
                                    R.string.tracking_driver_subtitle,
                                    String.format(Locale.US, "%.1f", order.driverRating ?: 5.0)
                                ),
                                color = Muted,
                                fontSize = 12.sp,
                                modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 2.dp)
                            )
                        }
                        EmojiTile(
                            emoji = "ðŸ“ž",
                            background = TileGreen,
                            size = 40.dp,
                            fontSize = 18.sp,
                            modifier = Modifier.clickable {
                                Toast.makeText(context, contactSoon, Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                            }
                        )
                        EmojiTile(
                            emoji = "ðŸ’¬",
                            background = TileBlue,
                            size = 40.dp,
                            fontSize = 18.sp,
                            modifier = Modifier.padding(start = 8.dp).clickable {
                                Toast.makeText(context, contactSoon, Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                            }
                        )
                    }
                }
            }

            // ----- Progreso del pedido -----
            AppCard(Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(top = 16.dp)) {
                Column(Modifier.padding(16.dp)) {
                    Text(
                        text = stringResource(R.string.tracking_progress_title),
                        color = Ink,
                        fontSize = 16.sp,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                        modifier = Modifier.padding(bottom = 12.dp)
                    )
                    OrderStatus.entries.forEachIndexed { index, step ->
                        val state = when {
                            order.status == OrderStatus.ENTREGADO || order.status.ordinal > index -> StepState.DONE
                            order.status.ordinal == index -> StepState.CURRENT
                            else -> StepState.PENDING
                        }
                        val driverName = order.driverName
                        val description = if (step == OrderStatus.EN_CAMINO && driverName != null) {
                            stringResource(R.string.step_on_the_way_desc_named, driverName)
                        } else {
                            stringResource(descriptionFor(step))
                        }
                        TimelineStep(
                            title = stringResource(step.labelRes),
                            description = description,
                            time = if (step == OrderStatus.SOLICITADO) formatOrderTime(order.createdAtMillis) else null,
                            state = state,
                            isLast = index == OrderStatus.entries.lastIndex
                        )
                    }
                }
            }

            // ----- Resumen del pedido -----
            AppCard(Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(top = 16.dp)) {
                Column(Modifier.padding(16.dp)) {
                    Text(
                        text = order.restaurantEmoji + " " + order.restaurantName,
                        color = Ink,
                        fontSize = 16.sp,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
                    )
                    Text(
                        text = stringResource(R.string.order_summary_title),
                        color = Muted,
                        fontSize = 12.sp,
                        modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 2.dp, bottom = 8.dp)
                    )
                    order.lines.forEach { line ->
                        Row(Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(vertical = 3.dp)) {
                            Text(
                                text = line.quantity.toString() + " Ã— " + line.name,
                                color = Muted,
                                fontSize = 14.sp,
                                modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)
                            )
                            Text(text = line.total.toCop(), color = Ink, fontSize = 14.sp)
                        }
                    }
                    HorizontalDivider(color = DividerGray, modifier = Modifier.padding(vertical = 10.dp))
                    Row(Modifier.fillMaxWidth()) {
                        Text(
                            text = stringResource(R.string.summary_total),
                            color = Ink,
                            fontSize = 16.sp,
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                            modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)
                        )
                        Text(
                            text = order.total.toCop(),
                            color = Primary,
                            fontSize = 18.sp,
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
                        )
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        // Acciones fijas abajo
        val canSimulate = isDebug && order.status.next() != null
        val delivered = order.status == OrderStatus.ENTREGADO
        if (canSimulate || delivered) {
            BottomActionBar {
                if (delivered) {
                    PrimaryButton(
                        text = stringResource(R.string.btn_rate),
                        onClick = { Toast.makeText(context, rateSoon, Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show() }
                    )
                }
                if (canSimulate) {
                    OutlinedActionButton(
                        text = stringResource(R.string.debug_next_status),
                        onClick = {
                            ordersViewModel.simulateNextStatus(order) {
                                Toast.makeText(context, simulateError, Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                            }
                        }
                    )
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

private fun subtitleFor(status: OrderStatus): Int = when (status) {
    OrderStatus.SOLICITADO -> R.string.tracking_sub_received
    OrderStatus.TOMADO -> R.string.tracking_sub_preparing
    OrderStatus.PREPARADO -> R.string.tracking_sub_prepared
    OrderStatus.EN_CAMINO -> R.string.tracking_sub_on_the_way
    OrderStatus.ENTREGADO -> R.string.tracking_sub_delivered
}

private fun descriptionFor(status: OrderStatus): Int = when (status) {
    OrderStatus.SOLICITADO -> R.string.step_received_desc
    OrderStatus.TOMADO -> R.string.step_preparing_desc
    OrderStatus.PREPARADO -> R.string.step_prepared_desc
    OrderStatus.EN_CAMINO -> R.string.step_on_the_way_desc
    OrderStatus.ENTREGADO -> R.string.step_delivered_desc
}

/** Un paso de la lÃ­nea de tiempo: cÃ­rculo (hecho / actual / pendiente), lÃ­nea y textos. */
@Composable
private fun TimelineStep(
    title: String,
    description: String,
    time: String?,
    state: StepState,
    isLast: Boolean
) {
    Row(Modifier.fillMaxWidth().height(IntrinsicSize.Min)) {
        Column(horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally, modifier = Modifier.width(28.dp)) {
            Box(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .size(24.dp)
                    .clip(CircleShape)
                    .background(
                        when (state) {
                            StepState.DONE -> IconGreen
                            StepState.CURRENT -> TileOrange
                            StepState.PENDING -> DividerGray
                        }
                    )
                    .then(
                        if (state == StepState.CURRENT) Modifier.border(2.dp, Primary, CircleShape) else Modifier
                    ),
                contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
            ) {
                when (state) {
                    StepState.DONE -> Text("âœ“", color = Color.White, fontSize = 13.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                    StepState.CURRENT -> Box(Modifier.size(8.dp).clip(CircleShape).background(Primary))
                    StepState.PENDING -> Unit
                }
            }
            if (!isLast) {
                Box(
                    Modifier
                        .width(2.dp)
                        .weight(1f)
                        .background(if (state == StepState.DONE) IconGreen else OutlineGray)
                )
            }
        }
        Column(Modifier.weight(1f).padding(start = 12.dp, bottom = if (isLast) 0.dp else 18.dp)) {
            Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                Text(
                    text = title,
                    color = if (state == StepState.PENDING) Hint else Ink,
                    fontSize = 15.sp,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                    modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)
                )
                if (time != null) {
                    Text(text = time, color = Muted, fontSize = 12.sp)
                }
            }
            Text(
                text = if (state == StepState.CURRENT) stringResource(R.string.tracking_in_progress) else description,
                color = if (state == StepState.CURRENT) Primary else Muted,
                fontSize = 13.sp,
                modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 2.dp)
            )
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun StatTile(emoji: String, value: String, label: String, modifier: Modifier = Modifier) {
    Column(
        modifier = modifier
            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(16.dp))
            .background(White20)
            .padding(12.dp),
        horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally
    ) {
        Text(text = emoji, fontSize = 20.sp)
        Text(
            text = value,
            color = Color.White,
            fontSize = 18.sp,
            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
            modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 4.dp)
        )
        Text(text = label, color = White80, fontSize = 12.sp)
    }
}

@Composable
private fun OptionsCard(
    options: List<ProfileOption>,
    onOptionClick: ((ProfileOption) -> Unit)? = null
) {
    AppCard(Modifier.fillMaxWidth()) {
        Column {
            options.forEachIndexed { index, option ->
                if (index > 0) HorizontalDivider(color = DividerGray, modifier = Modifier.padding(start = 72.dp))
                Row(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .clickable { onOptionClick?.invoke(option) }
                        .padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 14.dp),
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                ) {
                    IconTile(icon = option.icon, iconTint = option.iconTint, background = option.tile)
                    Column(Modifier.weight(1f).padding(start = 12.dp)) {
                        Text(
                            text = stringResource(option.title),
                            color = Ink,
                            fontSize = 15.sp,
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
                        )
                        Text(
                            text = stringResource(option.subtitle),
                            color = Muted,
                            fontSize = 12.sp,
                            modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 2.dp)
                        )
                    }
                    Icon(
                        painter = painterResource(R.drawable.ic_chevron_right),
                        contentDescription = null,
                        tint = Hint,
                        modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp)
                    )
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

import androidx.compose.foundation.background

@Composable
fun RateOrderScreen(
    restaurantName: String,
    restaurantEmoji: String,
    orderDateLabel: String,
    orderShortCode: String,
    isSubmitting: Boolean,
    onBack: () -> Unit,
    onSubmit: (stars: Int, comment: String) -> Unit
) {
    var selectedStars by remember { mutableIntStateOf(0) }
    var comment by remember { mutableStateOf("") }

    val primaryOrange = Color(0xFFEA4C1D)
    val starYellow = Color(0xFFFFB800)

    Column(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxSize()
            .background(Color(0xFFF8F9FA))
            .padding(16.dp)
    ) {
        // Barra superior
        Row(
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(vertical = 8.dp)
        ) {
            IconButton(
                onClick = onBack,
                modifier = Modifier
                    .size(40.dp)
                    .clip(CircleShape)
                    .background(Color(0xFFEDEDED))
            ) {
                Icon(Icons.AutoMirrored.Filled.ArrowBack, contentDescription = "Volver")
            }
            Spacer(Modifier.width(12.dp))
            Text(
                text = "Calificar pedido",
                fontSize = 20.sp,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                color = Color(0xFF1A1A2E)
            )
        }

        Spacer(Modifier.height(16.dp))

        // Tarjeta resumen del pedido
        Card(
            shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp),
            colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = Color.White),
            elevation = CardDefaults.cardElevation(defaultElevation = 2.dp),
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()
        ) {
            Row(
                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                modifier = Modifier.padding(16.dp)
            ) {
                Box(
                    contentAlignment = Alignment.Center,
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .size(56.dp)
                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(12.dp))
                        .background(Color(0xFFFFF3EE))
                ) {
                    Text(text = restaurantEmoji, fontSize = 28.sp)
                }
                Spacer(Modifier.width(14.dp))
                Column {
                    Text(text = "Tu pedido del $orderDateLabel", fontSize = 12.sp, color = Color.Gray)
                    Text(
                        text = restaurantName,
                        fontSize = 16.sp,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                        color = Color(0xFF1A1A2E)
                    )
                    Text(text = orderShortCode, fontSize = 13.sp, color = Color.Gray)
                }
            }
        }

        Spacer(Modifier.height(16.dp))

        // Tarjeta de estrellas y comentario
        Card(
            shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp),
            colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = Color.White),
            elevation = CardDefaults.cardElevation(defaultElevation = 2.dp),
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()
        ) {
            Column(
                horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
                modifier = Modifier.padding(20.dp).fillMaxWidth()
            ) {
                Text(
                    text = "Â¿CÃ³mo estuvo tu experiencia?",
                    fontSize = 18.sp,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                    color = Color(0xFF1A1A2E)
                )
                Spacer(Modifier.height(6.dp))
                Text(
                    text = "Toca las estrellas para calificar",
                    fontSize = 14.sp,
                    color = Color.Gray
                )
                Spacer(Modifier.height(16.dp))

                // Selector de 5 estrellas
                Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
                    for (i in 1..5) {
                        Icon(
                            imageVector = if (i <= selectedStars) Icons.Filled.Star else Icons.Outlined.StarOutline,
                            contentDescription = "$i estrellas",
                            tint = if (i <= selectedStars) starYellow else Color(0xFFD0D5DD),
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .size(44.dp)
                                .clickable { selectedStars = i }
                        )
                    }
                }

                Spacer(Modifier.height(20.dp))

                // Comentario opcional
                OutlinedTextField(
                    value = comment,
                    onValueChange = { if (it.length <= 280) comment = it },
                    label = { Text("Comentario (opcional)") },
                    placeholder = { Text("CuÃ©ntanos quÃ© te pareciÃ³ la comida y el servicio...") },
                    minLines = 3,
                    maxLines = 4,
                    shape = RoundedCornerShape(12.dp),
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()
                )
            }
        }

        Spacer(Modifier.weight(1f))

        // BotÃ³n enviar
        Button(
            onClick = { onSubmit(selectedStars, comment) },
            enabled = selectedStars > 0 && !isSubmitting,
            colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = primaryOrange),
            shape = RoundedCornerShape(14.dp),
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().height(54.dp)
        ) {
            if (isSubmitting) {
                CircularProgressIndicator(color = Color.White, modifier = Modifier.size(22.dp), strokeWidth = 2.dp)
            } else {
                Text("Enviar calificaciÃ³n", fontSize = 16.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold)
            }
        }
    }
}

package co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.ui.role

import android.content.Intent
import android.net.Uri
import android.widget.Toast
import androidx.activity.compose.BackHandler
import androidx.compose.foundation.BorderStroke
import androidx.compose.foundation.Canvas
import androidx.compose.foundation.background
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.*
import androidx.compose.foundation.rememberScrollState
