/**
 * Pantalla "Detalles del pedido" para el rol Restaurante.
 * Muestra el estado operativo en tiempo real, stepper de 5 pasos, productos solicitados,
 * nota del cliente, desglose de totales, datos del cliente y del repartidor.
 */
@Composable
fun OrderDetailScreen(
    order: RestaurantOrder,
    onBack: () -> Unit,
    onAcceptOrder: () -> Unit,
    onAdvanceToPrepared: () -> Unit,
    onAdvanceToEnCamino: () -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
) {
    StatusBarAppearance(darkIcons = true)
    BackHandler(onBack = onBack)

    Scaffold(
        modifier = modifier.fillMaxSize(),
        containerColor = ScreenBg,
        contentWindowInsets = WindowInsets(0.dp),
        topBar = {
            OrderDetailHeader(order = order, onBack = onBack)
        },
        bottomBar = {
            OrderDetailBottomBar(
                order = order,
                onAcceptOrder = onAcceptOrder,
                onAdvanceToPrepared = onAdvanceToPrepared,
                onAdvanceToEnCamino = onAdvanceToEnCamino
            )
        }
    ) { innerPadding ->
        Column(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxSize()
                .padding(innerPadding)
                .verticalScroll(rememberScrollState())
                .padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 16.dp),
            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(18.dp)
        ) {
            OrderStatusStepper(currentStepIndex = order.stepIndex)
            RequestedProductsSection(order = order)
            CustomerDataSection(order = order)
            CourierSection(order = order)
            Spacer(Modifier.height(8.dp))
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun OrderDetailHeader(
    order: RestaurantOrder,
    onBack: () -> Unit
) {
    Row(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .background(Color.White)
            .statusBarsPadding()
            .padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 14.dp),
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
    ) {
        IconButton(
            onClick = onBack,
            modifier = Modifier
                .size(40.dp)
                .clip(CircleShape)
                .background(CircleGray)
        ) {
            Icon(
                painter = painterResource(R.drawable.ic_arrow_back),
                contentDescription = stringResource(R.string.cd_back),
                tint = Ink,
                modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp)
            )
        }

        Spacer(Modifier.width(12.dp))

        Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
            Text(
                text = stringResource(R.string.orders_number_prefix, order.orderNumber),
                color = Ink,
                fontSize = 19.sp,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
            )
            Spacer(Modifier.height(2.dp))
            Text(
                text = order.formattedDateTime,
                color = Muted,
                fontSize = 13.sp
            )
        }

        val (bgColor, textColor) = when (order.status) {
            OrderStatus.SOLICITADO -> TileOrange to Primary
            OrderStatus.TOMADO -> Color(0xFFFEF3C7) to Color(0xFF92400E)
            OrderStatus.PREPARADO -> Color(0xFFEDE9FE) to Color(0xFF6D28D9)
            OrderStatus.EN_CAMINO -> Color(0xFFDBEAFE) to Color(0xFF1D4ED8)
            OrderStatus.ENTREGADO -> Color(0xFFDCFCE7) to Color(0xFF15803D)
        }

        Surface(
            shape = RoundedCornerShape(50),
            color = bgColor
        ) {
            Text(
                text = stringResource(order.status.labelRes()),
                color = textColor,
                fontSize = 12.sp,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = 12.dp, vertical = 5.dp)
            )
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun OrderStatusStepper(
    currentStepIndex: Int,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
) {
    val steps = listOf(
        1 to R.string.orders_status_solicitado,
        2 to R.string.orders_status_tomado,
        3 to R.string.orders_status_preparado,
        4 to R.string.orders_status_en_camino,
        5 to R.string.orders_status_entregado
    )

    Row(
        modifier = modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.Top
    ) {
        steps.forEachIndexed { index, (stepNum, labelRes) ->
            val isActive = stepNum <= currentStepIndex

            Column(
                modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
                horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally
            ) {
                Row(
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()
                ) {
                    if (index > 0) {
                        HorizontalDivider(
                            modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
                            thickness = 2.dp,
                            color = if (stepNum <= currentStepIndex) Primary else OutlineGray
                        )
                    } else {
                        Spacer(Modifier.weight(1f))
                    }

                    Box(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .size(28.dp)
                            .clip(CircleShape)
                            .background(if (isActive) Primary else CircleGray),
                        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                    ) {
                        Text(
                            text = stepNum.toString(),
                            color = if (isActive) Color.White else Hint,
                            fontSize = 12.sp,
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
                        )
                    }

                    if (index < steps.size - 1) {
                        val nextStepNum = steps[index + 1].first
                        HorizontalDivider(
                            modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
                            thickness = 2.dp,
                            color = if (nextStepNum <= currentStepIndex) Primary else OutlineGray
                        )
                    } else {
                        Spacer(Modifier.weight(1f))
                    }
                }

                Spacer(Modifier.height(6.dp))

                Text(
                    text = stringResource(labelRes),
                    color = if (isActive) Ink else Hint,
                    fontSize = 10.sp,
                    fontWeight = if (isActive) FontWeight.Bold else FontWeight.Normal,
                    textAlign = TextAlign.Center,
                    maxLines = 1,
                    overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis
                )
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun RequestedProductsSection(
    order: RestaurantOrder
) {
    Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
        Text(
            text = stringResource(R.string.order_detail_requested_products),
            color = Ink,
            fontSize = 16.sp,
            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
        )

        Surface(
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
            shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp),
            color = Color.White,
            border = BorderStroke(1.dp, OutlineGray)
        ) {
            Column(modifier = Modifier.padding(16.dp)) {
                // Lista de items
                order.items.forEachIndexed { idx, item ->
                    if (idx > 0) Spacer(Modifier.height(12.dp))
                    Row(
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                    ) {
                        Box(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .size(32.dp)
                                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(8.dp))
                                .background(TileOrange),
                            contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                        ) {
                            Text(
                                text = item.quantity.toString(),
                                color = Primary,
                                fontSize = 14.sp,
                                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
                            )
                        }

                        Spacer(Modifier.width(12.dp))

                        Text(
                            text = item.name,
                            color = Ink,
                            fontSize = 14.sp,
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold,
                            modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
                            maxLines = 2,
                            overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis
                        )

                        Text(
                            text = item.total.toCop(),
                            color = Ink,
                            fontSize = 14.sp,
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
                        )
                    }
                }

                // Nota del cliente condicional
                if (order.customerNote.isNotBlank()) {
                    Spacer(Modifier.height(14.dp))
                    Box(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(12.dp))
                            .background(Color(0xFFFEF3C7))
                            .padding(12.dp)
                    ) {
                        Text(
                            text = stringResource(R.string.order_detail_customer_note_prefix, order.customerNote),
                            color = Color(0xFF92400E),
                            fontSize = 13.sp,
                            lineHeight = 18.sp
                        )
                    }
                }

                Spacer(Modifier.height(14.dp))

                // LÃ­nea divisoria punteada
                Canvas(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .height(1.dp)
                ) {
                    drawLine(
                        color = OutlineGray,
                        start = Offset(0f, 0f),
                        end = Offset(size.width, 0f),
                        pathEffect = PathEffect.dashPathEffect(floatArrayOf(10f, 10f), 0f)
                    )
                }

                Spacer(Modifier.height(14.dp))

                // Filas de resumen de precios
                SummaryRow(
                    label = stringResource(R.string.summary_subtotal),
                    value = order.subtotalCop.toCop(),
                    isTotal = false
                )
                Spacer(Modifier.height(6.dp))
                SummaryRow(
                    label = stringResource(R.string.summary_delivery),
                    value = order.deliveryFeeCop.toCop(),
                    isTotal = false
                )
                Spacer(Modifier.height(8.dp))
                SummaryRow(
                    label = stringResource(R.string.summary_total),
                    value = order.totalCop.toCop(),
                    isTotal = true
                )

                Spacer(Modifier.height(12.dp))

                // MÃ©todo de pago
                val paymentText = order.paymentMethod.ifBlank { stringResource(R.string.order_detail_payment_not_specified) }
                Text(
                    text = stringResource(R.string.order_detail_payment_prefix, paymentText),
                    color = Hint,
                    fontSize = 12.sp
                )
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun SummaryRow(
    label: String,
    value: String,
    isTotal: Boolean
) {
    Row(
        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
    ) {
        Text(
            text = label,
            color = if (isTotal) Ink else Muted,
            fontSize = if (isTotal) 16.sp else 14.sp,
            fontWeight = if (isTotal) FontWeight.Bold else FontWeight.Normal
        )
        Text(
            text = value,
            color = Ink,
            fontSize = if (isTotal) 18.sp else 14.sp,
            fontWeight = if (isTotal) FontWeight.Bold else FontWeight.Medium
        )
    }
}

@Composable
private fun CustomerDataSection(
    order: RestaurantOrder
) {
    val context = LocalContext.current

    Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
        Text(
            text = stringResource(R.string.order_detail_customer_data),
            color = Ink,
            fontSize = 16.sp,
            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
        )

        Surface(
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
            shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp),
            color = Color.White,
            border = BorderStroke(1.dp, OutlineGray)
        ) {
            Column(modifier = Modifier.padding(16.dp)) {
                Row(
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                ) {
                    Box(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .size(44.dp)
                            .clip(CircleShape)
                            .background(Primary),
                        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                    ) {
                        Text(
                            text = order.customerInitial,
                            color = Color.White,
                            fontSize = 18.sp,
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
                        )
                    }

                    Spacer(Modifier.width(12.dp))

                    Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                        Text(
                            text = order.customerName.ifBlank { stringResource(R.string.order_detail_customer_fallback) },
                            color = Ink,
                            fontSize = 15.sp,
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
                        )
                        if (order.customerPhone.isNotBlank()) {
                            Spacer(Modifier.height(2.dp))
                            Text(
                                text = order.customerPhone,
                                color = Muted,
                                fontSize = 13.sp
                            )
                        }
                    }

                    if (order.customerPhone.isNotBlank()) {
                        IconButton(
                            onClick = {
                                val phone = order.customerPhone.trim()
                                if (phone.isBlank()) {
                                    Toast.makeText(context, R.string.error_phone_empty, Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                                    return@IconButton
                                }
                                val intent = Intent(Intent.ACTION_DIAL, Uri.parse("tel:$phone"))
                                runCatching {
                                    context.startActivity(intent)
                                }.onFailure {
                                    Toast.makeText(context, R.string.error_call_unavailable, Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                                }
                            },
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .size(40.dp)
                                .clip(CircleShape)
                                .background(Color(0xFFDCFCE7))
                        ) {
                            Icon(
                                painter = painterResource(R.drawable.ic_phone),
                                contentDescription = stringResource(R.string.label_phone),
                                tint = Color(0xFF15803D),
                                modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp)
                            )
                        }
                    }
                }

                if (order.customerAddress.isNotBlank()) {
                    Spacer(Modifier.height(14.dp))
                    Row(
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                    ) {
                        Icon(
                            painter = painterResource(R.drawable.ic_location),
                            contentDescription = null,
                            tint = Primary,
                            modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp)
                        )
                        Spacer(Modifier.width(8.dp))
                        Text(
                            text = order.customerAddress,
                            color = InkSoft,
                            fontSize = 13.sp
                        )
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun CourierSection(
    order: RestaurantOrder
) {
    val context = LocalContext.current

    Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
        Text(
            text = stringResource(R.string.order_detail_courier),
            color = Ink,
            fontSize = 16.sp,
            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
        )

        if (order.courierName.isBlank()) {
            val textRes = when (order.status) {
                OrderStatus.SOLICITADO, OrderStatus.TOMADO -> R.string.order_detail_courier_info_solicitado
                OrderStatus.PREPARADO -> R.string.order_detail_courier_info_preparado
                else -> R.string.order_detail_courier_unassigned
            }
            Surface(
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                shape = RoundedCornerShape(14.dp),
                color = ScreenBg
            ) {
                Text(
                    text = stringResource(textRes),
                    color = Muted,
                    fontSize = 13.sp,
                    lineHeight = 18.sp,
                    modifier = Modifier.padding(16.dp)
                )
            }
        } else {
            Surface(
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp),
                color = Color.White,
                border = BorderStroke(1.dp, OutlineGray)
            ) {
                Row(
                    modifier = Modifier.padding(16.dp),
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                ) {
                    Box(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .size(44.dp)
                            .clip(CircleShape)
                            .background(Color(0xFF3B82F6)),
                        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                    ) {
                        Icon(
                            painter = painterResource(R.drawable.ic_person),
                            contentDescription = null,
                            tint = Color.White,
                            modifier = Modifier.size(24.dp)
                        )
                    }

                    Spacer(Modifier.width(12.dp))

                    Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                        Text(
                            text = order.courierName,
                            color = Ink,
                            fontSize = 15.sp,
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
                        )
                        if (order.courierPhone.isNotBlank()) {
                            Spacer(Modifier.height(2.dp))
                            Text(
                                text = order.courierPhone,
                                color = Muted,
                                fontSize = 13.sp
                            )
                        }
                    }

                    if (order.courierPhone.isNotBlank()) {
                        IconButton(
                            onClick = {
                                val phone = order.courierPhone.trim()
                                if (phone.isBlank()) {
                                    Toast.makeText(context, R.string.error_phone_empty, Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                                    return@IconButton
                                }
                                val intent = Intent(Intent.ACTION_DIAL, Uri.parse("tel:$phone"))
                                runCatching {
                                    context.startActivity(intent)
                                }.onFailure {
                                    Toast.makeText(context, R.string.error_call_unavailable, Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                                }
                            },
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .size(40.dp)
                                .clip(CircleShape)
                                .background(Color(0xFFDCFCE7))
                        ) {
                            Icon(
                                painter = painterResource(R.drawable.ic_phone),
                                contentDescription = stringResource(R.string.label_phone),
                                tint = Color(0xFF15803D),
                                modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp)
                            )
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun OrderDetailBottomBar(
    order: RestaurantOrder,
    onAcceptOrder: () -> Unit,
    onAdvanceToPrepared: () -> Unit,
    onAdvanceToEnCamino: () -> Unit
) {
    val (buttonTextRes, action) = when (order.status) {
        OrderStatus.SOLICITADO -> R.string.orders_action_accept to onAcceptOrder
        OrderStatus.TOMADO -> R.string.orders_action_mark_prepared to onAdvanceToPrepared
        OrderStatus.PREPARADO -> R.string.orders_action_mark_en_camino to onAdvanceToEnCamino
        OrderStatus.EN_CAMINO, OrderStatus.ENTREGADO -> null to null
    }

    if (buttonTextRes != null && action != null) {
        Surface(
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
            color = Color.White,
            shadowElevation = 8.dp
        ) {
            Box(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .navigationBarsPadding()
                    .padding(horizontal = 20.dp, vertical = 12.dp)
            ) {
                PrimaryButton(
                    text = stringResource(buttonTextRes),
                    onClick = action,
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()
                )
            }
        }
    }
}

package co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.ui.role

import androidx.activity.compose.BackHandler
import androidx.compose.foundation.BorderStroke
import androidx.compose.foundation.background
import androidx.compose.foundation.clickable
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.*
import androidx.compose.foundation.rememberScrollState
import androidx.compose.foundation.shape.CircleShape
import androidx.compose.foundation.shape.RoundedCornerShape
import androidx.compose.foundation.verticalScroll
import androidx.compose.material3.*
import androidx.compose.runtime.*
import androidx.compose.runtime.saveable.rememberSaveable
import androidx.compose.ui.Alignment
import androidx.compose.ui.Modifier
import androidx.compose.ui.draw.clip
import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Brush
import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Color
import androidx.compose.ui.res.painterResource
import androidx.compose.ui.res.stringResource
import androidx.compose.ui.text.font.FontWeight
import androidx.compose.ui.text.style.TextOverflow
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.dp
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.sp
import androidx.lifecycle.compose.collectAsStateWithLifecycle
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.R
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.model.MenuItem
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.model.UserProfile
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.ui.components.IconTile
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.ui.components.StatusBarAppearance
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.ui.main.SessionViewModel
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.ui.theme.*
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.util.toCop

private enum class RestaurantSection {
    PANEL, ORDERS, HISTORY, MENU, PROFILE, REVIEWS
}

@Composable
fun RestaurantDashboardScreen(
    sessionViewModel: SessionViewModel,
    dashboardViewModel: RestaurantDashboardViewModel,
    onLogout: () -> Unit,
) {
    StatusBarAppearance(darkIcons = true)
    val profile by sessionViewModel.profile.collectAsStateWithLifecycle()
    val dashboard by dashboardViewModel.state.collectAsStateWithLifecycle()
    var section by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(RestaurantSection.PANEL) }
    var isMenuEditorVisible by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var menuEditorInstance by rememberSaveable { mutableIntStateOf(0) }
    var menuItemInEditor by remember { mutableStateOf<MenuItem?>(null) }
    var selectedOrderId by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf<String?>(null) }

    val selectedOrder = dashboard.allOrders.firstOrNull { it.id == selectedOrderId }

    DisposableEffect(profile?.uid) {
        val currentProfile = profile
        if (currentProfile != null) {
            dashboardViewModel.observeMenu(currentProfile.uid, currentProfile.fullName)
            dashboardViewModel.observeOrders(currentProfile.uid)
            dashboardViewModel.observeReviews(currentProfile.uid)
            dashboardViewModel.observeProfile(currentProfile.uid, currentProfile)
        }
        onDispose {
            dashboardViewModel.stopObservingMenu()
            dashboardViewModel.stopObservingOrders()
            dashboardViewModel.stopObservingReviews()
            dashboardViewModel.stopObservingProfile()
        }
    }

    if (selectedOrder != null) {
        OrderDetailScreen(
            order = selectedOrder,
            onBack = { selectedOrderId = null },
            onAcceptOrder = { dashboardViewModel.acceptOrder(selectedOrder) },
            onAdvanceToPrepared = { dashboardViewModel.markOrderAsPrepared(selectedOrder) },
            onAdvanceToEnCamino = { dashboardViewModel.markOrderAsEnCamino(selectedOrder) }
        )
        return
    }

    BackHandler(enabled = (section == RestaurantSection.MENU && isMenuEditorVisible) || section != RestaurantSection.PANEL) {
        if (section == RestaurantSection.MENU && isMenuEditorVisible) {
            isMenuEditorVisible = false
            menuItemInEditor = null
            dashboardViewModel.clearMenuMessage()
        } else {
            section = RestaurantSection.PANEL
        }
    }

    Scaffold(
        containerColor = ScreenBg,
        contentWindowInsets = WindowInsets(0.dp),
        bottomBar = {
            if (!(section == RestaurantSection.MENU && isMenuEditorVisible) && section != RestaurantSection.HISTORY && section != RestaurantSection.REVIEWS) {
                NavigationBar(containerColor = Color.White) {
                    listOf(
                        RestaurantSection.PANEL to R.string.dashboard_tab_panel,
                        RestaurantSection.ORDERS to R.string.dashboard_tab_orders,
                        RestaurantSection.MENU to R.string.dashboard_tab_menu,
                        RestaurantSection.PROFILE to R.string.nav_profile,
                    ).forEach { (destination, label) ->
                        val showBadge = destination == RestaurantSection.ORDERS && dashboard.solicitadoCount > 0
                        NavigationBarItem(
                            selected = section == destination,
                            onClick = { section = destination },
                            icon = {
                                if (showBadge) {
                                    BadgedBox(
                                        badge = {
                                            Badge(
                                                containerColor = Primary,
                                                contentColor = Color.White
                                            ) {
                                                Text(dashboard.solicitadoCount.toString())
                                            }
                                        }
                                    ) {
                                        Icon(
                                            painter = painterResource(destination.iconResource()),
                                            contentDescription = null,
                                        )
                                    }
                                } else {
                                    Icon(
                                        painter = painterResource(destination.iconResource()),
                                        contentDescription = null,
                                    )
                                }
                            },
                            label = { Text(stringResource(label), fontSize = 11.sp) },
                            colors = NavigationBarItemDefaults.colors(
                                selectedIconColor = Primary,
                                selectedTextColor = Primary,
                                indicatorColor = TileOrange,
                                unselectedIconColor = Hint,
                                unselectedTextColor = Hint,
                            ),
                        )
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    ) { innerPadding ->
        if (section == RestaurantSection.REVIEWS) {
            RestaurantReviewsScreen(
                dashboard = dashboard,
                onSelectStarFilter = dashboardViewModel::selectStarFilter,
                onReplyToReview = { reviewId, replyText ->
                    dashboardViewModel.replyToReview(reviewId, replyText)
                },
                onBack = { section = RestaurantSection.PANEL },
                modifier = Modifier.padding(innerPadding)
            )
        } else if (section == RestaurantSection.HISTORY) {
            SalesHistoryScreen(
                allOrders = dashboard.allOrders,
                onBack = { section = RestaurantSection.PANEL },
                onOrderClick = { order -> selectedOrderId = order.id },
                modifier = Modifier.padding(innerPadding)
            )
        } else if (section == RestaurantSection.MENU) {
            if (isMenuEditorVisible) {
                MenuProductEditorScreen(
                    item = menuItemInEditor,
                    state = dashboard,
                    editorKey = menuEditorInstance,
                    modifier = Modifier.padding(innerPadding),
                    onBack = {
                        isMenuEditorVisible = false
                        menuItemInEditor = null
                        dashboardViewModel.clearMenuMessage()
                    },
                    onSave = { item ->
                        dashboardViewModel.saveMenuItem(item) { saved ->
                            if (saved) {
                                isMenuEditorVisible = false
                                menuItemInEditor = null
                            }
                        }
                    }
                )
            } else {
                RestaurantMenuScreen(
                    state = dashboard,
                    onCreateProduct = {
                        menuItemInEditor = null
                        menuEditorInstance++
                        dashboardViewModel.clearMenuMessage()
                        isMenuEditorVisible = true
                    },
                    onEditProduct = { item ->
                        menuItemInEditor = item
                        menuEditorInstance++
                        dashboardViewModel.clearMenuMessage()
                        isMenuEditorVisible = true
                    },
                    modifier = Modifier.padding(innerPadding),
                    onAvailabilityChange = dashboardViewModel::setAvailability,
                    onRetry = {
                        profile?.let { dashboardViewModel.retryMenu(it.uid, it.fullName) }
                    },
                    onClearError = dashboardViewModel::clearMenuMessage
                )
            }
        } else if (section == RestaurantSection.ORDERS) {
            RestaurantOrdersScreen(
                dashboard = dashboard,
                onSelectFilter = dashboardViewModel::selectOrderFilter,
                onAcceptOrder = dashboardViewModel::acceptOrder,
                onAdvanceToPrepared = dashboardViewModel::markOrderAsPrepared,
                onAdvanceToEnCamino = dashboardViewModel::markOrderAsEnCamino,
                onOrderClick = { order -> selectedOrderId = order.id },
                modifier = Modifier.padding(innerPadding)
            )
        } else if (section == RestaurantSection.PROFILE) {
            RestaurantProfileScreen(
                profile = profile,
                dashboard = dashboard,
                onNavigateToHistory = { section = RestaurantSection.HISTORY },
                onNavigateToReviews = { section = RestaurantSection.REVIEWS },
                onLogout = {
                    dashboardViewModel.stopAllObserving()
                    onLogout()
                },
                onSaveProfile = { updatedProfile ->
                    dashboardViewModel.saveRestaurantProfile(updatedProfile) { success ->
                        if (success) {
                            sessionViewModel.load()
                        }
                    }
                },
                onInviteCourier = { phone, onDone ->
                    val uid = profile?.uid ?: dashboard.restaurantProfile.uid
                    dashboardViewModel.inviteCourier(uid, phone, onDone)
                },
                modifier = Modifier.padding(innerPadding),
                onClearMessage = {
                    dashboardViewModel.clearProfileMessage()
                    dashboardViewModel.clearCourierMessage()
                }
            )
        } else {
            Column(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxSize()
                    .padding(innerPadding)
            ) {
                RestaurantHeader(profile = profile, section = section)
                Column(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .weight(1f)
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .verticalScroll(rememberScrollState())
                        .padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 16.dp),
                    verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(16.dp)
                ) {
                    when (section) {
                        RestaurantSection.PANEL -> DashboardContent(
                            dashboard = dashboard,
                            onOpenOrders = { section = RestaurantSection.ORDERS },
                            onOpenMenu = { section = RestaurantSection.MENU },
                            onOpenHistory = { section = RestaurantSection.HISTORY },
                            onOpenReviews = { section = RestaurantSection.REVIEWS },
                            onOrderClick = { orderId -> selectedOrderId = orderId }
                        )
                        RestaurantSection.ORDERS -> Unit
                        RestaurantSection.HISTORY -> HistoryContent(
                            orders = dashboard.orderHistory,
                            onOrderClick = { orderId -> selectedOrderId = orderId }
                        )
                        RestaurantSection.MENU -> Unit
                        RestaurantSection.PROFILE -> Unit
                        RestaurantSection.REVIEWS -> Unit
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun RestaurantHeader(profile: UserProfile?, section: RestaurantSection) {
    Row(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .background(Color.White)
            .statusBarsPadding()
            .padding(horizontal = 20.dp, vertical = 14.dp),
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
    ) {
        Box(
            modifier = Modifier
                .size(48.dp)
                .clip(CircleShape)
                .background(Primary),
            contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
        ) {
            if (profile?.initials.isNullOrBlank()) {
                Icon(
                    painter = painterResource(R.drawable.ic_restaurant),
                    contentDescription = null,
                    tint = Color.White,
                    modifier = Modifier.size(24.dp)
                )
            } else {
                Text(
                    text = profile.initials,
                    color = Color.White,
                    fontSize = 17.sp,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
                )
            }
        }
        Spacer(Modifier.width(12.dp))
        Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
            if (section == RestaurantSection.PANEL) {
                val firstName = profile?.firstName.orEmpty()
                Text(
                    text = if (firstName.isBlank()) stringResource(R.string.dashboard_greeting_default)
                    else stringResource(R.string.dashboard_greeting, firstName),
                    color = Muted,
                    fontSize = 12.sp
                )
                Text(
                    text = profile?.fullName?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() }
                        ?: stringResource(R.string.dashboard_restaurant_fallback),
                    color = Ink,
                    fontSize = 16.sp,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                    maxLines = 1,
                    overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis
                )
            } else {
                Text(
                    text = stringResource(section.titleResource()),
                    color = Ink,
                    fontSize = 18.sp,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
                )
                Text(
                    text = profile?.fullName.orEmpty(),
                    color = Muted,
                    fontSize = 12.sp,
                    maxLines = 1,
                    overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis
                )
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun DashboardContent(
    dashboard: RestaurantDashboardUiState,
    onOpenOrders: () -> Unit,
    onOpenMenu: () -> Unit,
    onOpenHistory: () -> Unit,
    onOpenReviews: () -> Unit,
    onOrderClick: (String) -> Unit = {}
) {
    NewOrdersCard(dashboard.newOrders.size, onOpenOrders)
    RevenueCard(dashboard.dailyRevenueCop)
    Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp)) {
        MetricCard(
            modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
            icon = R.drawable.ic_history,
            iconTint = IconGreen,
            iconBackground = TileGreen,
            value = dashboard.completedOrders.toString(),
            label = stringResource(R.string.dashboard_completed_orders),
            onClick = onOpenHistory
        )
        MetricCard(
            modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
            icon = R.drawable.ic_star,
            iconTint = Gold,
            iconBackground = TileAmber,
            value = stringResource(R.string.dashboard_rating_format, dashboard.formattedRating),
            label = stringResource(R.string.dashboard_review_count, dashboard.reviewCount),
            onClick = onOpenReviews
        )
    }
    Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
        Text(
            text = stringResource(R.string.dashboard_orders_in_progress),
            color = Ink,
            fontSize = 17.sp,
            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
            modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)
        )
        Text(
            text = stringResource(R.string.dashboard_view_board),
            color = Primary,
            fontSize = 13.sp,
            fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold,
            modifier = Modifier
                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(6.dp))
                .clickable(onClick = onOpenOrders)
                .padding(4.dp)
        )
    }
    if (dashboard.inProgressOrders.isEmpty()) {
        EmptyCard(
            icon = R.drawable.ic_shopping_bag,
            title = stringResource(R.string.dashboard_orders_empty_title),
            message = stringResource(R.string.dashboard_orders_empty_message)
        )
    } else {
        dashboard.inProgressOrders.forEach { order ->
            OrderCard(order = order, onClick = { onOrderClick(order.id) })
        }
    }
    Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)) {
        QuickAction(
            modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
            icon = R.drawable.ic_restaurant,
            label = stringResource(R.string.dashboard_action_menu),
            onClick = onOpenMenu
        )
        QuickAction(
            modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
            icon = R.drawable.ic_history,
            label = stringResource(R.string.dashboard_action_history),
            onClick = onOpenHistory
        )
        QuickAction(
            modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
            icon = R.drawable.ic_notifications,
            label = stringResource(R.string.dashboard_action_simulate),
            onClick = onOpenOrders
        )
    }
}

@Composable
private fun NewOrdersCard(count: Int, onClick: () -> Unit) {
    Surface(
        onClick = onClick,
        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
        shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp),
        color = Peach,
        border = BorderStroke(1.dp, PeachStroke)
    ) {
        Row(
            modifier = Modifier.padding(14.dp),
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
        ) {
            IconTile(
                icon = R.drawable.ic_notifications,
                iconTint = Color.White,
                background = Primary,
                size = 44.dp
            )
            Spacer(Modifier.width(12.dp))
            Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                Text(
                    text = if (count == 0) stringResource(R.string.dashboard_no_new_orders)

                    fontSize = 14.sp,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
                )
                Text(
                    text = stringResource(R.string.dashboard_waiting_orders),
                    color = Muted,
                    fontSize = 12.sp

            Icon(
                painter = painterResource(R.drawable.ic_chevron_right),
                contentDescription = null,
                tint = Primary,
                modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp)
            )
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun MetricCard(
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    icon: Int,
    iconTint: Color,
    iconBackground: Color,
    value: String,
    label: String,
    onClick: (() -> Unit)? = null
) {
    val cardModifier = if (onClick != null) modifier.clickable(onClick = onClick) else modifier
    Column(
        modifier = cardModifier
            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(18.dp))
            .background(Color.White)
            .padding(14.dp)
    ) {
        IconTile(icon = icon, iconTint = iconTint, background = iconBackground, size = 38.dp)
        Text(
            text = value,
            color = Ink,
            fontSize = 22.sp,
            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
            modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 6.dp)
        )
        Text(text = label, color = Muted, fontSize = 11.sp, lineHeight = 15.sp)
    }
}

@Composable
private fun QuickAction(
    modifier: Modifier,
    icon: Int,
    label: String,
    onClick: () -> Unit
) {
    Surface(
        onClick = onClick,
        modifier = modifier.heightIn(min = 70.dp),
        shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp),
        color = Peach
    ) {
        Column(
            modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = 6.dp, vertical = 12.dp),
            horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.Center
        ) {
            Icon(
                painter = painterResource(icon),
                contentDescription = null,
                tint = Primary,
                modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp)
            )
            Text(
                text = label,
                color = PrimaryDark,
                fontSize = 11.sp,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold,
                maxLines = 1,
                overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
                modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 4.dp)
            )
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun OrdersContent(
    orders: List<RestaurantDashboardOrder>,
    onOrderClick: (String) -> Unit = {}
) {
    if (orders.isEmpty()) {
        EmptyCard(
            icon = R.drawable.ic_shopping_bag,
            title = stringResource(R.string.dashboard_orders_empty_title),
            message = stringResource(R.string.dashboard_orders_empty_message)
        )
    } else {
        orders.forEach { order ->
            OrderCard(order = order, onClick = { onOrderClick(order.id) })
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun HistoryContent(
    orders: List<RestaurantDashboardOrder>,
    onOrderClick: (String) -> Unit = {}
) {
    if (orders.isEmpty()) {
        EmptyCard(
            icon = R.drawable.ic_history,
            title = stringResource(R.string.dashboard_history_empty_title),
            message = stringResource(R.string.dashboard_history_empty_message)
        )
    } else {
        orders.forEach { order ->
            OrderCard(order = order, onClick = { onOrderClick(order.id) })
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun EmptyCard(icon: Int, title: String, message: String) {
    Surface(
        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
        shape = RoundedCornerShape(18.dp),
        color = Color.White
    ) {
        Column(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .padding(horizontal = 20.dp, vertical = 28.dp),
            horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally
        ) {
            IconTile(icon = icon, iconTint = Primary, background = TileOrange, size = 52.dp)
            Text(
                text = title,
                color = Ink,
                fontSize = 16.sp,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 12.dp)
            )
            Text(
                text = message,
                color = Muted,
                fontSize = 13.sp,
                modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 4.dp)
            )
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun OrderCard(
    order: RestaurantDashboardOrder,
    onClick: () -> Unit = {}
) {
    Surface(
        onClick = onClick,
        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
        shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp),
        color = Color.White,
        border = BorderStroke(1.dp, FieldStroke)
    ) {
        Row(
            modifier = Modifier.padding(14.dp),
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
        ) {
            Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                Text(order.customerName, color = Ink, fontSize = 14.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                Text(
                    stringResource(R.string.dashboard_order_item_count, order.itemCount),
                    color = Muted,
                    fontSize = 12.sp
                )
            }
            Text(order.totalCop.toCop(), color = Ink, fontSize = 14.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
        }
    }
}

private fun RestaurantSection.iconResource(): Int = when (this) {
    RestaurantSection.PANEL -> R.drawable.ic_home
    RestaurantSection.ORDERS -> R.drawable.ic_shopping_bag
    RestaurantSection.HISTORY -> R.drawable.ic_history
    RestaurantSection.MENU -> R.drawable.ic_restaurant
    RestaurantSection.PROFILE -> R.drawable.ic_person
    RestaurantSection.REVIEWS -> R.drawable.ic_star
}

private fun RestaurantSection.titleResource(): Int = when (this) {
    RestaurantSection.PANEL -> R.string.dashboard_title
    RestaurantSection.ORDERS -> R.string.dashboard_tab_orders
    RestaurantSection.HISTORY -> R.string.dashboard_action_history
    RestaurantSection.MENU -> R.string.dashboard_tab_menu
    RestaurantSection.PROFILE -> R.string.nav_profile
    RestaurantSection.REVIEWS -> R.string.reviews_title
}

package co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.ui.role

import androidx.annotation.StringRes
import androidx.lifecycle.ViewModel
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.R
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.data.RestaurantRepository
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.model.MenuItem
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.model.OrderStatus
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.model.RestaurantOrder
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.model.RestaurantProfile
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.model.Review
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.model.UserProfile
import com.google.firebase.firestore.ListenerRegistration
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow
import java.util.Locale

data class RestaurantDashboardOrder(
    val id: String,
    val customerName: String,
    val itemCount: Int,
    val totalCop: Int,
)

data class RestaurantDashboardUiState(
    val dailyRevenueCop: Int = 0,
    val completedOrders: Int = 0,
    val rating: Double = 0.0,
    val reviewCount: Int = 0,
    val newOrders: List<RestaurantDashboardOrder> = emptyList(),
    val inProgressOrders: List<RestaurantDashboardOrder> = emptyList(),
    val orderHistory: List<RestaurantDashboardOrder> = emptyList(),
    val menuItems: List<MenuItem> = emptyList(),
    val restaurantDocumentId: String? = null,
    val isMenuLoading: Boolean = false,
    val isSavingMenuItem: Boolean = false,
    @param:StringRes val menuMessageRes: Int? = null,
    val allOrders: List<RestaurantOrder> = emptyList(),
    val selectedFilter: OrderStatus = OrderStatus.SOLICITADO,
    val isOrdersLoading: Boolean = false,
    val ordersError: String? = null,
    val reviews: List<Review> = emptyList(),
    val selectedStarFilter: Int = 0,
    val isReviewsLoading: Boolean = false,
    val reviewsError: String? = null,
    val restaurantProfile: RestaurantProfile = RestaurantProfile(),
    val isProfileLoading: Boolean = false,
    val isSavingProfile: Boolean = false,
    val profileSaveSuccess: Boolean = false,
    @param:StringRes val profileMessageRes: Int? = null,
    val courierInviteSuccess: Boolean = false,
    @param:StringRes val courierInviteMessageRes: Int? = null,
) {
    val solicitadoCount: Int get() = allOrders.count { it.status == OrderStatus.SOLICITADO }
    val tomadoCount: Int get() = allOrders.count { it.status == OrderStatus.TOMADO }
    val preparadoCount: Int get() = allOrders.count { it.status == OrderStatus.PREPARADO }
    val enCaminoCount: Int get() = allOrders.count { it.status == OrderStatus.EN_CAMINO }
    val entregadoCount: Int get() = allOrders.count { it.status == OrderStatus.ENTREGADO }

    /** Pedidos activos (no entregados): Solicitado + Tomado + Preparado + En camino */
    val activeOrdersCount: Int get() = allOrders.count { it.isActive }

    /** Lista de pedidos filtrados segÃºn la pestaÃ±a de estado seleccionada */
    val filteredOrders: List<RestaurantOrder>
        get() = allOrders.filter { it.status == selectedFilter }

    /** Promedio formateado a 1 decimal (ej. "4.1" o "0.0") */
    val formattedRating: String
        get() = if (reviewCount > 0) String.format(Locale.US, "%.1f", rating) else "0.0"

    val star5Count: Int get() = reviews.count { it.ratingInt == 5 }
    val star4Count: Int get() = reviews.count { it.ratingInt == 4 }
    val star3Count: Int get() = reviews.count { it.ratingInt == 3 }
    val star2Count: Int get() = reviews.count { it.ratingInt == 2 }
    val star1Count: Int get() = reviews.count { it.ratingInt == 1 }

    fun starProgress(starLevel: Int): Float {
        if (reviewCount == 0) return 0f
        val count = when (starLevel) {
            5 -> star5Count
            4 -> star4Count
            3 -> star3Count
            2 -> star2Count
            1 -> star1Count
            else -> 0
        }
        return (count.toFloat() / reviewCount).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
    }

    /** Lista de reseÃ±as filtradas por la cantidad de estrellas seleccionada */
    val filteredReviews: List<Review>
        get() = if (selectedStarFilter == 0) reviews else reviews.filter { it.ratingInt == selectedStarFilter }
}

class RestaurantDashboardViewModel : ViewModel() {

    private val _state = MutableStateFlow(RestaurantDashboardUiState())
    val state: StateFlow<RestaurantDashboardUiState> = _state.asStateFlow()

    private var menuOwnerUid: String? = null
    private var menuListener: ListenerRegistration? = null

    private var ordersOwnerUid: String? = null
    private var ordersListener: ListenerRegistration? = null

    fun observeMenu(ownerUid: String, restaurantName: String) {
        if (menuOwnerUid == ownerUid) return
        menuListener?.remove()
        menuListener = null
        menuOwnerUid = ownerUid
        _state.value = _state.value.copy(
            menuItems = emptyList(),
            restaurantDocumentId = null,
            isMenuLoading = true,
            menuMessageRes = null
        )
        RestaurantRepository.findOrCreateOwnedRestaurant(ownerUid, restaurantName) { result ->
            if (menuOwnerUid != ownerUid) return@findOrCreateOwnedRestaurant
            val documentId = result.getOrNull()
            if (documentId == null) {
                _state.value = _state.value.copy(
                    isMenuLoading = false,
                    menuMessageRes = R.string.restaurant_menu_load_error
                )
                return@findOrCreateOwnedRestaurant
            }
            _state.value = _state.value.copy(restaurantDocumentId = documentId)
            val registration = RestaurantRepository.listenMenu(documentId) { menuResult ->
                if (menuOwnerUid != ownerUid) return@listenMenu
                val items = menuResult.getOrNull()
                _state.value = _state.value.copy(
                    menuItems = items ?: _state.value.menuItems,
                    isMenuLoading = false,
                    menuMessageRes = if (items == null) R.string.restaurant_menu_load_error else null
                )
            }
            if (menuOwnerUid == ownerUid) {
                menuListener = registration
            } else {
                registration.remove()
            }
        }
    }

    fun retryMenu(ownerUid: String, restaurantName: String) {
        menuListener?.remove()
        menuListener = null
        menuOwnerUid = null
        observeMenu(ownerUid, restaurantName)
    }

    fun stopObservingMenu() {
        menuOwnerUid = null
        menuListener?.remove()
        menuListener = null
        _state.value = _state.value.copy(
            menuItems = emptyList(),
            restaurantDocumentId = null,
            isMenuLoading = false
        )
    }

    fun observeOrders(ownerUid: String) {
        if (ordersOwnerUid == ownerUid && ordersListener != null) return
        ordersListener?.remove()
        ordersListener = null
        ordersOwnerUid = ownerUid
        _state.value = _state.value.copy(isOrdersLoading = true)

        ordersListener = RestaurantRepository.listenRestaurantOrders(ownerUid) { result ->
            if (ordersOwnerUid != ownerUid) return@listenRestaurantOrders
            val orders = result.getOrNull()
            if (orders != null) {
                val newOrders = orders.filter { it.status == OrderStatus.SOLICITADO }.map { it.toDashboardOrder() }
                val inProgress = orders.filter { it.isActive }.map { it.toDashboardOrder() }
                val history = orders.filter { it.status == OrderStatus.ENTREGADO }.map { it.toDashboardOrder() }
                val completed = orders.count { it.status == OrderStatus.ENTREGADO }
                val dailyRevenue = orders.filter { it.status == OrderStatus.ENTREGADO }.sumOf { it.totalCop }

                _state.value = _state.value.copy(
                    allOrders = orders,
                    newOrders = newOrders,
                    inProgressOrders = inProgress,
                    orderHistory = history,
                    completedOrders = completed,
                    dailyRevenueCop = dailyRevenue,
                    isOrdersLoading = false,
                    ordersError = null
                )
            } else {
                _state.value = _state.value.copy(
                    isOrdersLoading = false,
                    ordersError = result.exceptionOrNull()?.message
                )
            }
        }
    }

    fun stopObservingOrders() {
        ordersOwnerUid = null
        ordersListener?.remove()
        ordersListener = null
        _state.value = _state.value.copy(
            allOrders = emptyList(),
            isOrdersLoading = false
        )
    }

    fun selectOrderFilter(filter: OrderStatus) {
        _state.value = _state.value.copy(selectedFilter = filter)
    }

    fun acceptOrder(order: RestaurantOrder, onComplete: (Boolean) -> Unit = {}) {
        advanceOrderStatus(order, OrderStatus.TOMADO, onComplete)
    }

    fun markOrderAsPrepared(order: RestaurantOrder, onComplete: (Boolean) -> Unit = {}) {
        advanceOrderStatus(order, OrderStatus.PREPARADO, onComplete)
    }

    fun markOrderAsEnCamino(order: RestaurantOrder, onComplete: (Boolean) -> Unit = {}) {
        advanceOrderStatus(order, OrderStatus.EN_CAMINO, onComplete)
    }

    fun advanceOrderStatus(order: RestaurantOrder, nextStatus: OrderStatus, onComplete: (Boolean) -> Unit = {}) {
        RestaurantRepository.updateOrderStatus(order.id, nextStatus) { result ->
            if (result.isSuccess) {
                _state.value = _state.value.copy(
                    allOrders = _state.value.allOrders.map {
                        if (it.id == order.id) it.copy(status = nextStatus) else it
                    }
                )
                onComplete(true)
            } else {
                onComplete(false)
            }
        }
    }

    fun setAvailability(item: MenuItem, isAvailable: Boolean) {
        val documentId = _state.value.restaurantDocumentId
        if (documentId == null) {
            _state.value = _state.value.copy(menuMessageRes = R.string.restaurant_menu_update_error)
            return
        }
        _state.value = _state.value.copy(
            menuItems = _state.value.menuItems.map {
                if (it.id == item.id) it.copy(isAvailable = isAvailable) else it
            },
            menuMessageRes = null
        )
        RestaurantRepository.setMenuItemAvailability(documentId, item.id, isAvailable) { result ->
            if (result.isFailure) {
                _state.value = _state.value.copy(
                    menuItems = _state.value.menuItems.map {
                        if (it.id == item.id) it.copy(isAvailable = item.isAvailable) else it
                    },
                    menuMessageRes = R.string.restaurant_menu_update_error
                )
            }
        }
    }

    fun saveMenuItem(item: MenuItem, onComplete: (Boolean) -> Unit) {
        val documentId = _state.value.restaurantDocumentId
        if (documentId == null) {
            _state.value = _state.value.copy(menuMessageRes = R.string.restaurant_menu_save_error)
            onComplete(false)
            return
        }
        _state.value = _state.value.copy(isSavingMenuItem = true, menuMessageRes = null)
        RestaurantRepository.saveMenuItem(documentId, item) { result ->
            _state.value = _state.value.copy(
                isSavingMenuItem = false,
                menuMessageRes = if (result.isFailure) R.string.restaurant_menu_save_error else null
            )
            onComplete(result.isSuccess)
        }
    }

    fun clearMenuMessage() {
        _state.value = _state.value.copy(menuMessageRes = null)
    }

    fun observeReviews(ownerUid: String) {
        if (reviewsOwnerUid == ownerUid && reviewsListener != null) return
        reviewsListener?.remove()
        reviewsListener = null
        reviewsOwnerUid = ownerUid
        _state.value = _state.value.copy(isReviewsLoading = true)

        reviewsListener = RestaurantRepository.listenRestaurantReviews(ownerUid) { result ->
            if (reviewsOwnerUid != ownerUid) return@listenRestaurantReviews
            val reviews = result.getOrNull()
            if (reviews != null) {
                val count = reviews.size
                val rawAvg = if (count > 0) reviews.sumOf { it.rating.toDouble() } / count else 0.0
                val roundedAvg = (Math.round(rawAvg * 10.0) / 10.0)

                _state.value = _state.value.copy(
                    reviews = reviews,
                    rating = roundedAvg,
                    reviewCount = count,
                    isReviewsLoading = false,
                    reviewsError = null
                )
            } else {
                _state.value = _state.value.copy(
                    isReviewsLoading = false,
                    reviewsError = result.exceptionOrNull()?.message
                )
            }
        }
    }

    fun stopObservingReviews() {
        reviewsOwnerUid = null
        reviewsListener?.remove()
        reviewsListener = null
        _state.value = _state.value.copy(
            reviews = emptyList(),
            isReviewsLoading = false
        )
    }

    fun selectStarFilter(star: Int) {
        _state.value = _state.value.copy(selectedStarFilter = star)
    }

    fun replyToReview(reviewId: String, replyText: String, onComplete: (Boolean) -> Unit = {}) {
        val ownerUid = reviewsOwnerUid ?: _state.value.restaurantDocumentId ?: return
        RestaurantRepository.replyToReview(ownerUid, reviewId, replyText) { result ->
            if (result.isSuccess) {
                _state.value = _state.value.copy(
                    reviews = _state.value.reviews.map {
                        if (it.id == reviewId) it.copy(reply = replyText.trim()) else it
                    }
                )
                onComplete(true)
            } else {
                onComplete(false)
            }
        }
    }

    private var reviewsOwnerUid: String? = null
    private var reviewsListener: ListenerRegistration? = null

    private var profileOwnerUid: String? = null
    private var profileListener: ListenerRegistration? = null

    fun observeProfile(ownerUid: String, fallbackProfile: UserProfile? = null) {
        if (profileOwnerUid == ownerUid && profileListener != null) return
        profileListener?.remove()
        profileListener = null
        profileOwnerUid = ownerUid
        _state.value = _state.value.copy(isProfileLoading = true)

        profileListener = RestaurantRepository.listenRestaurantProfile(ownerUid) { result ->
            if (profileOwnerUid != ownerUid) return@listenRestaurantProfile
            val loaded = result.getOrNull()
            if (loaded != null) {
                val merged = loaded.copy(
                    name = loaded.name.ifBlank { fallbackProfile?.fullName.orEmpty() },
                    email = loaded.email.ifBlank { fallbackProfile?.email.orEmpty() },
                    phone = loaded.phone.ifBlank { fallbackProfile?.phone.orEmpty() }
                )
                _state.value = _state.value.copy(
                    restaurantProfile = merged,
                    isProfileLoading = false
                )
            } else {
                _state.value = _state.value.copy(isProfileLoading = false)
            }
        }
    }

    fun stopObservingProfile() {
        profileOwnerUid = null
        profileListener?.remove()
        profileListener = null
    }

    fun saveRestaurantProfile(profile: RestaurantProfile, onComplete: (Boolean) -> Unit = {}) {
        _state.value = _state.value.copy(isSavingProfile = true, profileMessageRes = null)
        RestaurantRepository.saveRestaurantProfile(profile) { result ->
            val success = result.isSuccess
            _state.value = _state.value.copy(
                isSavingProfile = false,
                restaurantProfile = if (success) profile else _state.value.restaurantProfile,
                profileSaveSuccess = success,
                profileMessageRes = if (success) R.string.profile_save_success else R.string.profile_save_error
            )
            onComplete(success)
        }
    }

    fun inviteCourier(restaurantId: String, phone: String, onComplete: (Boolean) -> Unit = {}) {
        RestaurantRepository.inviteCourier(restaurantId, phone) { result ->
            val success = result.isSuccess
            _state.value = _state.value.copy(
                courierInviteSuccess = success,
                courierInviteMessageRes = if (success) R.string.profile_invite_success else R.string.profile_save_error
            )
            onComplete(success)
        }
    }

    fun clearProfileMessage() {
        _state.value = _state.value.copy(profileMessageRes = null)
    }

    fun clearCourierMessage() {
        _state.value = _state.value.copy(courierInviteMessageRes = null)
    }

    fun stopAllObserving() {
        stopObservingMenu()
        stopObservingOrders()
        stopObservingReviews()
        stopObservingProfile()
    }

    override fun onCleared() {
        stopAllObserving()
    }

    private fun RestaurantOrder.toDashboardOrder() = RestaurantDashboardOrder(
        id = id,
        customerName = customerName,
        itemCount = itemCount,
        totalCop = totalCop
    )
}

===== C:\Users\vgzar\Documents\UdeA\COMPUTACION MOVIL\PidePues\app\src\main\java\co\edu\udea\compumovil\labs20262_gr08\pidepues\ui\role\RestaurantMenuScreen.kt =====
package co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.ui.role

@Composable
fun RestaurantMenuScreen(
    state: RestaurantDashboardUiState,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    onCreateProduct: () -> Unit,
    onEditProduct: (MenuItem) -> Unit,
    onAvailabilityChange: (MenuItem, Boolean) -> Unit,
    onRetry: () -> Unit,
    onClearError: () -> Unit
) {
    var query by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf("") }
    var selectedCategoryKey by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf<String?>(null) }
    val availableCount = state.menuItems.count { it.isAvailable }

    Column(modifier.fillMaxSize().background(Color.White)) {
        Row(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .statusBarsPadding()
                .padding(start = 20.dp, end = 16.dp, top = 12.dp, bottom = 12.dp),
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
        ) {
            Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                Text(
                    text = stringResource(R.string.restaurant_menu_title),
                    color = Ink,
                    fontSize = 22.sp,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
                )
                Text(
                    text = stringResource(
                        R.string.restaurant_menu_available_count,
                        availableCount,
                        state.menuItems.size
                    ),
                    color = Muted,
                    fontSize = 13.sp,
                    modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 2.dp)
                )
            }
            Box(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .size(48.dp)
                    .clip(CircleShape)
                    .background(Primary)
                    .clickable(onClick = onCreateProduct),
                contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
            ) {
                Icon(
                    painter = painterResource(R.drawable.ic_add),
                    contentDescription = stringResource(R.string.restaurant_product_add),
                    tint = Color.White,
                    modifier = Modifier.size(24.dp)
                )
            }
        }

        Column(
            modifier = Modifier
                .weight(1f)
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .verticalScroll(rememberScrollState())
                .padding(horizontal = 16.dp),
            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(14.dp)
        ) {
            OutlinedTextField(
                value = query,
                onValueChange = { query = it },
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                singleLine = true,
                shape = RoundedCornerShape(14.dp),
                placeholder = { Text(stringResource(R.string.restaurant_product_search), color = Hint) },
                leadingIcon = {
                    Icon(
                        painter = painterResource(R.drawable.ic_search),
                        contentDescription = null,
                        tint = Muted,
                        modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp)
                    )
                },
                colors = OutlinedTextFieldDefaults.colors(
                    focusedBorderColor = FieldStroke,
                    unfocusedBorderColor = FieldStroke,
                    focusedContainerColor = FieldBg,
                    unfocusedContainerColor = FieldBg
                )
            )

            Row(
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().horizontalScroll(rememberScrollState()),
                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)
            ) {
                MenuCategoryChip(
                    label = stringResource(R.string.restaurant_menu_all),
                    selected = selectedCategoryKey == null,
                    onClick = { selectedCategoryKey = null }
                )
                MenuCategory.entries.forEach { category ->
                    MenuCategoryChip(
                        label = category.label,
                        selected = selectedCategoryKey == category.key,
                        onClick = { selectedCategoryKey = category.key }
                    )
                }
            }

            AvailabilityInfoBanner()

            val filteredItems = state.menuItems.filter { item ->
                val matchesCategory = selectedCategoryKey == null || item.category.key == selectedCategoryKey
                val matchesQuery = query.isBlank() || item.name.contains(query, ignoreCase = true) ||
                    item.description.contains(query, ignoreCase = true)
                matchesCategory && matchesQuery
            }

            when {
                state.menuMessageRes != null -> MenuErrorMessage(
                    messageRes = state.menuMessageRes,
                    onRetry = onRetry,
                    onClear = onClearError
                )
                state.isMenuLoading && state.menuItems.isEmpty() -> Box(
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(vertical = 28.dp),
                    contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                ) {
                    CircularProgressIndicator(color = Primary)
                }
                state.menuItems.isEmpty() -> MenuEmptyState()
                filteredItems.isEmpty() -> Text(
                    text = stringResource(R.string.restaurant_menu_no_search_results),
                    color = Muted,
                    fontSize = 14.sp,
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(vertical = 30.dp)
                )
                else -> {
                    val categories = selectedCategoryKey?.let { key ->
                        MenuCategory.entries.filter { it.key == key }
                    } ?: MenuCategory.entries
                    categories.forEach { category ->
                        val productsInCategory = filteredItems.filter { it.category == category }
                        if (productsInCategory.isNotEmpty()) {
                            val allInCategory = state.menuItems.filter { it.category == category }
                            MenuCategoryHeader(
                                category = category.label,
                                available = allInCategory.count { it.isAvailable },
                                total = allInCategory.size
                            )
                            productsInCategory.forEach { product ->
                                MenuProductCard(
                                    product = product,
                                    onClick = { onEditProduct(product) },
                                    onAvailabilityChange = { onAvailabilityChange(product, it) }
                                )
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            Spacer(Modifier.height(8.dp))
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun MenuCategoryChip(label: String, selected: Boolean, onClick: () -> Unit) {
    Surface(
        onClick = onClick,
        shape = RoundedCornerShape(22.dp),
        color = if (selected) Primary else CircleGray,
        contentColor = if (selected) Color.White else InkSoft
    ) {
        Text(
            text = label,
            fontSize = 12.sp,
            fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold,
            modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 9.dp)
        )
    }
}

@Composable
private fun AvailabilityInfoBanner() {
    Row(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(16.dp))
            .background(FieldBg)
            .padding(horizontal = 14.dp, vertical = 12.dp),
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
    ) {
        Box(Modifier.size(8.dp).clip(CircleShape).background(IconGreen))
        Text(
            text = stringResource(R.string.restaurant_menu_availability_info),
            color = Muted,
            fontSize = 12.sp,
            lineHeight = 17.sp,
            modifier = Modifier.padding(start = 10.dp)
        )
    }
}

@Composable
private fun MenuCategoryHeader(category: String, available: Int, total: Int) {
    Row(
        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(top = 2.dp, bottom = 2.dp),
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
    ) {
        Text(category, color = Ink, fontSize = 16.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, modifier = Modifier.weight(1f))
        Text(
            text = stringResource(R.string.restaurant_menu_category_count, available, total),
            color = Hint,
            fontSize = 12.sp
        )
    }
}

@Composable
private fun MenuProductCard(
    product: MenuItem,
    onClick: () -> Unit,
    onAvailabilityChange: (Boolean) -> Unit
) {
    Surface(
        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
        onClick = onClick,
        shape = RoundedCornerShape(17.dp),
        color = Color.White,
        border = BorderStroke(1.dp, FieldStroke),
        shadowElevation = 1.dp
    ) {
        Row(
            modifier = Modifier.padding(10.dp),
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
        ) {
            ProductThumbnail(
                imageUri = product.imageUri,
                name = product.name,
                size = 62.dp,
                cornerRadius = 14.dp
            )
            Column(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .weight(1f)
                    .padding(horizontal = 10.dp)
            ) {
                Text(
                    text = product.name,
                    color = Ink,
                    fontSize = 14.sp,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                    maxLines = 1,
                    overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis
                )
                Text(
                    text = product.description,
                    color = Muted,
                    fontSize = 11.sp,
                    maxLines = 1,
                    overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
                    modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 2.dp)
                )
                Text(
                    text = product.price.toCop(),
                    color = Primary,
                    fontSize = 13.sp,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                    modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 4.dp)
                )
            }
            Column(horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally) {
                Switch(
                    checked = product.isAvailable,
                    onCheckedChange = onAvailabilityChange,
                    colors = SwitchDefaults.colors(
                        checkedThumbColor = Color.White,
                        checkedTrackColor = IconGreen,
                        uncheckedThumbColor = Color.White,
                        uncheckedTrackColor = OutlineGray
                    )
                )
                Text(
                    text = stringResource(
                        if (product.isAvailable) R.string.restaurant_product_available
                        else R.string.restaurant_product_sold_out
                    ),
                    color = if (product.isAvailable) IconGreen else Hint,
                    fontSize = 10.sp,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold
                )
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun ProductThumbnail(
    imageUri: String?,
    name: String,
    size: androidx.compose.ui.unit.Dp,
    cornerRadius: androidx.compose.ui.unit.Dp
) {
    if (imageUri.isNullOrBlank()) {
        val swatches = listOf(PrimaryDark, Primary, IconGreen, IconPink, IconBlue)
        val colorIndex = abs(name.hashCode() % swatches.size)
        Box(
            modifier = Modifier
                .size(size)
                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(cornerRadius))
                .background(swatches[colorIndex]),
            contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
        ) {
            Text(
                text = name.firstOrNull()?.uppercase() ?: "?",
                color = Color.White,
                fontSize = 23.sp,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
            )
        }
    } else {
        ProductPhotoPreview(
            imageUri = imageUri,
            modifier = Modifier.size(size).clip(RoundedCornerShape(cornerRadius))
        )
    }
}

@Composable

    Column(
        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(horizontal = 12.dp, vertical = 24.dp),
        horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally
    ) {
        Box(
            modifier = Modifier.size(68.dp).clip(CircleShape).background(TileOrange),
