/**
 * Pantalla "ReseÃ±as" para el rol Restaurante.
 * Conectada en tiempo real a Firestore. Muestra la tarjeta resumen con desglose de estrellas,
 * filtros por estrellas, listado de reseÃ±as de clientes y opciÃ³n para responder a cada una.
 */
@Composable
fun RestaurantReviewsScreen(
    dashboard: RestaurantDashboardUiState,
    onSelectStarFilter: (Int) -> Unit,
    onReplyToReview: (reviewId: String, replyText: String) -> Unit,
    onBack: () -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
) {
    StatusBarAppearance(darkIcons = true)
    BackHandler(onBack = onBack)

    val context = LocalContext.current
    val appContext = context.applicationContext
    val coroutineScope = rememberCoroutineScope()
    val snackbarHostState = remember { SnackbarHostState() }
    var replyingReview by remember { mutableStateOf<Review?>(null) }

    Scaffold(
        modifier = modifier.fillMaxSize(),
        containerColor = ScreenBg,
        contentWindowInsets = WindowInsets(0.dp),
        snackbarHost = { SnackbarHost(snackbarHostState) },
        topBar = {
            ReviewsHeader(onBack = onBack)
        }
    ) { innerPadding ->
        LazyColumn(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxSize()
                .padding(innerPadding),
            contentPadding = PaddingValues(start = 16.dp, end = 16.dp, top = 12.dp, bottom = 24.dp),
            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(16.dp)
        ) {
            // Tarjeta de resumen de calificaciÃ³n general y barras de progreso por estrella
            item {
                RatingSummaryCard(dashboard = dashboard)
            }

            // Barra horizontal de filtros por estrellas
            item {
                StarFilterRow(
                    selectedFilter = dashboard.selectedStarFilter,
                    onSelectFilter = onSelectStarFilter
                )
            }

            // Lista de reseÃ±as filtradas o estado vacÃ­o
            if (dashboard.filteredReviews.isEmpty()) {
                item {
                    EmptyReviewsState()
                }
            } else {
                items(dashboard.filteredReviews, key = { it.id }) { review ->
                    ReviewCard(
                        review = review,
                        onOpenReplyDialog = { replyingReview = review }
                    )
                }
            }
        }
    }

    // DiÃ¡logo modal para escribir y enviar la respuesta a la reseÃ±a
    if (replyingReview != null) {
        ReplyDialog(
            review = replyingReview!!,
            onDismiss = { replyingReview = null },
            onSendReply = { replyText ->
                onReplyToReview(replyingReview!!.id, replyText)
                replyingReview = null
                coroutineScope.launch {
                    snackbarHostState.showSnackbar(appContext.getString(R.string.reviews_reply_success))
                }
            }
        )
    }
}

@Composable
private fun ReviewsHeader(onBack: () -> Unit) {
    Row(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .background(Color.White)
            .statusBarsPadding()
            .padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 14.dp),
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
    ) {
        IconButton(
            onClick = onBack,
            modifier = Modifier
                .size(40.dp)
                .clip(CircleShape)
                .background(CircleGray)
        ) {
            Icon(
                painter = painterResource(R.drawable.ic_arrow_back),
                contentDescription = stringResource(R.string.cd_back),
                tint = Ink,
                modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp)
            )
        }

        Spacer(Modifier.width(12.dp))

        Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
            Text(
                text = stringResource(R.string.reviews_title),
                color = Ink,
                fontSize = 20.sp,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
            )
            Spacer(Modifier.height(2.dp))
            Text(
                text = stringResource(R.string.reviews_subtitle),
                color = Muted,
                fontSize = 13.sp
            )
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun RatingSummaryCard(dashboard: RestaurantDashboardUiState) {
    Surface(
        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
        shape = RoundedCornerShape(20.dp),
        color = Color(0xFFF7F7F7)
    ) {
        Row(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .padding(18.dp),
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
        ) {
            // Columna izquierda: Promedio general, 5 estrellas e indicaciones de conteo
            Column(
                modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
                horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally
            ) {
                Text(
                    text = dashboard.formattedRating,
                    color = Ink,
                    fontSize = 36.sp,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
                )
                Spacer(Modifier.height(4.dp))
                RatingStarsRow(
                    rating = dashboard.rating.toFloat(),
                    starSize = 18.dp
                )
                Spacer(Modifier.height(6.dp))
                Text(
                    text = stringResource(R.string.dashboard_review_count, dashboard.reviewCount),
                    color = Muted,
                    fontSize = 13.sp
                )
            }

            Spacer(Modifier.width(16.dp))

            // Columna derecha: 5 barras de progreso apiladas de la 5 a la 1
            Column(
                modifier = Modifier.weight(1.4f),
                verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(5.dp)
            ) {
                (5 downTo 1).forEach { starLevel ->
                    val count = when (starLevel) {
                        5 -> dashboard.star5Count
                        4 -> dashboard.star4Count
                        3 -> dashboard.star3Count
                        2 -> dashboard.star2Count
                        1 -> dashboard.star1Count
                        else -> 0
                    }
                    val progress = dashboard.starProgress(starLevel)

                    StarLevelProgressBarRow(
                        starLevel = starLevel,
                        progress = progress,
                        count = count
                    )
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun StarLevelProgressBarRow(
    starLevel: Int,
    progress: Float,
    count: Int
) {
    Row(
        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
    ) {
        Text(
            text = starLevel.toString(),
            color = Muted,
            fontSize = 12.sp,
            fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium,
            modifier = Modifier.width(14.dp)
        )
        Spacer(Modifier.width(6.dp))
        Box(
            modifier = Modifier
                .weight(1f)
                .height(7.dp)
                .clip(CircleShape)
                .background(OutlineGray)
        ) {
            Box(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxHeight()
                    .fillMaxWidth(fraction = progress)
                    .clip(CircleShape)
                    .background(Gold)
            )
        }
        Spacer(Modifier.width(8.dp))
        Text(
            text = count.toString(),
            color = Muted,
            fontSize = 12.sp,
            fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium,
            textAlign = TextAlign.End,
            modifier = Modifier.width(16.dp)
        )
    }
}

@Composable
private fun StarFilterRow(
    selectedFilter: Int,
    onSelectFilter: (Int) -> Unit
) {
    Row(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .horizontalScroll(rememberScrollState()),
        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)
    ) {
        listOf(
            0 to stringResource(R.string.reviews_filter_all),
            5 to stringResource(R.string.reviews_filter_stars_format, 5),
            4 to stringResource(R.string.reviews_filter_stars_format, 4),
            3 to stringResource(R.string.reviews_filter_stars_format, 3),
            2 to stringResource(R.string.reviews_filter_stars_format, 2),
            1 to stringResource(R.string.reviews_filter_stars_format, 1)
        ).forEach { (starValue, label) ->
            val isSelected = starValue == selectedFilter
            Surface(
                onClick = { onSelectFilter(starValue) },
                shape = RoundedCornerShape(50),
                color = if (isSelected) Primary else Color.White,
                border = if (isSelected) null else BorderStroke(1.dp, OutlineGray),
                modifier = Modifier.height(38.dp)
            ) {
                Box(
                    contentAlignment = Alignment.Center,
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .padding(horizontal = 16.dp)
                        .fillMaxHeight()
                ) {
                    Text(
                        text = label,
                        color = if (isSelected) Color.White else Ink,
                        fontSize = 13.sp,
                        fontWeight = if (isSelected) FontWeight.Bold else FontWeight.Medium
                    )
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun ReviewCard(
    review: Review,
    onOpenReplyDialog: () -> Unit
) {
    Surface(
        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
        shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp),
        color = Color.White,
        border = BorderStroke(1.dp, OutlineGray)
    ) {
        Column(modifier = Modifier.padding(16.dp)) {
            // Cabecera de la reseÃ±a: Avatar, cliente, fecha relativa y estrellas
            Row(
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
            ) {
                Box(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .size(40.dp)
                        .clip(CircleShape)
                        .background(TileOrange),
                    contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                ) {
                    Text(
                        text = review.customerInitial,
                        color = Primary,
                        fontSize = 16.sp,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
                    )
                }

                Spacer(Modifier.width(12.dp))

                Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                    Text(
                        text = review.customerName,
                        color = Ink,
                        fontSize = 15.sp,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
                    )
                    Spacer(Modifier.height(2.dp))
                    Text(
                        text = review.formattedRelativeDate,
                        color = Muted,
                        fontSize = 12.sp
                    )
                }

                RatingStarsRow(
                    rating = review.rating,
                    starSize = 14.dp
                )
            }

            // Comentario de la reseÃ±a si no estÃ¡ vacÃ­o
            if (review.comment.isNotBlank()) {
                Spacer(Modifier.height(12.dp))
                Text(
                    text = review.comment,
                    color = InkSoft,
                    fontSize = 14.sp,
                    lineHeight = 20.sp
                )
            }

            Spacer(Modifier.height(14.dp))

            // SecciÃ³n de respuesta: BotÃ³n "Responder" o recuadro "Tu respuesta"
            if (review.hasReply) {
                Box(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(12.dp))
                        .background(ScreenBg)
                        .padding(12.dp)
                ) {
                    Column {
                        Text(
                            text = stringResource(R.string.reviews_reply_header),
                            color = Ink,
                            fontSize = 12.sp,
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
                        )
                        Spacer(Modifier.height(4.dp))
                        Text(
                            text = review.reply,
                            color = Muted,
                            fontSize = 13.sp,
                            lineHeight = 18.sp
                        )
                    }
                }
            } else {
                Text(
                    text = stringResource(R.string.reviews_reply_action),
                    color = Primary,
                    fontSize = 14.sp,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(6.dp))
                        .clickable(onClick = onOpenReplyDialog)
                        .padding(vertical = 4.dp, horizontal = 2.dp)
                )
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun RatingStarsRow(
    rating: Float,
    starSize: androidx.compose.ui.unit.Dp
) {
    val roundedRating = Math.round(rating).coerceIn(0, 5)
    Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(2.dp)) {
        (1..5).forEach { starIndex ->
            Icon(
                painter = painterResource(R.drawable.ic_star),
                contentDescription = null,
                tint = if (starIndex <= roundedRating) Gold else OutlineGray,
                modifier = Modifier.size(starSize)
            )
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun ReplyDialog(
    review: Review,
    onDismiss: () -> Unit,
    onSendReply: (String) -> Unit
) {
    var replyText by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf("") }

    AlertDialog(
        onDismissRequest = onDismiss,
        title = {
            Text(
                text = stringResource(R.string.reviews_reply_dialog_title, review.customerName),
                color = Ink,
                fontSize = 17.sp,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
            )
        },
        text = {
            Column {
                if (review.comment.isNotBlank()) {
                    Text(
                        text = "\"${review.comment}\"",
                        color = Muted,
                        fontSize = 13.sp,
                        fontStyle = androidx.compose.ui.text.font.FontStyle.Italic,
                        maxLines = 3,
                        overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis
                    )
                    Spacer(Modifier.height(12.dp))
                }
                OutlinedTextField(
                    value = replyText,
                    onValueChange = { replyText = it },
                    placeholder = { Text(stringResource(R.string.reviews_reply_dialog_hint), fontSize = 13.sp) },
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                    shape = RoundedCornerShape(12.dp),
                    maxLines = 4
                )
            }
        },
        confirmButton = {
            PrimaryButton(
                text = stringResource(R.string.reviews_reply_send),
                onClick = {
                    val trimmed = replyText.trim()
                    if (trimmed.isNotBlank()) {
                        onSendReply(trimmed)
                    }
                },
                enabled = replyText.trim().isNotBlank()
            )
        },
        dismissButton = {
            TextButton(onClick = onDismiss) {
                Text(
                    text = stringResource(R.string.cancel),
                    color = Muted,
                    fontSize = 14.sp
                )
            }
        },
        containerColor = Color.White,
        shape = RoundedCornerShape(20.dp)
    )
}

@Composable

    ) {

                size = 52.dp
            )
            Spacer(Modifier.height(14.dp))

                color = Ink,
                fontSize = 16.sp,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                textAlign = TextAlign.Center
            )
            Spacer(Modifier.height(6.dp))
            Text(
                text = stringResource(R.string.reviews_empty_message),
                color = Muted,
                fontSize = 13.sp,
                textAlign = TextAlign.Center
            )
        }
    }
}

/** Pantalla de espera para cuentas de domiciliario. */
@Composable
fun RoleHomeScreen(
    role: UserRole,
    sessionViewModel: SessionViewModel,
    onLogout: () -> Unit,
) {
    if (role == UserRole.RESTAURANT) {
        val dashboardViewModel: RestaurantDashboardViewModel = viewModel()
        RestaurantDashboardScreen(
            sessionViewModel = sessionViewModel,
            dashboardViewModel = dashboardViewModel,
            onLogout = onLogout,
        )
        return
    }

    StatusBarAppearance(darkIcons = true)
    val profile by sessionViewModel.profile.collectAsStateWithLifecycle()

    Column(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxSize()
            .background(Color.White)
            .statusBarsPadding()
            .navigationBarsPadding()
            .padding(32.dp),
        horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.Center,
    ) {
        Text(text = "ðŸ›µ", fontSize = 72.sp)
        Text(
            text = stringResource(R.string.role_home_delivery_title),
            color = Ink,
            fontSize = 30.sp,
            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
            textAlign = TextAlign.Center,
            modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 16.dp),
        )
        Text(
            text = profile?.fullName.orEmpty(),
            color = Primary,
            fontSize = 16.sp,
            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
            modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 8.dp),
        )
        Text(
            text = stringResource(R.string.role_home_delivery_subtitle),
            color = Muted,
            fontSize = 14.sp,
            textAlign = TextAlign.Center,
            modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 12.dp),
        )
        OutlinedActionButton(
            text = stringResource(R.string.profile_logout),
            onClick = onLogout,
            modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 32.dp),
        )
    }
}

package co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.ui.role

import androidx.activity.compose.BackHandler
import androidx.compose.foundation.BorderStroke
import androidx.compose.foundation.background
import androidx.compose.foundation.clickable

enum class SalesPeriodFilter(val labelRes: Int) {
    HOY(R.string.sales_filter_today),
    AYER(R.string.sales_filter_yesterday),
    SIETE_DIAS(R.string.sales_filter_7_days),
    TREINTA_DIAS(R.string.sales_filter_30_days)
}

data class DayGroupedOrders(
    val dayLabel: String,
    val dayTotalCop: Int,
    val orders: List<RestaurantOrder>
)

/**
 * Pantalla "Historial de ventas" para el rol Restaurante.
 * Muestra el resumen dinÃ¡mico de ventas (Ventas, Pedidos, Ticket prom.) y la lista de pedidos
 * entregados agrupados por dÃ­a con filtros por perÃ­odo (Hoy, Ayer, 7 dÃ­as, 30 dÃ­as).
 */
@Composable
fun SalesHistoryScreen(
    allOrders: List<RestaurantOrder>,
    onBack: () -> Unit,
    onOrderClick: (RestaurantOrder) -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
) {
    StatusBarAppearance(darkIcons = true)
    BackHandler(onBack = onBack)

    var selectedFilter by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(SalesPeriodFilter.SIETE_DIAS) }

    val filteredOrders = remember(allOrders, selectedFilter) {
        filterDeliveredOrders(allOrders, selectedFilter)
    }

    val totalSalesCop = remember(filteredOrders) {
        filteredOrders.sumOf { it.totalCop }
    }

    val ordersCount = filteredOrders.size

    val averageTicketCop = remember(totalSalesCop, ordersCount) {
        if (ordersCount > 0) totalSalesCop / ordersCount else 0
    }

    val todayLabel = stringResource(R.string.sales_filter_today)
    val yesterdayLabel = stringResource(R.string.sales_filter_yesterday)
    val daysAgoFormat = stringResource(R.string.sales_days_ago_format)

    val groupedOrders = remember(filteredOrders, todayLabel, yesterdayLabel, daysAgoFormat) {
        val todayStart = Calendar.getInstance().apply {
            set(Calendar.HOUR_OF_DAY, 0)
            set(Calendar.MINUTE, 0)
            set(Calendar.SECOND, 0)
            set(Calendar.MILLISECOND, 0)
        }.timeInMillis

        val yesterdayStart = Calendar.getInstance().apply {
            timeInMillis = todayStart
            add(Calendar.DAY_OF_MONTH, -1)
        }.timeInMillis

        groupOrdersByDay(
            orders = filteredOrders,
            todayStart = todayStart,
            yesterdayStart = yesterdayStart,
            todayLabel = todayLabel,
            yesterdayLabel = yesterdayLabel,
            daysAgoFormat = daysAgoFormat
        )
    }

    Scaffold(
        modifier = modifier.fillMaxSize(),
        containerColor = ScreenBg,
        contentWindowInsets = WindowInsets(0.dp),
        topBar = {
            SalesHistoryHeader(onBack = onBack)
        }
    ) { innerPadding ->
        LazyColumn(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxSize()
                .padding(innerPadding),
            contentPadding = PaddingValues(start = 16.dp, end = 16.dp, top = 12.dp, bottom = 24.dp),
            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(16.dp)
        ) {
            // Barra de filtros por perÃ­odo
            item {
                PeriodFilterRow(
                    selectedFilter = selectedFilter,
                    onSelectFilter = { selectedFilter = it }
                )
            }

            // Tarjeta de resumen de ventas del perÃ­odo
            item {
                SalesSummaryCard(
                    totalSalesCop = totalSalesCop,
                    ordersCount = ordersCount,
                    averageTicketCop = averageTicketCop
                )
            }

            // Estado vacÃ­o o lista agrupada por dÃ­a
            if (groupedOrders.isEmpty()) {
                item {
                    EmptySalesHistoryState()
                }
            } else {
                items(groupedOrders.size, key = { groupedOrders[it].dayLabel }) { idx ->
                    DayGroupSection(
                        dayGroup = groupedOrders[idx],
                        onOrderClick = onOrderClick
                    )
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun SalesHistoryHeader(onBack: () -> Unit) {
    Row(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .background(Color.White)
            .statusBarsPadding()
            .padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 14.dp),
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
    ) {
        IconButton(
            onClick = onBack,
            modifier = Modifier
                .size(40.dp)
                .clip(CircleShape)
                .background(CircleGray)
        ) {
            Icon(
                painter = painterResource(R.drawable.ic_arrow_back),
                contentDescription = stringResource(R.string.cd_back),
                tint = Ink,
                modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp)
            )
        }

        Spacer(Modifier.width(12.dp))

        Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
            Text(
                text = stringResource(R.string.sales_history_title),
                color = Ink,
                fontSize = 20.sp,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
            )
            Spacer(Modifier.height(2.dp))
            Text(
                text = stringResource(R.string.sales_history_subtitle),
                color = Muted,
                fontSize = 13.sp
            )
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun PeriodFilterRow(
    selectedFilter: SalesPeriodFilter,
    onSelectFilter: (SalesPeriodFilter) -> Unit
) {
    Row(
        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)
    ) {
        SalesPeriodFilter.entries.forEach { filter ->
            val isSelected = filter == selectedFilter
            Surface(
                onClick = { onSelectFilter(filter) },
                shape = RoundedCornerShape(50),
                color = if (isSelected) Primary else Color.White,
                border = if (isSelected) null else BorderStroke(1.dp, OutlineGray),
                modifier = Modifier
                    .weight(1f)
                    .height(38.dp)
            ) {
                Box(
                    contentAlignment = Alignment.Center,
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
                ) {
                    Text(
                        text = stringResource(filter.labelRes),
                        color = if (isSelected) Color.White else Ink,
                        fontSize = 13.sp,
                        fontWeight = if (isSelected) FontWeight.Bold else FontWeight.Medium
                    )
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun SalesSummaryCard(
    totalSalesCop: Int,
    ordersCount: Int,
    averageTicketCop: Int
) {
    Surface(
        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
        shape = RoundedCornerShape(20.dp),
        color = Primary
    ) {
        Row(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .padding(horizontal = 18.dp, vertical = 18.dp),
            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
        ) {
            // Izquierda: Ventas del perÃ­odo
            Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1.3f)) {
                Text(
                    text = stringResource(R.string.sales_period_total),
                    color = White90,
                    fontSize = 12.sp,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium
                )
                Spacer(Modifier.height(4.dp))
                Text(
                    text = totalSalesCop.toCop(),
                    color = Color.White,
                    fontSize = 25.sp,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                    maxLines = 1,
                    overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis
                )
            }

            // Centro: Pedidos
            Column(
                modifier = Modifier.weight(0.8f),
                horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally
            ) {
                Text(
                    text = stringResource(R.string.sales_period_orders),
                    color = White90,
                    fontSize = 12.sp,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium
                )
                Spacer(Modifier.height(4.dp))
                Text(
                    text = ordersCount.toString(),
                    color = Color.White,
                    fontSize = 25.sp,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
                )
            }

            // Derecha: Ticket prom.
            Column(
                modifier = Modifier.weight(1.1f),
                horizontalAlignment = Alignment.End
            ) {
                Text(
                    text = stringResource(R.string.sales_period_average_ticket),
                    color = White90,
                    fontSize = 12.sp,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium
                )
                Spacer(Modifier.height(4.dp))
                Text(
                    text = averageTicketCop.toCop(),
                    color = Color.White,
                    fontSize = 18.sp,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                    maxLines = 1,
                    overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis
                )
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun DayGroupSection(
    dayGroup: DayGroupedOrders,
    onOrderClick: (RestaurantOrder) -> Unit
) {
    Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
        // Encabezado del grupo por dÃ­a
        Row(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .padding(horizontal = 4.dp),
            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
        ) {
            Text(
                text = dayGroup.dayLabel,
                color = Ink,
                fontSize = 15.sp,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
            )
            Text(
                text = dayGroup.dayTotalCop.toCop(),
                color = Muted,
                fontSize = 14.sp,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium
            )
        }

        // Tarjeta contenedora de las filas del dÃ­a
        Surface(
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
            shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp),
            color = Color.White,
            border = BorderStroke(1.dp, OutlineGray)
        ) {
            Column(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()) {
                dayGroup.orders.forEachIndexed { index, order ->
                    if (index > 0) {
                        HorizontalDivider(
                            color = DividerGray,
                            thickness = 1.dp,
                            modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = 14.dp)
                        )
                    }
                    SalesOrderRow(
                        order = order,
                        onClick = { onOrderClick(order) }
                    )
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun SalesOrderRow(
    order: RestaurantOrder,

) {
    Row(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .clickable(onClick = onClick)
            .padding(horizontal = 14.dp, vertical = 14.dp),
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
    ) {
        // Cuadrado redondeado verde con Ã­cono de check
        Box(
            modifier = Modifier
                .size(36.dp)
                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(10.dp))
                .background(Color(0xFFDCFCE7)),
            contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
        ) {
            Icon(
                painter = painterResource(R.drawable.ic_check),
                contentDescription = null,
                tint = Color(0xFF15803D),
                modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp)
            )
        }

        Spacer(Modifier.width(12.dp))

        // Datos del pedido (cÃ³digo, cliente, hora, cantidad de productos)
        Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
            Text(
                text = "#${order.orderNumber} Â· ${order.customerName}",
                color = Ink,
                fontSize = 14.sp,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                maxLines = 1,
                overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis
            )
            Spacer(Modifier.height(2.dp))
            Text(
                text = "${order.formattedTime} Â· ${stringResource(R.string.dashboard_order_item_count, order.itemCount)}",
                color = Muted,
                fontSize = 12.sp,
                maxLines = 1,
                overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis
            )
        }

        Spacer(Modifier.width(8.dp))

        // Total en COP
        Text(
            text = order.totalCop.toCop(),
            color = Ink,
            fontSize = 15.sp,
            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
        )
    }
}

@Composable

    Surface(

        shape = RoundedCornerShape(18.dp),
        color = Color.White
    ) {
        Column(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()

            IconTile(
                icon = R.drawable.ic_history,
                iconTint = Primary,
                background = TileOrange,
                size = 52.dp

            Text(
                text = stringResource(R.string.sales_history_empty_title),
                color = Ink,
                fontSize = 16.sp,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                textAlign = TextAlign.Center
            )
            Spacer(Modifier.height(6.dp))
            Text(

                color = Muted,
                fontSize = 13.sp,
                textAlign = TextAlign.Center
            )
        }
    }
}

private fun filterDeliveredOrders(
    allOrders: List<RestaurantOrder>,
    filter: SalesPeriodFilter
): List<RestaurantOrder> {
    val deliveredOrders = allOrders.filter { it.status == OrderStatus.ENTREGADO }

    val todayStart = Calendar.getInstance().apply {
        set(Calendar.HOUR_OF_DAY, 0)
        set(Calendar.MINUTE, 0)
        set(Calendar.SECOND, 0)
        set(Calendar.MILLISECOND, 0)
    }.timeInMillis

    val yesterdayStart = Calendar.getInstance().apply {
        timeInMillis = todayStart
        add(Calendar.DAY_OF_MONTH, -1)
    }.timeInMillis

    val sevenDaysStart = Calendar.getInstance().apply {
        timeInMillis = todayStart
        add(Calendar.DAY_OF_MONTH, -6)
    }.timeInMillis

    val thirtyDaysStart = Calendar.getInstance().apply {
        timeInMillis = todayStart
        add(Calendar.DAY_OF_MONTH, -29)
    }.timeInMillis

    return when (filter) {
        SalesPeriodFilter.HOY -> deliveredOrders.filter { it.createdAtMillis >= todayStart }
        SalesPeriodFilter.AYER -> deliveredOrders.filter {
            it.createdAtMillis >= yesterdayStart && it.createdAtMillis < todayStart
        }
        SalesPeriodFilter.SIETE_DIAS -> deliveredOrders.filter { it.createdAtMillis >= sevenDaysStart }
        SalesPeriodFilter.TREINTA_DIAS -> deliveredOrders.filter { it.createdAtMillis >= thirtyDaysStart }
    }
}

private fun groupOrdersByDay(
    orders: List<RestaurantOrder>,
    todayStart: Long,
    yesterdayStart: Long,
    todayLabel: String,
    yesterdayLabel: String,
    daysAgoFormat: String
): List<DayGroupedOrders> {
    if (orders.isEmpty()) return emptyList()

    val map = orders.groupBy { order ->
        Calendar.getInstance().apply {
            timeInMillis = order.createdAtMillis
            set(Calendar.HOUR_OF_DAY, 0)
            set(Calendar.MINUTE, 0)
            set(Calendar.SECOND, 0)
            set(Calendar.MILLISECOND, 0)
        }.timeInMillis
    }

    val sortedDayStarts = map.keys.sortedDescending()

    return sortedDayStarts.map { dayStart ->
        val dayOrders = map.getValue(dayStart).sortedByDescending { it.createdAtMillis }
        val dayTotal = dayOrders.sumOf { it.totalCop }

        val label = when (dayStart) {
            todayStart -> todayLabel
            yesterdayStart -> yesterdayLabel
            else -> {
                val diffDays = ((todayStart - dayStart) / (24 * 60 * 60 * 1000)).toInt()
                if (diffDays in 2..7) {
                    String.format(daysAgoFormat, diffDays)
                } else {
                    SimpleDateFormat("dd MMM", Locale.getDefault()).format(Date(dayStart))
                }
            }
        }

        DayGroupedOrders(
            dayLabel = label,
            dayTotalCop = dayTotal,
            orders = dayOrders
        )
    }
}

package co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.ui.search

import androidx.compose.foundation.*
import androidx.compose.foundation.interaction.*
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.*
import androidx.compose.foundation.lazy.*
import androidx.compose.foundation.shape.*
import androidx.compose.foundation.text.*
import androidx.compose.material3.*
import androidx.compose.runtime.*
import androidx.compose.runtime.saveable.*
import androidx.compose.ui.*
import androidx.compose.ui.draw.*
import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.*
import androidx.compose.ui.res.*
import androidx.compose.ui.text.*
import androidx.compose.ui.text.font.*
import androidx.compose.ui.text.input.*
import androidx.compose.ui.text.style.*
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.*
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.R
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.ui.theme.*
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.ui.components.*
import androidx.compose.ui.platform.LocalContext
import androidx.compose.ui.platform.LocalFocusManager
import androidx.lifecycle.compose.collectAsStateWithLifecycle
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.model.Category
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.ui.main.RestaurantsViewModel

@Composable
fun SearchScreen(
    restaurantsViewModel: RestaurantsViewModel,
    onFiltersClick: () -> Unit,
    onRestaurantClick: (Int) -> Unit
) {
    StatusBarAppearance(darkIcons = true)
    val state by restaurantsViewModel.state.collectAsStateWithLifecycle()
    val focusManager = LocalFocusManager.current
    val resultsCount = state.searchResults.size
    val resultsLabel = LocalContext.current.resources.getQuantityString(R.plurals.results_count, resultsCount, resultsCount)

    // Texto local para que el cursor no salte; se sincroniza si cambia desde fuera (ej. una bÃºsqueda reciente)
    var text by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(state.query) }
    LaunchedEffect(state.query) {
        if (text != state.query) text = state.query
    }

    Column(Modifier.fillMaxSize().background(ScreenBg)) {
        // Barra superior: campo de bÃºsqueda + botÃ³n de filtros
        Row(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .shadow(2.dp)
                .background(Color.White)
                .statusBarsPadding()
                .padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 12.dp),
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
        ) {
            Row(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .weight(1f)
                    .height(48.dp)
                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(16.dp))
                    .background(FieldBg)
                    .border(1.dp, FieldStroke, RoundedCornerShape(16.dp))
                    .padding(horizontal = 14.dp),
                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
            ) {
                Icon(
                    painter = painterResource(R.drawable.ic_search),
                    contentDescription = null,
                    tint = Hint,
                    modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp)
                )
                BasicTextField(
                    value = text,
                    onValueChange = {
                        text = it
                        restaurantsViewModel.setQuery(it)
                    },
                    singleLine = true,
                    textStyle = TextStyle(fontSize = 14.sp, color = Ink),
                    cursorBrush = SolidColor(Primary),
                    keyboardOptions = KeyboardOptions(imeAction = ImeAction.Search),
                    keyboardActions = KeyboardActions(
                        onSearch = {
                            restaurantsViewModel.submitSearch(text)
                            focusManager.clearFocus()
                        }
                    ),
                    modifier = Modifier.weight(1f).padding(start = 8.dp),
                    decorationBox = { innerTextField ->
                        Box(contentAlignment = Alignment.CenterStart) {
                            if (text.isEmpty()) {
                                Text(
                                    text = stringResource(R.string.search_hint),
                                    color = Hint,
                                    fontSize = 14.sp
                                )
                            }
                            innerTextField()
                        }
                    }
                )
            }
            Box(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .padding(start = 12.dp)
                    .size(48.dp)
                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(14.dp))
                    .background(Primary)
                    .clickable(onClick = onFiltersClick),
                contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
            ) {
                Icon(
                    painter = painterResource(R.drawable.ic_tune),
                    contentDescription = stringResource(R.string.cd_filters),
                    tint = Color.White,
                    modifier = Modifier.size(24.dp)
                )
            }
        }

        LazyColumn(
            modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
            contentPadding = PaddingValues(16.dp)
        ) {
            if (!state.isSearching) {
                // ----- Sin texto ni filtros: recientes + categorÃ­as -----
                if (state.recentSearches.isNotEmpty()) {
                    item {
                        Row(
                            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(bottom = 12.dp),
                            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                        ) {
                            Text(
                                text = stringResource(R.string.recent_title),
                                color = Ink,
                                fontSize = 18.sp,
                                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                                modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)
                            )
                            LinkText(
                                text = stringResource(R.string.recent_clear),
                                onClick = { restaurantsViewModel.clearRecents() }
                            )
                        }
                    }
                    items(state.recentSearches, key = { it }) { recent ->
                        RecentSearchRow(
                            text = recent,
                            onClick = { restaurantsViewModel.selectRecent(recent) }
                        )
                    }
                }
                item {
                    Text(
                        text = stringResource(R.string.explore_title),
                        color = Ink,
                        fontSize = 18.sp,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                        modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 16.dp, bottom = 8.dp)
                    )
                }
                item {
                    Column {
                        Category.values().toList().chunked(4).forEach { rowItems ->
                            Row(
                                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(vertical = 4.dp),
                                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)
                            ) {
                                rowItems.forEach { category ->
                                    CategoryTile(
                                        category = category,
                                        onClick = { restaurantsViewModel.applyCategory(category) },
                                        modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)
                                    )
                                }
                                repeat(4 - rowItems.size) { Spacer(Modifier.weight(1f)) }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            } else {
                // ----- Con texto o filtros activos: resultados -----
                val count = resultsCount
                item {
                    Row(
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(bottom = 12.dp),
                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                    ) {
                        Text(
                            text = resultsLabel,
                            color = Ink,
                            fontSize = 16.sp,
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                            modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)
                        )
                        LinkText(
                            text = stringResource(R.string.search_clear),
                            onClick = { restaurantsViewModel.clearAll() }
                        )
                    }
                }
                if (count == 0 && !state.isLoading) {
                    item {
                        Column(
                            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(32.dp),
                            horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally
                        ) {
                            Text(
                                text = stringResource(R.string.empty_results),
                                color = Muted,
                                fontSize = 14.sp,
                                textAlign = TextAlign.Center
                            )
                            LinkText(
                                text = stringResource(R.string.empty_clear),
                                onClick = { restaurantsViewModel.clearAll() },
                                modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 8.dp)
                            )
                        }
                    }
                } else {
                    items(state.searchResults, key = { it.restaurant.id }) { item ->
                        RestaurantCard(
                            item = item,
                            onClick = { onRestaurantClick(item.restaurant.id) },
                            modifier = Modifier.padding(bottom = 16.dp)
                        )
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun RecentSearchRow(text: String, onClick: () -> Unit) {
    AppCard(
        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(bottom = 8.dp),
        onClick = onClick,
        cornerRadius = 14.dp,
        elevation = 1.dp
    ) {
        Row(
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(14.dp),
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
        ) {
            Icon(
                painter = painterResource(R.drawable.ic_schedule),
                contentDescription = null,
                tint = Muted,
                modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp)
            )
            Text(
                text = text,
                color = InkSoft,
                fontSize = 14.sp,
                modifier = Modifier.weight(1f).padding(start = 12.dp)
            )
            Icon(
                painter = painterResource(R.drawable.ic_chevron_right),
                contentDescription = null,
                tint = Hint,
                modifier = Modifier.size(16.dp)
            )
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun CategoryTile(category: Category, onClick: () -> Unit, modifier: Modifier = Modifier) {
    AppCard(modifier = modifier, onClick = onClick, cornerRadius = 16.dp) {
        Column(
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(horizontal = 4.dp, vertical = 12.dp),
            horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally
        ) {
            Text(text = category.emoji, fontSize = 28.sp)
            Text(
                text = category.label,
                color = InkSoft,
                fontSize = 10.sp,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                maxLines = 1,
                softWrap = false,
                textAlign = TextAlign.Center,
                modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 6.dp)
            )
        }
    }
}

package co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.ui.splash

import androidx.compose.foundation.*

import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.ui.theme.*
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.ui.components.*
import androidx.compose.ui.geometry.Offset
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.data.AuthRepository
import co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.model.UserProfile
import kotlinx.coroutines.delay

/**
 * Pantalla de arranque. Con sesiÃ³n iniciada devuelve el perfil (para ir segÃºn el rol);
 * sin sesiÃ³n devuelve null (ir al login).
 */
@Composable
fun SplashScreen(onFinished: (UserProfile?) -> Unit) {
    StatusBarAppearance(darkIcons = false)
    val currentOnFinished by rememberUpdatedState(onFinished)

    LaunchedEffect(Unit) {
        delay(1500)
        if (AuthRepository.isLoggedIn) {
            AuthRepository.loadProfile { result -> currentOnFinished(result.getOrNull()) }
        } else {
            currentOnFinished(null)
        }
    }

    Box(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxSize()
            .background(
                Brush.linearGradient(
                    colors = listOf(SplashStart, SplashEnd),
                    start = Offset(0f, 0f),
                    end = Offset.Infinite
                )
            )
    ) {
        // CÃ­rculos decorativos translÃºcidos, parcialmente fuera de pantalla
        Box(
            Modifier.size(260.dp).align(Alignment.TopEnd).offset(x = 90.dp, y = (-90).dp)
                .clip(CircleShape).background(White12)
        )
        Box(
            Modifier.size(130.dp).align(Alignment.CenterEnd).offset(x = 50.dp, y = (-140).dp)
                .clip(CircleShape).background(White12)
        )
        Box(
            Modifier.size(300.dp).align(Alignment.BottomStart).offset(x = (-70).dp, y = 130.dp)
                .clip(CircleShape).background(White12)
        )

        Column(
            modifier = Modifier.align(Alignment.Center),
            horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally
        ) {
            Box(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .size(100.dp)
                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(28.dp))
                    .background(White20)
                    .border(1.dp, White20, RoundedCornerShape(28.dp)),
                contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
            ) {
                Icon(
                    painter = painterResource(R.drawable.ic_restaurant),
                    contentDescription = stringResource(R.string.cd_logo),
                    tint = Color.White,
                    modifier = Modifier.size(48.dp)
                )
            }
            Text(
                text = stringResource(R.string.app_name),
                color = Color.White,
                fontSize = 44.sp,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 24.dp)
            )
            Text(
                text = stringResource(R.string.splash_tagline),
                color = White80,
                fontSize = 12.sp,
                letterSpacing = 2.sp,
                modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 8.dp)
            )
        }

        Row(
            modifier = Modifier
                .align(Alignment.BottomCenter)
                .navigationBarsPadding()
                .padding(bottom = 32.dp),
            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)
        ) {
            repeat(3) {
                Box(Modifier.size(8.dp).clip(CircleShape).background(White80))
            }
        }
    }
}

package co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.util

import java.text.NumberFormat
import java.util.Locale

private val COP: NumberFormat = NumberFormat.getInstance(Locale.forLanguageTag("es-CO"))

/** 28000 -> "$ 28.000" */

fun Int.toCopNumber(): String = COP.format(this)

/** 28000 -> "$ 28.000" */
fun Int.toCop(): String = "$ " + toCopNumber()

private val DATE_TIME = java.text.SimpleDateFormat("d MMM Â· h:mm a", Locale.forLanguageTag("es-CO"))
private val TIME_ONLY = java.text.SimpleDateFormat("h:mm a", Locale.forLanguageTag("es-CO"))

/** "15 nov Â· 2:45 p. m." */
fun formatOrderDate(millis: Long): String = DATE_TIME.format(java.util.Date(millis))

/** "2:45 p. m." */
fun formatOrderTime(millis: Long): String = TIME_ONLY.format(java.util.Date(millis))


===== C:\Users\vgzar\Documents\UdeA\COMPUTACION MOVIL\PidePues\app\src\main\java\co\edu\udea\compumovil\labs20262_gr08\pidepues\util\GeoUtils.kt =====
package co.edu.udea.compumovil.labs20262_gr08.pidepues.util

import kotlin.math.asin
import kotlin.math.cos
import kotlin.math.pow
import kotlin.math.sin
import kotlin.math.sqrt

private const val EARTH_RADIUS_KM = 6371.0088

/** Distancia en km entre dos coordenadas (fÃ³rmula de Haversine). */
fun haversineKm(lat1: Double, lon1: Double, lat2: Double, lon2: Double): Double {
    val dLat = Math.toRadians(lat2 - lat1)
    val dLon = Math.toRadians(lon2 - lon1)
    val a = sin(dLat / 2).pow(2) +
            cos(Math.toRadians(lat1)) * cos(Math.toRadians(lat2)) * sin(dLon / 2).pow(2)
    return 2 * EARTH_RADIUS_KM * asin(sqrt(a))
}

<?xml version="1.0" encoding="utf-8"?>
<manifest xmlns:android="http://schemas.android.com/apk/res/android"
    xmlns:tools="http://schemas.android.com/tools">

    <!-- UbicaciÃ³n: solo mientras se usa la app -->
    <uses-permission android:name="android.permission.ACCESS_COARSE_LOCATION" />
    <uses-permission android:name="android.permission.ACCESS_FINE_LOCATION" />

        android:name="android.hardware.location"
        android:required="false" />

    <application
        android:allowBackup="true"
        android:dataExtractionRules="@xml/data_extraction_rules"
        android:fullBackupContent="@xml/backup_rules"
        android:icon="@mipmap/ic_launcher"
        android:label="@string/app_name"
        android:roundIcon="@mipmap/ic_launcher_round"
        android:supportsRtl="true"
        android:theme="@style/Theme.PidePues"
        tools:targetApi="31">
